**Part 1**

**Various possibilities in the universe**

From possible universes to actualized structures

“Our universe is not the only correct answer,
It may be an observed phenotype among feasible realities.”

**Choi Won-sik**

# Opening words of Part 1

This book does not start with the right answer.It begins by temporarily letting go of the familiar premise that the universe must exist in only one way.There is certainly one universe as we observe it, but whether the general principles that describe it should allow for only one realization is another matter.

Part 1 does not declare the reality of other universes.By distinguishing between the plurality of physically possible models and the ontology of the actual multiverse, we open a logical space in which we can think of different combinations of laws, constants, dimensions, particles, and forces.Only by opening that space can we ask what is the common structure of our universe and what are the conditions of this instance.

At the end of these possibilities comes the reality renderer architecture.What lies between a possible state and the local reality that is actually observed.This question will guide all subsequent chapters.

## How to read math and physics terms

Readers who have not majored in mathematics or physics do not need to memorize all unfamiliar terms.For key terms encountered for the first time in the text, an ‘Appendix D Glossary of Terms’ guide has been added.Appendix D at the back of the book explains each word in the following order: its simple meaning, its role in the book, and its easily misunderstood boundaries.Already established concepts, such as the Bessel function and zeta function, and research concepts proposed in this book, such as WRRA, are also displayed separately.〔Refer to Appendix D ‘Bessel Function’〕 〔Refer to Appendix D ‘Riemann Zeta Function’〕

# Chapter 1 Is the universe we know the only correct answer?

When you look up at the night sky, the universe appears as one.The stars and galaxies we observe, the darkness between them, the space through which light moves, and the time in which events follow all form one gigantic reality.We were born within this reality, we think by the rules of this reality, and we have never directly looked outside of this reality.Therefore, the question ‘Why is the universe like this?’ often slips into the belief that ‘Does the universe have to be like this?’

However, observed facts and logical necessity are different.It is an observed fact that our universe has three spatial dimensions and one time dimension.It is also a confirmed fact in this universe that electrons have a constant mass and charge, that the speed of light is constant, and that gravity, electromagnetic force, and strong and weak forces operate.However, the conclusion that this value and structure must be unique in all possible realities does not follow from observations alone.

We often think of natural laws as the constitution of the universe.We imagine that the Constitution came first and that the universe was created according to its articles.But what physics actually offers is a little different.Physics finds repeating relationships between observed phenomena and expresses them in equations.The equation includes symmetry, conservation laws, fields, state space, coupling constants, and boundary conditions.Some terms are determined by structure, but some numbers are determined by experiment.

Therefore, a physical theory is usually not a sentence that describes only one world, but rather defines a space of solutions that satisfy certain conditions.The same equation creates a different history if the initial conditions and boundary conditions are different.Even the structure of the same field produces different particle phenomena if the mass and combination are different.A law is not a single result, but is closer to a grammar in which possible outcomes can exist.

The starting point of this book is simple.The idea is not to assume in advance that real-life grammar allows only one sentence.The universe we live in can be a single sentence that its grammar allows.It's a very long sentence, the only one we've been able to read so far, but it's not the same as the grammar itself.

Here, the term ‘possible universe’ does not immediately mean that another universe actually exists somewhere.Defining possible triangles mathematically does not mean that all triangles can be physically drawn.Likewise, the fact that we can conceive of different coherent models of the universe must be separated from the claim that all of those universes actually coexist.

What we want to ask first is not the number of existences, but the conditions of realization.What kind of structure must there be for it to become a reality?What allows a state physically, what reveals it in observable form, and what creates the joint order of particles, space, and time.This question precedes ‘Are there other universes?’

Then, understanding our universe may not mean proving that the universe is unique.Rather, it can be about establishing a space of possible structures and revealing the place our universe occupies within it.There is no need to prove that there is only one city on Earth in order to place it on a map.What is important is the coordinate system, location, and the structure observed at that location.

From this perspective, our universe does not become less important.On the contrary, it becomes a more accurate research subject.If we place the universe as a unique mystery, all its properties seem inevitable.But by placing it among the possible models, we can isolate which properties come from common laws and which properties come from selected conditions of this universe.

This book pushes that separation to its end.We distinguish between the source of a possible state, the structure that translates it into visible reality, and the result that finally appears as an object with mass, charge, spin, and position.Later on, these three layers will be called SOURCE, RENDERER, and PHENOTYPE.But before defining that name, we must first look at how different the universe could be.

# Chapter 2 The idea that laws and constants can be different

What changes when we say that the universe is changing?The easiest idea is to change the value of a constant.The mass of electrons, the strength of electric charge, the magnitude of gravity, the energy of vacuum, and the bonds between particles have measured values ​​in our universe.However, equations in physical theories often receive these values ​​as input from outside.The form of the expression only allows values, but does not necessarily fix the value to one.

Even a slight change in the constant can change the lifespan of a star, the stability of its atoms, and the path of nuclear reactions.However, there is no need to explain all physics focusing on the ‘values ​​at which life is possible.’What WRRA is interested in is not the selection of a universe that is friendly to humans, but how given values ​​and conditions create a consistent physical phenotype.

A deeper change occurs not in the value of the constant but in the type of state.A model has a certain symmetry, and that symmetry determines the acceptable particle representations and force carriers.Different symmetries can lead to different types of charges and different grammars of interaction.A model without the electrons and quarks we are familiar with can also be thought of mathematically, and a model with the same symmetry but a different mass spectrum is also possible.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Spectrum’]

Dimensions also do not need to be viewed only as a fixed background.The representation of field motions, wave propagation, distance dependence of forces, and rotational groups are dimension-sensitive.As the spatial dimension changes, the possibility of a stable structure also changes.However, there is no reason to immediately conclude here that three-dimensional space is the only possible one.This is because the allowable structure may vary depending on which conditions are important.

In our research, dimension is not simply a concept of counting the number of rooms.It is connected to how many directions independent relationships can be preserved and transmitted and what degrees of freedom the observable kinematics have.Instead of showing all possible directions, the dimensional filter started out as a device that selects and organizes directions that are physically realized under specific conditions.

In this case, the word ‘choice’ does not mean will.Just as we say that a magnet selects iron and a filter selects wavelengths, what we mean is that the structure passes the acceptance criteria and excludes the rest from the visible sector.Choice is not a hand outside the law, but an action within the law.

Even within the same general-purpose structure, different inputs and boundary conditions can produce different realizations.To put it simply, one universe instance can be represented by a combination of source data Ξ, renderer conditions Ω, and parameters and boundary conditions θ.The notation U = E(Ξ, Ω; θ) does not mean that the universe has been calculated with one equation, but is a map to separate what is a common structure and what is an instance input.

*U = E(Ξ, Ω; θ)* (1.1)

This distinction also changes the attitude toward using observational constants.Calibrating parameters with observed values ​​is not a failure of the theory.The Standard Model and General Relativity also use measured inputs.The problem is that the entered values ​​are later called predictions.So this book distinguishes between INPUT, CALIBRATED, DERIVED, and PREDICTED.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Standard Model of Particle Physics’]

The role of a universal model is not to create all numbers from scratch.First, it is necessary to find out what data can be input, whether the inputs do not conflict with each other, what relationships one value enforces between several physical quantities, and what is no longer free after correction.

In this way, the explanatory power of physical theory comes not from the absence of input, but from the compression of structure.Translating a hundred observations into a hundred independent functions adds little to the explanation.On the other hand, even if the same number of inputs are used, if they are placed under one common action and the permitted and prohibited relationships are revealed, a new organizing principle is obtained.

WRRA is an attempt to find this organizing principle.It does not first ask, ‘Why is the natural constant always this value?’Instead, it asks, ‘How does a universe with these values ​​realize mass, charge, spin, and position together in the same local reality?’

# Chapter 3 A different reality

We experience space in three directions.Front and back, left and right, and up and down are independent of each other, and objects move in these three directions.Time determines the order of movement, but it is not experienced in the same round-trip direction as space.This 3+1-dimensional structure is so familiar that it feels like the definition of reality.

But in mathematics, dimensions are much more common.The number of independent coordinates needed to specify a state, the size of the expression space in which the symmetry group operates, and the number of independent directions in which information can move can all be expressed in terms of dimension.The dimension of physics is a broader concept than simple visual direction.

As the dimension changes, the physical relationship also changes.The way the force spreads, the Green's function of the wave, the structure of rotation, the expression of spin and the engineering dimensions of the field change.A simple theory at one dimension may not be renormalizable at another dimension, and some topological structures only appear at certain dimensions.

However, we cannot directly say, ‘Because there are complex substances and observers, only three-dimensional space is possible.’The argument judges possibilities based on what we know about matter and life.We must first calculate whether different stability principles and different types of observability exist in other dimensions.

In Minimal Computing Cosmology, dimension becomes a key variable that distinguishes the size of the entire source from the size of visible reality.Even if the source has more degrees of freedom, the observable kinematics may be a compressed representation of some of them.Conversely, there is no need for the four visible dimensions to be stored as four completed spaces in the source.

WRRA describes the visible kinematics of our universe as D_visible = 4.This is the condition that fixes the currently observed 3+1 dimensional phenotype.However, this result does not extend to the claim that the four dimensions must uniquely emerge from all possible sources.

This difference is important.There is a huge logical gap between ‘our instances have four dimensions’ and ‘every possible instance must have four dimensions’.WRRA models the first sentence and does not target the second sentence.

Whether different renderer conditions can create different visible dimensions, whether the same source allows for multiple dimensional phenotypes, or whether different sources can be compressed into the same 3+1 dimensional reality are all open questions.These open problems are not deficiencies but rather research spaces defined by WRRA.

In this view, dimension becomes a property of embodied kinematics rather than the absolute appearance of the universe.We do not look directly at the dimension of the source itself, but measure the dimensionality operating on the local observable algebra and physical state space.

In the second half of the book we will see what conditions this compression must have in order not to lose its positivity.It is enough to remember one thing here.The four dimensions are an important characteristic of our universe that WRRA must explain, but they are not a unique proposition that closes off all possible realities.

# Chapter 4 A universe with different combinations of particles and forces

If you write down a list of what constitutes reality, the particle table of the Standard Model comes to mind.Quarks and leptons are arranged in three generations, photons, gluons, and W·Z bosons transmit force, and the Higgs field participates in electroweak symmetry breaking and mass creation.This structure has achieved tremendous experimental success.

However, the success of the Standard Model does not mean that its structure is the only logically possible quantum field theory.You can write theories with different gauge families, different material representations, different numbers of generations, and different masses and combinations.Many of them are rejected due to anomalies, negative probability, unstable vacuum, and non-locality, but no single mathematically consistent candidate is known.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Gauge Symmetry’]

Possibilities are not imagination without limits.In relativistic quantum theory, energy must not fall infinitely downward, the probability of physical states must not be negative, and distant local observations must maintain causal order.In a gauge theory, non-physical degrees of freedom and physical states must be distinguished, and the structure of necessary terms must be managed even after quantum correction.[See Appendix D ‘Causality and Delayed Response’]

These conditions reduce the likelihood of a universe, but do not necessarily make it a point.The space of feasible universes may be a model space with constraints, neither unlimited chaos nor the only correct answer.

In WRRA, particles are not defined as small beads attached to a source.Physical particles are the phenotypes allowed by the local observation algebra of the visible sector, the Poincaré expression, the gauge expression, and the spectral structure together.Stable isolated poles are one type of particle, but thresholds, resonances, and gauge invariant complexes are also physically manifest different types.

This definition is advantageous for dealing with other universe possibilities.In some instances, an isolated pole may appear as a stable particle, while in other instances the same source material may appear only as a broad resonance or continuous spectrum.The difference in realization is not simply a replacement of the particle list, but a difference in ‘what is seen as a physical object.’

Force is also not understood solely as a way to draw separate lines between particles.Gauge symmetry, local observable algebras, and coupled structures allow for specific interactions.Choosing different renderers and representations leaves open the possibility that the same source may have different visible binding structures.

Of course, there is still no evidence that this possibility has been realized in actual nature.This book does not create a list of universes without evidence and present it as fact.Instead, we separate step by step the structures allowed by the theory, those that are quantum consistent, and those that are consistent with our observations.

This separation is also necessary for new particle predictions.Adding random peaks to a spectrum and calling them new particles is not a prediction.When the conditions of positivity, symmetry, locality, and closure are simultaneously required, a pole or resonance that cannot be removed must remain.And the mass range and charge/spin/decay path must be fixed with the result not being used for calibration.

The purpose of thinking about possible universes is not to say that anything is possible.Rather, it is to strictly narrow down what is possible.WRRA's open universe view is not the absence of rules, but a structure that allows multiple realizations under common rules.

# Chapter 5 Computable Universe and Realizable Universe

Just because you can write down the solution to an equation does not mean it is a physical universe.Mathematical representability, computability, quantum consistency, physical realizability, and observational adequacy are different layers.When these layers are mixed, an error occurs where a beautiful equation looks like an explanation of nature, or a model that fits well observed values ​​looks like a fundamental theory.

In Minimal Computing Cosmology, ‘computation’ does not mean that an external computer runs the universe.This means that the laws of physics themselves are an inherent process of selecting, transforming, and preserving states.Therefore, there is no need to assume an extra-universe programmer or simulator.

The word minimum does not simply mean the smallest amount of information.The principle is to selectively implement necessary relationships and states without visually duplicating all unnecessary degrees of freedom in order to establish reality.Between the entire source and the reality of observation lies the organization of physical accessibility, not the erasure of information.

*U_possible ⊇ U_consistent ⊇ U_realized ⊇ U_observed* (1.2)

What you need to be most careful about at this time is loss during the compression process.If a positive state changes to a negative probability, different charge sectors are mixed incorrectly, or causality breaks down, the output cannot become a physical reality.So the renderer must be an interface that preserves physical tolerances, not just a data compressor.

WRRA expresses this condition in a positive selection or compression structure.The detailed math will be covered later, but the intuition is simple.Even if we make some parts visible in a larger space of possibilities, the dot product and probability interpretation of the visible states must remain consistent.

There is also a need for dynamics between the computable universe and the realizable universe.Just defining a state for a moment does not create a universe in which events continue.Time evolution must preserve the physical state space, and local observations must have consistent order and causal structure.

It also requires interaction.Constructing the spectrum of a free particle is different from maintaining the interaction theory under quantum correction.A model initially expressed with finite parameters may continue to require new terms after loop correction.This may be a failure of the model as a whole, but more often it is a boundary that sets the acceptable range for effective field theory.

*C_total = C_state + C_relation + C_boundary + C_update* (1.3)

Our study does not conceal this boundary.Some free quantum realizations of Bessel common carriers and positive spectral realizations can be constructed, but radical closure in the full strong sense does not automatically follow.Some realizations created the problem of infinitely continuous charged species, while other realizations of a single interacting spectral field provided a way around the problem.

Distinguishing between failed runtime and surviving runtime is itself a function of WRRA.Architecture is not doomed to one implementation method.There may be multiple runtimes producing the same visible phenotype, and we test which of them survive under interaction and quantum correction.

Therefore, feasibility is not achieved with a single declaration.It must pass through the gates of positivity, symmetry, locality, dynamics, interaction, renormalization, and observation matching in order.This book does not say that all gates have already been closed.Instead, it provides a map showing which gates are closed and which are open.

# Chapter 6 Universes we actually created

To not stop at describing possible universes, we created a multi-level model using dimension filters as a virtual-universe creation language.Rather than being a complete list of multiverses independently of one another, these are experimental systems that have gradually descended from universal creation rules into instances of our universe.

At the top was the generic virtual-universe dimensional filter.This layer explored how the formation of relationships, the emergence of dimensions, locality, and information transport could be expressed in a structure without pre-loading the constants of our universe.The goal was not to recreate a specific universe, but to establish a common language into which different inputs could be accepted.

In the next step we tried to close the kinematic interface.We confirmed that equivalence between inertial frames, Lorentz covariance, and energy-momentum relationships do not conflict with the dimensional filter output.This step was not to calculate all the masses and bonds of the particles, but to prepare an interface that could be equipped with visible 3+1-dimensional kinematics.

WJDF-OUM, or Wonsik-Jeongin Dimensional Filter Our Universe Model, was the first instance of a general-purpose model being specified for our universe.In the dimensional filter, 3+1 dimensions were realized, and layers leading to vacuum, boundary, relational geometry, and mass, charge, and interaction were placed.Here again, our universe was defined not as a privileged final solution but as a realization reflecting observations.

OUM's physics layer first dealt with gravity, electromagnetism, and charged leptons.Rather than trying to calculate all the contents of the Standard Model at once, we tested whether electrons, muons, tau, and electromagnetic representations could coexist in a common structure.Neutrinos, quarks, mixing matrices, and strong coupling were not brought into the completed results but were left as open items.

In the process, a simple Kaluza-Klein identification was also tested.An attempt was made to tie the charge and mass into one geometric quantity by placing the electron in the first mode n = 1 in the internal direction, but the actual electron mass and charge scale could not be satisfied at the same time.This route was closed as a NO-GO.

*K_DF = F†F, L_DF = I − K_DF* (1.4)

But the failure did not mean the abandonment of the entire geometric unity.Charge and mass are separated into mutually commutative projections of the common operator W.The charge has the electromagnetic expression Q_EM, and the mass has the leptonic mass Laplacian.It was a method that was based on common geometry but did not force the same eigenvalues.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Eigenvalues/Eigenvectors’]

The scale obtained from this crystal was kR ≈ 1.91766 × 10^-18 GeV^-1, and the inverse scale was approximately 5.21470 × 10^17 GeV.These numbers are derived from selected inputs and configurations and are not classified as independent natural constant predictions.

Afterwards, in WJDF-OUS v2.0, 『A Tuned Model of Our Universe Based on the Dimensional Filter』, the forced values ​​were fully audited.The spatial dimension d = 3, the time dimension t = 1, the speed of light c, α(0), the charged leptons and the W/Z/H masses, G_F, were declared as the inputs of our universe instance.In the physical scale layer, G, H₀, density fraction, and observation radius 46.5 Gly were also entered.

In the numerical experiment, a 3D periodic graph with 40×40 Hermitian W_eff and 1,000 vertices was used.The heat trace spectral dimension showed a plateau of d\* = 3, the calculated filter value was DF₃ = 0.17133483592264773, and the plateau section was \[158, 179).The error of the permutation regression test was 3.55 × 10^-14, which was smaller than the prior standard of 10^-10.

However, this figure does not mean that the 3D was predicted without input.This is a calibrated reproduction that confirms that the structure has been accurately reconstructed after entering the 3D graph and related settings.This distinction later led to WRRA's input audit principles.

Since the size of the entire universe was not observed, it was parameterized rather than set as a single fixed value.Internal derivation of the Planck scale, neutrino, quark, CKM, strong coupling, global topology, and cosmic dynamics of the source were also left as OPEN or HELD OUT.

What this model genealogy shows is not the fact that a single correct universe has been created.The fact is that we were able to isolate a universal generative language, an instance of our universe, a failed identification, a calibrated representation, and a still-empty physical layer.WRRA is the result of raising this separation to a more general source-renderer-phenotype structure.

# Chapter 7 Our universe is not special

The sentence ‘Our universe is not special’ does not mean that our universe is insignificant.The research principle is not to treat it as an exception to a special rule.This means that we will not add separate terms just for our universe or grant hidden privileges to general-purpose structures to obtain observed results.

This principle was already used as a central assumption in previous dimensional filter universe models.The question at the time was not ‘Why was only this universe chosen?’The question was how accurately our universe, which is one of the many cosmic structures allowed by the general dimensional filter model, can be expressed.

In our tuned model of the universe, observation constants are treated as observed locations in a generic parameter space.In other words, using constants was not a matter of forcing a miracle unique to our universe into a general-purpose equation, but rather specifying the location of our actual observations within the allowed parameter space.

Let's write this division in symbols.If the model space of universe instances allowed by WRRA is 𝔘, it can be written as 𝔘 = {E_WRRA(Ξ, Ω; θ) : (Ξ, Ω, θ) ∈ 𝒫}.Here, 𝒫 is not a set of random values, but a region that satisfies acceptance conditions such as positivity, causality, and locality.

Our universe lies in one instance: U_ours = E_WRRA(Ξ_ours, Ω_ours; θ_ours).This notation is not a declaration that all the data in our universe has already been calculated.It is a ledger for isolating and auditing the common structures and inputs of our universe.

The general-purpose structure provides relationships and acceptance conditions for source, renderer, and phenotype.Our universe instance has observational anchors such as electron mass, coupling constant, vacuum conditions, boundaries and raw data.Only when this input is not hidden can we see what is derived from the structure and what has been compensated for.

Therefore, proof of uniqueness is not a condition for success in this study.If we force that 3+1 dimensions, a specific gauge group, three generations, and current constants must be unique in all possible realities, we confuse the general model and the instance.

Instead, more productive questions arise.What is the minimal input that consistently constitutes an instance of our universe.What is the relationship that is automatically fixed after input?What observation result determines that the runtime of this instance or WRRA is wrong?

The non-privilege principle does not make verification lax.Rather, it makes it more stringent.No matter what the outcome is, it cannot be avoided by saying, ‘It’s because it’s a different universe.’The instances declared to describe our universe must match observations of our universe.The existence of other possible instances does not reduce this responsibility.

This principle is also distinct from the anthropocentric principle.Instead of explaining that the constants were chosen this way because humans exist, we first describe the physically acceptable structures and observational locations of our universe.Life and observer conditions can be studied later, but do not replace the definition of a general-purpose renderer.

Our universe is not special.However, it is irreplaceable in that it is the only universe that we can actually observe.The model space of the theory should be kept wide open, but verification should come from this single observed instance.This is a way to simultaneously maintain open possibilities and empirical responsibility.

# Chapter 8 Differences between the multiverse and possible universe models

When talking about possible universes, the multiverse immediately comes to mind.However, the two concepts are not the same.A possible universe model is a mathematical statement that certain equations and acceptance conditions can have different solutions or instances.The multiverse is an ontological claim that multiple instances of it actually physically exist.

The mere existence of several possible models does not follow from the actual existence of all models.Just because building codes allow multiple plans does not mean that all buildings are erected.Just because the score allows for multiple performances, it does not mean that all performances sound at the same time.

WRRA first deals with blueprints and playing conditions.It defines what possibilities the source provides, what physical parts the renderer selects and compresses, and what local reality the phenotype represents.This structure alone does not determine whether other sources and renderers actually exist.

This moderation is important.The multiverse may be philosophically attractive, but direct verification is difficult.WRRA's reliance on an untested ontology distracts from its original goal of explaining the physical manifestations of our universe.

*∃M : Consistent(M) ⇏ ∃x : Realized(x, M)* (1.5)

Therefore, this book does not say that ‘all possible universes exist.’He says, ‘We are not limited a priori to having only one way of realizing physically consistent reality.’The former is a declaration of existence, and the latter is an openness of research methods.

Model pluralism actually makes comparison possible.We can test whether stochastic positivity is broken without certain conditions, which symmetric expressions create anomalies and fall out, and which dimensions do not maintain the desired local structure.The other possibility is not a decoration that relativizes our universe, but a control that decomposes the conditions of our universe.

Now, the question is divided into two branches: ‘Why does the universe look like this?’First, what common principles define the feasibility of reality?Second, what input and renderer conditions does our universe have within that allowable space?The first question is directed to a universal WRRA, the second question is directed to a quantum and phenomenological realization of instances of our universe.

Between these two questions lies the central concept of this book.Not all possible states will soon be observed.Between the source and visible reality, a structure is needed to select and translate states while preserving physical tolerances.We call that structure the reality renderer.

The renderer is not an out-of-universe computer, not an observer's consciousness, or a random selector.It is an implicit architecture that jointly realizes the source possibilities as phenotypes with local observation algebras and physical Hilbert spaces, Poincaré and gauge representations.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Hilbert Space’]

The question that started from possibility is here narrowed down to the structure of reality.In the next part, we will follow the lineage of minimum computation and dimension filters and look at why a layer called renderer is needed between source and phenotype.

We are not looking for a unique blueprint for the universe.We are looking for the conditions that possible realities must share in order to become reality, and asking how our universe appears in a concrete form under those conditions.The possibilities don't end here.Now we are finally starting to get structure.

# Part 1 Boundaries and next questions

What is confirmed in this section is not the actual existence of other universes.What has been confirmed are the starting rules for the study.It does not presuppose the observed structure of our universe as the sole necessity of all possible realities.It separates the general model from the instances of our universe, allows observations as explicit inputs to the instances, but distinguishes them in derivation and prediction.

The remaining question is clear.If there are many possible states, why do we experience a coherent local reality of just one of them?What compresses the possibilities of the source into physical state space and jointly realizes mass, charge, spin, and position within one object?

In the next part, we follow the lineage of research from decimal operators to dimensional filters and common carriers.At the end of the journey, it will be revealed why a layer called RENDERER is needed between SOURCE and PHENOTYPE.

**Part 2**

**From possibility to reality**

The birth of minimum computation and dimensional filters

# Opening words of Part 2

If Part 1 opens up the various possibilities of the universe, Part 2 asks how those possibilities are narrowed down and implemented into one reality.This process is not a theory completed at once, but a research lineage formed through multiple hypotheses, failures, repairs, and redefinitions.

The grammar of the relationship was found in the independence of prime numbers, the visible direction was divided using a dimensional filter, and the dynamics and internal channels were connected using the Hamiltonian and the common carrier.Finally, the missing layer between the SOURCE and the observable object was called the RENDERER.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Hamiltonian’]

Each chapter distinguishes between established theorems and intuitive suggestions, calibrated models and open problems.The purpose of this part is not to declare WRRA a complete theory of everything, but to show in a reproducible sequence why such an architecture is needed.

# Chapter 9 Is it necessary to realize all possibilities at once?

Opening up the space of possible universes does not mean that all of those possibilities must become physical realities at the same time.Mathematics can allow for numerous states, but the observer always experiences events within a reality with some persistence and locality.The problem of selection and compression remains between the set of possibilities and the realized reality.

Selection here does not mean that an external designer selects the candidate.It is a process of removing incompatible relationships, leaving behind a preserveable structure, and updating a consistent state within limited resources.In reality, it may be a process of implementing necessary relationships at the moment they are needed, rather than a list of all answers calculated and stored in advance.

*R = Π_constraints(S_possible)* (2.1)

This idea differs from simulation cosmology, which asserts that the universe is a giant computer.Minimal computation cosmology does not assume extra-universe programmers or machines.The term ‘calculation’ is not limited to digital commands.It is a functional language that combines distinction of states, propagation of relationships, application of constraints, and updating of observable results.

The key question is not what should be counted, but what should not be counted.Reality can operate at a much lower representational cost if the necessary consistency and conservation can be maintained at the boundaries, without having to realize the details of every possible path independently.

*C_realized ≪ C_enumerated* (2.2)

Quantum mechanics stimulates this intuition.The state before measurement is described as an amplitude of possible outcomes rather than a complete list of classical properties, and interactions leave recordable outcomes on a certain basis.However, this book does not claim to have already solved the problem of interpretation of standard quantum mechanics.However, it raises the structural question that an implementation layer is needed between possibility and record.

Therefore, the minimal computation does not mean a lazy universe, but a universe that avoids duplication.Rather than creating the same relationship multiple times independently, multiple phenomena are organized together through common transmission and constraints.Subsequent dimensional filter and common carrier research attempted to translate this compression into a physical structure.

# Chapter 10 Minimal Computing Cosmology

The first principle of Minimal Computing Cosmology is that there is no reason for reality to classically determine and store all unobserved details.What is needed is not a state of nothing, but consistent rules between possible states and actual records.Therefore, the object of minimization includes not only the calculation method, but also the size of the source data, the order of reading the data, time evolution, and reality reproduction conditions.

The second principle is that minimizing the amount of computation does not simply mean minimizing the number of operations.It is a structural minimization that reduces not only the independent degrees of freedom that must be expressed, the information that must be conveyed, the constraints that must be maintained, and the range that must be recalculated when modified, but also the costs of initial loading and selective output.

C_min = min\[L_source + C_read + C_evolution + C_render + C_record\] (2.3)

The third principle is locality.If a small change in one point causes all technology in the entire universe to be rewritten from scratch, it is difficult for it to become a stable law of physics.Reality is updated through local interactions and limited transfers, and relationships in distant areas must be linked under common rules.

The fourth principle is reuse.If the mass, charge, spin, and position are made separately in four factories that are unrelated to each other and then attached to one particle, the binding rules explode.Explanation costs are reduced if multiple observations are jointly revealed in a common state and common transfer structure.

*ΔR(x) → ΔR(N_local(x))* (2.4)

In this view, laws are not complete scripts of all events, but compressed rules that limit the states that can be implemented.The initial conditions, boundary conditions, vacuum, and coupling constants specify one universe instance.Inputting observations is natural, but we must distinguish between input, structural derivation, and new predictions.

## A universe read once

From this perspective, physical laws and constants are not commands that are recalled from outside at every moment.The laws, constants and initial seeds recorded in SOURCE are read once at the beginning of the universe's execution and constitute an executable state.The subsequent time evolution is calculated autonomously based on the already loaded rules and current state.

(laws, constants, seed) ─BOOT→ Ψ₀, Ψ(t+Δt)=T_Δt\[Ψ(t)\]

Here, ‘once’ does not literally mean the scene where a computer outside the universe opens a file.It is a structural expression in which laws and constants do not require new external input at each time step and remain in effect within the initialized dynamics.In this way, the universe becomes a self-contained execution system that does not depend on external calls.

Therefore, Minimal Computing Cosmology covers the entire process of storage-reading-execution-output-recording.The description length of the source L_source, activation sequence C_read, autonomous evolution C_evolution, optional output C_render at the observation boundary and irreversible record C_record are included in one cost ledger.

## Information is provided in six ways

Just because we ask about a certain physical quantity does not mean that the answer must all be contained in numbers in the SOURCE.The WRRA information provider divides the path through which the answer appears in reality into six levels: INPUT, DERIVED, CALIBRATED, OBSERVED, OPEN EVENT, and OPEN.This distinction prevents both the error of calling a value already entered a prediction and the error of calling an event that has not yet occurred a pre-stored future.

I_direct={law, constant, initial relationship, topology, boundary}, I_derived(t)=E_WRRA^t\[I_direct\], I_observed(Q,t)=R_Q\[I_derived(t)\]

For example, the speed of light and Planck's constant could be INPUTs read once from the law header.The current distribution of matter and the age of the universe are DERIVED calculated from the initial state and dynamics.At this stage, the electron mass and some coupling constants are CALIBRATED based on observed values.A specific electronic detection location is an OPEN EVENT, which is added as a record when an event occurs within the probability distribution.The correct final SOURCE itself is still OPEN.

The key point is that the level of information is not a permanent status.Mass values ​​that are CALIBRATED today can be moved to DERIVED in the future if deeper generation rules are discovered that do not use the same observations again.Scientific progress lies not in adding more values, but in independently deriving the information that was left as input.

## What is the difference between simulation cosmology and the standard model?

The three perspectives do not compete on the same level.Rather than being a unified standard theory, simulation cosmology is a set of philosophical and computational hypotheses that suggest that the universe can be run on an external computer.The Standard Model is a proven quantum field theory that precisely describes particles and the strong, weak, and electromagnetic forces.Minimal Computing Cosmology is a research program that asks what information structure, execution order, and selective output conditions a universe instance, including these laws and constants, constitutes reality.

|**Classification** |**Minimal Computing Cosmology** |**Simulation Cosmology** |**Standard Model** |
|-----------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
|**Personality** |A research program that questions the cost and order of realizing reality |A group of hypotheses that assume the execution of external calculations |Proven quantum field theory that describes the material phenotype and internal dynamics of our universe |
|**Key Questions** |How to realize one reality with few sources and output |Is our universe a simulation of another computational system?With what symmetries and dynamics do the realized phenotypes of particles, fields, and three basic forces operate? |
|**External Executor** |Not necessary.Aims to implement self-completeness within the universe |In general, a higher-level calculator or execution environment is assumed |don't assume |
|**Law/Constant** |It is loaded once with the initial boot input and its ownership/size is audited |It can be viewed as the code/parameters of the simulator |Some of the parameters of the phenotype are received as observation input and calculated as RG flow |
|**Time Evolution** |Autonomous calculation based on loaded rules and current state |Depends on external computational resources and implementation methods |Expression follows Lagrangian and quantum field theory rules |
|**Observation** |Selectively output local reality from recordable interactions |The analogy of rendering only necessary scenes is possible, but it is not a necessary proposition |The observable quantities and event probabilities of the phenotype are precisely calculated, but the measurement interpretation is not fixed to one |
|**Forecast Status** |Currently, structural hypothesis, partial calculation, and boundary verification are mixed |Making unique and falsifiable predictions is difficult |Many precise predictions confirmed experimentally |
|**Gravity·Cosmology** |Integration and early universe connection are still OPEN |Depends on the selected simulation rules |It does not include gravity and is not the ultimate SOURCE of phenotypes or a complete cosmology |

In this comparison, Minimal Computing Cosmology does not claim to discard or replace the Standard Model.Rather, for a renderer of our universe to be successful, it must reproduce the symmetries, particle catalogs, and precise observations that the Standard Model already describes.The difference is not in transferring the parameters of the standard model to a different symbol, but in expanding the size and ownership of the input, reading order, execution and observation records into a single computational ledger.

The name Minimum Computation is not a theorem that has been finalized, but the name of a research program.It is open what to count as cost, how to define it in quantum field theory and gravity, and how it is related to entropy, action, and circuit complexity.This book moves on to the next structure without hiding its imperfections.

In this implementation, SOURCE includes physical laws and constants, initial complex seeds, dependency sequences, boundaries, SOURCE-to-state interface, and event queries.Independent items can be read in parallel, but downstream objects cannot be read before their owner and parser are loaded.After boot, the actual state evolves autonomously without an observer, and classical images are optionally constructed when a stable and shareable physical record is required.

Universe = BOOT\[law, constants, schema\] + EVOLVE\[Actual\] + RENDER\[record query\] + APPEND\[event\]

# Chapter 11 From prime numbers to relational grammar

In the early days of research, there was the idea that prime numbers could be the seeds of independent cycles and dimensions.Composite numbers can be decomposed into combinations of prime factors, but prime numbers are not reducible to the product of cycles of smaller integers.This arithmetic independence seemed similar to the independence of physical degrees of freedom.

From this intuition, the exploration of the Prime Operator and WJNS series began.By giving each prime number a unique phase or cycle direction and reading composite numbers as a combination between those directions, the structure of numbers can be transformed into a network of relationships.A sequence is not a list but a grammar of creation and combination.

*n = ∏ₚ p^νₚ(n) ⇒ composite = coupled prime relations* (2.5)

However, arithmetic analogy is not physics.The mere fact that prime numbers are independent does not lead to spatial dimensions or particles.Exploring the connection with the Riemann hypothesis does not provide proof.The outcome of this stage was a change in research language rather than a correct answer.

The change was significant.This is because, instead of directly assigning the properties of an object, the independent source and the relationship between them are placed first.A way was created to ask which directions are preserved, which combinations overlap, and which boundaries create observable patterns.

The Minority Program is therefore not a complete version of WRRA, but rather a starting point for its genealogy.Independence became a candidate language for SOURCE, combination became the grammar for RELATION, and the actual remaining patterns shifted to the problem of BOUNDARY records.

This book does not exaggerate its genealogy.It does not say that the standard model has been calculated from prime numbers, nor does it say that arithmetic uniquely enforces the universe.However, it is recorded that the idea of ​​separating independent terms and combined terms gave rise to the dimensional filter and renderer structures.

# Chapter 12 The birth of dimensional filters

How do observable directions come about when there are many possible directions?Dimensional filters were the first structural answer to this question.A filter is not a device that creates dimensions out of nothing, but an operation that passes through those parts of relationship candidates that exist at the source that can be consistently transmitted and recorded locally.

When filter F acts on the source state, K_DF=F†F can be read as the effective kernel of the passing section, and L_DF=I−K_DF can be read as the complementary section that does not pass.This notation does not mean that a specific micro-theory has been completed, but is a ledger that clarifies ownership of the visible and invisible sectors.〔Refer to Appendix D ‘Kernel’〕

*K_DF = F†F, L_DF = I − K_DF* (2.6)

In the dimensional filter study, we performed a numerical experiment in which a 3D structure was input and heat trace and spectrum indicators were used to stably reconstruct it.This is meaningful as a regression test of the algorithm, but it is not the result of predicting 3D with no input.Recovering the input structure and why nature chose it are two different arguments.

Because of this distinction, proof of uniqueness is not a necessary goal of research.Our universe may be one of the possible universes.The task of a dimensional filter is not to declare that only 3+1 dimensions are possible, but to make it possible to calculate which valid dimensions appear reliably given the source and boundary conditions.

*d_eff(E) = −2 · d log Tr(e^(−tK_DF)) / d log t* (2.7)

Dimensions are no longer empty boxes in the background, but are now the number of independent directions in which the relationship continues.This perspective does not place dimensions as a separate preceding stage from particles or forces, but deals with them together within the structure of transmission and observation.

But filters alone weren't enough.Even if you specify a subspace to pass through, it does not determine how the state moves over time.A Hamiltonian bridge was needed to go from static selection operators to dynamic physics.

## Dimensional filter to WRRA

The dimensional filter does not remain as a separate theory competing with the current WRRA.The dimension selection and representation transformation that were initially handled by the dimensional filter were preserved as the first implementation module inside WRRA, and SOURCE's dependency order reading, common propagation, joint attribution of properties, autonomous evolution, selective realization, and stable record formation were added.

Dimensional Filter ⊂ WRRA

The initial one-dimensional point is not fixed to a point in already visible physical space.It is the minimum address of the SOURCE relationship structure.The dimensional filter inside WRRA converts the allowed relationships between these addresses into visible three-dimensional geometry and expression direction, and executes the remaining WRRA modules so that mass, charge, spin, and position are attributed to the same causal structure.

SOURCE address → dimensional selection → common transport → lawful phenotype → stable record

# Chapter 13 SOURCE-RELATION-BOUNDARY

As the language of dimensional filters was expanded, three basic terms were fixed.SOURCE is a repository of possible independent components and potential states.RELATION refers to how components combine, constrain each other, and convey information.BOUNDARY is a place where the process interacts with the outside world and leaves a recordable difference.

SOURCE should not be understood only as a material warehouse.It is not yet a collection of particles whose mass, charge, and location have all been determined.It is the source of possibilities and freedom necessary for implementation.Conversely, BOUNDARY does not only mean the physical end of the universe.Contains all valid interfaces through which one system writes results to another system.

RELATION is not a simple line connecting two floors.Symmetry, topology, combination, selection rules, conservation laws, and transfer kernels all belong here.It determines which source states are the same or different, and determines which combinations can be realized together and which combinations are prohibited.

*Ω = (SOURCE, RELATION, BOUNDARY)* (2.8)

These three terms become a universal framework for describing various universes.Different universe instances can be created if the type of SOURCE, the symmetry and combination of RELATION, and the recording conditions of BOUNDARY are different.Our universe is one of the observed settings.

*U_i = E(Ξ_i, R_i, B_i; θ_i)* (2.9)

## SOURCE does not store all answers directly

For each questionRather than storing the answer as just a single number, it also preserves where that number came from.

P(X)=(owner, payload, execution map, trigger, record, status, precision)

Here, owner is the layer that first has independent information.The law can be owned by the LAW HEADER, the initial relationship and global phase of each universe can be owned by SOURCE, the particle expression rule can be owned by the phenotype grammar, the evolution of the current state can be owned by the execution engine, and the specific observation record can be owned by the renderer and event instrument.If the same information is stored repeatedly in multiple layers, it violates the minimal-computation principle.

Therefore, the one-dimensional point in SOURCE is not a small physical point in three-dimensional space, but the address of a relational input.The three directions of the visible space are the structural output of the dimensional filter in WRRA that converts the relationship into an expression space.If the global connection method of space, the topology, the initial curvature scale, and the boundary data are not determined by dynamics alone, that part remains as a conditional INPUT of the space instance.

Initially, this trichotomy seemed sufficient.But as soon as the physical object appeared, a problem arose.The intermediate process of converting the possibility of a source into a boundary record was too much pressed into one word.The mere fact that they are related does not mean that mass, charge, spin, and position are jointly realized as a single object.

This omission later gets the name RENDERER.But before that, it was necessary to make the static structure of the filter dynamic and bind several internal channels under a single transfer action.

# Chapter 14 From filter to Hamiltonian

While a filter tells us what passes through, the Hamiltonian tells us how the passed state changes.If the two layers are not connected, the dimensional filter can remain in a classifier or projector, but it is difficult to become a physical model with energy, stability, transition, and scattering.

In the study, a bridge was designed that connects the vacuum candidate V₀(θ), the effective Hamiltonian H_B(θ), and the Green function G(E,θ) at the resolution E according to the filter parameter θ.These three terms in turn are responsible for the selected subspace, the dynamics on it, and the response to external stimuli.

*F(θ) → V₀(θ) → H_B(θ) → G(E,θ)* (2.10)

V₀(θ) records which background among the possible vacuums serves as the basis for the instance.H_B(θ) determines the time evolution and energy structure of the allowed state on the background.G(E,θ) shows what propagates at a given energy and where poles or resonances appear.

*G(E,θ) = \[E + i0 − H_B(θ)\]⁻¹* (2.11)

This bridge also organizes the positions of observation constants.Some values ​​are forced by the structure, some values ​​are correction inputs that specify vacuum and boundary conditions, and some values ​​are outputs calculated after fixing the inputs.Although it is natural to use known constants for corrections, it is important not to count corrections back as predictions.

The Standard Model is also not a theory that numerically predicts all parameters without observation.Although there are strong constraints on the symmetries, field contents, and forms of interaction, many values ​​of mass, mixing, and coupling are determined experimentally.The value of the new structure lies not in hiding the inputs, but in which relationships are enforced after the inputs and which new observations remain independent.

Currently, the bridge is in the stage of design principles and partial calculations.It cannot be claimed that complete action, renormalization, unitarity, causality, and standard model precise data are closed at the same time.Therefore, Part 2 is not complete, but rather specifies the technical thresholds that WRRA must address.

# Chapter 15 Common Carrier and 15 channels

The next problem was the internal degree of freedom.Describing a generation of chiral materials requires multiple orientations with different charges and expressions.Rather than dispersing the 15 channels into separate worlds, the study explored a structure in which one common continuum carries them all.

Common carrier D_C is a device that places all 15 directions under the same transportation law.Channels may have different internal landmarks, but local transport, kinematics, and boundary records share a common framework.This applies the reuse principle of minimum computation to particle content.

*D_C : ⊕ᵢ₌₁¹⁵ H_i → ⊕ᵢ₌₁¹⁵ H_i* (2.12)

Early democratic rank-one combinations had the problem of delivering only one bright combination and leaving the remaining 14 directions dark.Just because a symmetrical shape is beautiful does not automatically result in a sufficient physical rank.This failure was recorded as a counterexample that should be closed down.

*rank(D_C│band) = 15* (2.13)

The conservative direction was not to discard the common carrier, but to add a rank-opening bond.A continuum must preserve the overall band rank while preserving the charge and selection rules for each channel.Commonality and distinguishability must be satisfied at the same time.

The serial rotor concept also came from this.This is not a sequential switch that mechanically turns on channels one by one, but a structure in which an autonomously rotating rank-one port relates to all channels through phase encoding.It is not a chronological list, but phase-coded seriality over a common transport.

*P(ϕ) = \|v(ϕ)⟩⟨v(ϕ)\|, rank P(ϕ)=1* (2.14)

15 channels does not mean that all final particle mass and mixing are accounted for.It is a candidate boundary record of one generation chiral carrier, and generation replication, Yukawa, quark mixing, strong coupling dynamics and upstream generation method of mass remain as separate layers.However, in Chapter 30, low integer quantum relationships are found and frozen in the observed first generation values, and candidates for the absolute mass of neutrinos that have not yet been directly measured are presented.This is not an independent upstream derivation, but a phenomenological prediction awaiting verification.

# Chapter 16 Hardware–Firmware–Software–Execution

One of the reasons complex physical models falter is that they mix the responsibilities of different explanatory layers.In the WRRA genealogy, it is divided into four layers: Hardware, Firmware, Software, and Execution.This does not mean that the computer is outside the universe, but is a metaphor for structural separation of ownership.

Hardware is responsible for what exists and what directions can be connected, such as expression and charge ratio, 15 channels, and common transport.When this layer changes, the particle content and the accepted expression itself change.

The firmware is responsible for spectral weighting, quantification of kinetic terms, activation of poles, matching and vacuum selection.Even with the same hardware, which modes are visible at low energy and which combinations are effective depends on this layer.

Software takes care of specific patterns of materials such as Yukawa structure, generation, mixing angle and CP phase.If the observed mass and mixing were simply changed and transferred to this layer, it would only be a re-expression.New theoretical achievements require reducing independent inputs or computing relationships that have not yet been written.

*M_Y = Y(Hardware, Firmware; θ_software)* (2.15)

Execution is a calculation that adapts this structure to the actual observation scale, such as renormalization-group flow, threshold, and pole mass.Even when talking about matching observations after quantum realization, a distinction must be made between simple number matching and physical execution.This is the reason for dividing correction data and post-verification data.

*O_obs(μ) = RG_μ ∘ Threshold ∘ Pole\[M_Y\]* (2.16)

This hierarchy positions research failures rather than hiding them.Even if the hardware is correct, the software may be open, and even if there is a tree level relationship, execution may be broken.Conversely, a good reproduction of observed values ​​does not prove the hardware principle.

# Chapter 17 Why you need a renderer

After going through the dimensional filter, Hamiltonian, common carrier, and class distinction, the last omission became clear.Mass can be defined separately in the mass operator, charge in gauge expression, spin in rotation and Lorentz expression, and position in local algebra.However, actual detectors do not observe these four properties separately and then arbitrarily attach them to one particle.

A physical object is a pattern of events to which mass, charge, spin, and position are jointly attributed.Therefore, the identity of an object must not be the sum of several lists of properties, but rather a joint phenotype that holds together under the same local observational algebra and the same translation operation.

*Phenotype = Joint(m, q, s, x │ A_local, T_a)* (2.17)

This is why a renderer is needed between SOURCE and BOUNDARY.A renderer is not an ornament that paints a picture of possibilities.It is an implementation architecture that binds different degrees of freedom into compatible common states, preserves locality, causality, symmetry, and conservation laws, and exports reproducible records at the boundary.

At this time, phenotype is not a literal borrowing of biological phenotype.It refers to the result of internal sources and relationships appearing as actual observable physical objects under specific environments and boundary conditions.Even for the same general-purpose renderer, different phenotype universes are possible if the instance input and vacuum are different.

Therefore, the basic flow of WRRA is summarized as SOURCE → RENDERER → PHENOTYPE.RELATION and BOUNDARY provide the renderer's operational syntax and recording interface.Dimensional filters and common carriers are candidates for their internal modules, and responsibility layers are divided from Hardware to Execution.

*PHENOTYPE = RENDERER(SOURCE; RELATION, BOUNDARY, θ)* (2.18)

This structure is not yet proof of the final physical theory.The current state is a research program with conceptual architecture, partial mathematical models, numerical regression testing, and closed paths and open layers of failure.However, it is now clearer than before what the theory is asking, how far it is closed, and what needs to be verified.

*SOURCE → RENDERER → PHENOTYPE* (2.19)

## Is observation a request for output of reality?

Even before observation, the temporal evolution of the internal state continues.What Minimal Computing Cosmology saves is not the existence itself, but the cost of rendering all possibilities into one complete classical image at every moment.When a recordable interaction occurs, the renderer outputs to reality only the state at that point and the local results allowed by the observation interface.

Y_a(t) = O_a\[Ψ(t)\]

Therefore, the observer is not a being who freely creates reality outside the universe.Physical interactions, including the observation device and the environment, form the output conditions, and the results are limited by the combination of internal state, Born rule, conservation law, and observation algebra.The intuition that ‘an image is output when someone looks at it’ is not translated as consciousness-centered magic, but as a selective realization proposition that common reality is broken down only in recordable interactions.

SOURCE → READ ORDER → AUTONOMOUS EVOLUTION → SELECTIVE REALIZATION → SHARED RECORD

# Part 2 Boundaries and next questions

What Part 2 shows is that possibility is not reality.The dimensional filter divides the visible sector, the Hamiltonian gives the dynamics above it, and the common carrier binds several internal channels under one transport.However, even if all of these modules work, they must account for the joint identity of the observable objects.

Currently, WRRA is not a complete standard model replacement.The part where a known constant is translated into a different symbol will be classified as a re-expression, the part where the observed value is matched will be labeled as a correction, and only the independent prediction for data that has not been entered will be called a prediction.Proof of a unique universe is not even a goal.

In the next part, SOURCE, RENDERER, and PHENOTYPE are formally defined, and the internal modules of the renderer and the order of quantum realization, observation correction, and falsifiable prediction are established.Leaving open possibilities is not about hiding flaws; it is about indicating where research will actually begin.

# Opening words of Part 3

Part 3 fixes the intuition formed earlier as the minimum specification of WRRA and examines how that specification can be implemented in the language of existing physics.At the center is the renderer path independence paper published on August 27, 2026, v1.1, which repairs this with restorability and metric faithfulness, and v1.2, which deals with time uniformity stability of repeated rendering.

The point of this section is not to declare a particular five-dimensional model to be the ultimate reality of the universe.The 5-dimensional parent is one of the possible renderers, and the Einstein gravity is also a conditional low-energy branch selected under explicit additional assumptions.

For WRRA to survive, it must go beyond beautiful interpretation and show observational equivalence of different implementation paths, anomaly-free constraint closure, and prediction for uninput data.Part 3 records the currently closed structure and the boundaries of the next verification.

# Chapter 18 What does WRRA try to explain?

Wonsik Reality-Renderer Architecture, or WRRA, does not assume that any underlying source is exactly the same as the reality we observe.The source may have more degrees of freedom, continuums, and potential relationships, and the observable 3+1 dimensional reality is the result of their joint realization into one local, coherent form.

In this view, reality is not a copy of the source but a phenotype.Just as the same genetic information in biology appears as specific traits depending on the expression process and environment, physical sources also appear as objects with mass, charge, spin, and position after passing the renderer's rules and boundary conditions.The purpose of the analogy is not to forcefully translate biology into physics, but to separate the layers of implementation between the source and the observed results.

The minimum structure of WRRA is three layers: SOURCE, RENDERER, and PHENOTYPE.SOURCE provides possible degrees of freedom and potential states, RENDERER applies acceptance relations and locality, symmetry, transfer, and recording conditions, and PHENOTYPE is revealed as an observable physical reality.

*P = R(S; RELATION, BOUNDARY, θ)* (3.1)

RELATION and BOUNDARY are included in RENDERER.RELATION determines what can be combined and preserved, and BOUNDARY is the interface where the results interact with other systems and remain as recordable differences.The dimensional filter and common forwarder are candidate modules for the renderer and are not identical to the entire WRRA.

Therefore, WRRA does not require the ontology that ‘the universe is a computer simulation’.Without assuming an external computer or programmer, we can ask about a structure that is implemented as an observational reality with limited source possibilities.Minimum computation is an operating principle where the renderer avoids duplication and reuses common relationships.

What WRRA has currently frozen is a minimum specification, not a single final action.Candidate physical implementations are tested to ensure that they meet this specification, and if a particular 5D model or Bessel carrier fails, the entire architecture does not automatically collapse.Conversely, just because one candidate works does not mean that the entire WRRA can be proven.

*WRRA = {S, R, P;compatibility, locality, recordability}* (3.2)

# Chapter 19 Co-realized physical objects

Let's consider one electron.We can measure the mass of an electron in one experiment, its charge in another, and its spin in another.However, electrons in nature do not possess these properties separately as unrelated labels.All properties are jointly attributed to the same event and the same propagation history.

WRRA calls this joint attribution the joint phenotype.A physical object is not simply a list of eigenvalues ​​of mass operators, gauge expressions, Lorentz expressions, and local positions in one line.They must be compatible and persist under the same local observation algebra and the same translation operation.

*P_joint = (m, q, s, x) │ (A_local, T_a)* (3.3)

Stable particles can appear as poles on the real axis, and charged particles such as electrons can appear as infraparticle thresholds instead of strictly single particle poles due to soft photon clouds.Unstable particles are described analytically as extended resonance poles, and proton-like composites are realized as gauge-invariant composite states.The renderer must distinguish between these different spectral ownerships.

*stable pole ⊕ infraparticle threshold ⊕ resonance ⊕ composite* (3.4)

Without this distinction, errors arise such as misreading the resonance width while saying that mass has been created, equating the gauge-dependent field with a physical particle, or treating the mass of a composite as if it were a fundamental field parameter.WRRA's ledger first asks which spectral sector each observation belongs to.

Co-realization also includes location issues.If the charge and mass of an object are defined at different space-time locations, it cannot become a detectable particle.All properties must move and react together at the same translation history and local interaction point.

This is why a renderer is not just a classifier.The filter can decide whether to pass or not, but the renderer must bind the passed degrees of freedom into a single physical identity and preserve it over time.

# Chapter 20 Visible Interface and Hidden Continuum

WRRA v1.0 divides between visible reality and hidden sources with a self-adjoint block architecture.The visible sector is the interface we read as particles and fields, while the hidden sector may include broader continuums or degrees of freedom that have not yet been directly observed.

If we divide the entire Hamiltonian into blocks of visible space and hidden space, the effective dynamics of the visible sector are not the result of simply deleting the hidden sector.Through resolvent, the hidden part returns to self-energy and spectral density.This structure provides a standard way to preserve the self-involvement of the entire operator while preserving the effects of open systems.

*H = \[\[H_vis, V\], \[V†, H_hid\]\]* (3.5)

*G_vis(z) = \[z − H_vis − V(z−H_hid)⁻¹V†\]⁻¹* (3.6)

The important point is that it is not assumed that the hidden continuum is dark matter or another universe.It is the mathematical ownership of degrees of freedom outside the visible interface of the renderer implementation.Actual physical interpretations must be verified separately using pole, threshold, scattering data, and cosmological observations.

Bessel common carriers are one candidate for this hidden continuum.This is an illustration of a single compressed spectral channel carrying several internal directions in common and emitting valid states of matter at the boundaries.The name Bessel comes not from an external program but from a specific differential equation and its spectral structure.

However, it should not be assumed that exact Bessel transport remains the same in all sectors.Scalar stabilization, gauge interactions, defects and boundary conditions can modify the spectrum.Therefore, it is necessary to distinguish between the correct Bessel core, the deformed sector, and the low-energy matching area.

The advantage of this block structure is that it localizes failure.If the UV behavior of a particular hidden continuum fails, the implementation can be discarded, but there is no need to discard WRRA's higher-level question of separating the source and visible phenotypes.

# Chapter 21 Quantum Realization Has Multiple Layers

There is not one path to quantum realization of the Bessel common carrier.Research genealogy distinguished three levels.The first is a finite Jacobi runtime, the second is a single generalized-field spectral runtime, and the third is a warped defect-holographic parent.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Jacobean Matrix’]

*Jacobi runtime → spectral field → warped defect parent* (3.7)

A finite Jacobi runtime approximates a continuous spectrum with a finite matrix and discrete states.It is the most conservative, computable, and local quantum runtime.It is good to numerically fit the response of poles and residues and limited energy windows, but it does not preserve the exact infinite continuum.

A single generalized field preserves the continuum compressed within a single non-local or pseudodifferential spectral symbol.Although it is possible to express the target two-point function without introducing an infinite number of independent charged fields, closure of the interaction subgraph, gauge completion, and renormalization are separate thresholds.

Warped defect parents are one step more geometric.Higher-order local fields produce Bessel-type spectra at boundaries or defects.Although it may give rise to claims of bulk causality and positive spectral compression, this does not mean that it is a fundamental dimension of nature.

Even though the three runtimes reproduce the same amount of two-point spectral target in a limited matching window, they are not physically the same theory.The Hilbert space in which the number of fields, locality, gauge completion, ultraviolet ray cost and unitarity are defined is different.

*G₁(E) ≈ G₂(E) on E∈W ⇏ Theory₁ = Theory₂* (3.8)

Therefore, the statement that ‘the quantum realization stage is over’ must be said on a layer-by-layer basis.Two-point representation, interaction consistency, one-loop relative closure, all-loop completion, and standard model embedding are different thresholds.The current study has closed some layers, but has not completed the entire quantum gravity.

# Chapter 22 A Possible Local Standard-Model Parent

In the quantum realization genealogy, the 5-dimensional Einstein–scalar parent was presented as the first explicit local implementation chassis of WRRA.It is a structure that stabilizes the finite warped interval with a scalar boundary condition and places a Bessel radial core and a chiral standard model zero mode on it.

*ds² = e^(−2A(y))η_μνdx^μdx^ν + dy²* (3.9)

The advantage of this model is that length, gap, chirality, and transmission can be treated together in one local field theory framework.A finite Kaluza–Klein gap separates the low-energy visible sector from the heavy mode, and the generation-universal transport is structured in a way that preserves the observed Yukawa·CKM·PMNS data.

However, ‘preserve’ and ‘predict’ are different.Inserting the observed Yukawa and mixed data as matching input and maintaining it at low energy is a test of feasibility of implementation.The figure was not derived from geometry alone.

*O_low(μ) = Match_μ\[O_parent;θ_obs\]* (3.10)

Additionally, low-energy theories should not be defined by simple Kaluza–Klein cuts.Gauge-covariant matching and threshold correction are required, and the scalar-deformed sector and exact Bessel sector must be separated.Stability, anomaly inflow, flavor counterterm, BRST and cutoff corridor conditions must also be met.

Therefore, the exact status of the 5-dimensional parent is a possible local implementation chassis.It is not the final body of WRRA, nor is it a structure necessarily used by nature, nor is it a fundamental theory that is valid at all scales.

This limitation is not only a weakness.The fact that one of the possible renderers was actually built in an existing field theory language means that the architecture does not remain pure metaphor.Now the deeper question is, what conditions are necessary for different implementation paths to yield the same observational reality?
