# Chapter 23 Renderer path independence

Let's say we have two rendering paths that start from the same physical boundary and end up at the same boundary.Even if the intermediate slice and gauge selection and decomposition order are different, the final observation content should not change.WRRA calls this renderer path equivalence or path independence.

*R_γ₁(B_f,B_i) ≃\_gauge R_γ₂(B_f,B_i)* (3.11)

This principle does not mean that all intermediate expressions must be literally identical.Differences corresponding to gauge redundancy are allowed, but gauge-invariant observables must match.Therefore, path independence is a condition that separates the overlap of observations and expressions.

When small deformations are applied in different orders, the differences should be closed back to the permitted deformations.If a local slice can be embedded in a non-degenerate space-time geometry, a hypersurface-deformation algebra provides the kinematics of this deformation.

In the canonical representation, these transformations appear as first-class constraints.If the constraint algebra is not closed, different slicing paths create different physical states or leave behind an anomaly.Then the renderer cannot consistently implement the same reality.

*{H\[N\],H\[M\]} = D\[qᵃᵇ(N∂\_bM−M∂\_bN)\]* (3.12)

However, it would be an exaggeration to say that path independence itself directly creates the Einstein equation.It requires the necessary gauge and deformation structures.Which dynamics branch is chosen depends on the canonical variables used, locality, differential order and additional degrees of freedom.

This principle changes WRRA in a verifiable direction.The difference between boundary observables calculated in different renderer paths can be measured, and if constraint anomaly or source-clock leakage remains, the implementation will fail.

# Chapter 24 Restorable renderer and metric faithfulness

When path equivalence is applied to quantum renderers, a new problem arises.General quantum channels are not reversible.Even a fully positive, trace-preserving CPTP map can make two different states more similar, erasing location-distinguishing information and metric rank.

Root fidelity does not decrease under the CPTP map.Therefore, the distance defined by fidelity shrinks, and the information geometry metric derived from a sufficiently smooth state family can also become small in the output.The mere fact that it is a quantum channel does not preserve spatial orientation and localization.

*F(Φρ, Φσ) ≥ F(ρ,σ) ⇒ g_out ≤ g_in* (3.13)

WRRA v1.1 does not solve this problem with the inverse function of the entire renderer.Specifies the code subspace or observable algebra that needs to be physically protected, and requires that a recovery map exist only in that area.Restoration is not about reviving the entire microstate, but about preserving the logical information necessary for visible reality.

*Recovery ∘ Φ = id on A_protected* (3.14)

At this time, three conditions must be distinguished.First, localization rank must be preserved.Second, the output state family must achieve faithful immersion in a common corrected space-time geometry.Third, the protected observable algebra must be restored after recovery, including the intended logical time evolution.

Path equivalence changes to recovered path equivalence.The raw output of two different paths need not be the same.After appropriate recovery of each path, the same intended transition with the same physical content must be obtained in the protected observation log.

*R₁∘Φ_γ₁ ≃ R₂∘Φ_γ₂ on A_protected* (3.15)

Here, invariants and physical transitions should not be confused.Flavor mixing and decay do not preserve the original state.What the renderer must preserve is not the fact that there is no change, but that the allowed sector-changing evolution and its probability, charge, and local records are implemented correctly.For this, Fock space or direct-sum sector may be required.

# Chapter 25 Maintaining the same reality over time

We cannot immediately conclude that a renderer that is restorable at one point in time will maintain the same reality over time.Even if the quantum channels at each stage can be restored individually, if small errors accumulate repeatedly, the protected observation algebra and local geometry may change after a long time.Snapshot recoverability and long-time stability are different thresholds.

The most direct sufficient condition is that the disturbance size ε_k at each stage can be summed.If the cumulative error up to n steps is controlled by the sum of the errors at each step and the infinite sum is finite, the error can be kept within a time-independent range even after repeated rendering.This is a stronger condition than saying that each step is small.

*‖E_n‖ ≤ Σ\_(k=1)^n ε_k, Σ\_(k=1)^∞ ε_k \< ∞* (3.16)

v1.2 distinguishes three sufficient mechanisms for time-uniform restoration.There is a summable disturbance in which the sum of the disturbances is finite, a noiseless observable algebra in which the protected observable algebra is exactly preserved, and a contractive recovery in which the state after recovery is contracted to the attractor code manifold.The three mechanisms can replace each other or be used together.

In shrinkage reconstruction, the current error is reduced to less than λ times at each step and a new disturbance ε_k is added.If λ, which is above 0 but below 1, remains independent of time, past errors are exponentially forgotten, and under limited new perturbations, the state stays around the code manifold.However, it should not be a restoration that stops the intended logical time development.

*d\_(k+1) ≤ λd_k + ε_k, 0 ≤ λ \< 1* (3.17)

The fact that the metric rank at one point in time is 3 does not guarantee a continuous three-dimensional space.Uniform ellipticity is required, where the effective metrics at all points in time are uniformly limited above and below a common reference metric.Additionally, quantum Fisher or Bures metric, which can vary from probe to probe, should not be directly equated with shared space-time geometry.The characteristic cones of bosons and fermions must also be compatible with the same corrected causal structure.

*c g\_\* ≤ g_k ≤ C g\_\* for all k* (3.18)

Reality is not something that is restored separately for each local area and then simply put together.The recovery defined in the overlapping regions U and V must be compatible with the protected observations of the intersection.Without this quasi-local compatibility, each local observation, even if normal, is not stitched into a single global reality.The stability of geometric and quantum states also feeds back to each other and must be controlled together.

*R_U│\_(U∩V) ≃ R_V│\_(U∩V)* (3.19)

Restoration also follows a thermodynamic ledger.The cost of reading the syndrome, erasing information, and leaving feedback in geometry and matter should not be hidden.The conclusion of this chapter is the conditional theorem that a time-uniform WRRA interface is sufficient under these conditions.This is not a declaration that the derivation of the microscopic renderer or the actual implementation of the entire time in the universe has been completed.

*L_thermo = S_syndrome + C_erasure + B_backreaction* (3.20)

# Chapter 26: How is the Einstein branch selected?

Even if renderer path independence requires consistency of hypersurface deformation, the possible gravitational dynamics are not fixed to one without additional assumptions.To obtain the Einstein branch, a Hojman–Kuchař–Teitelboim type condition must be specified.

The key assumptions are a local metric canonical variable, standard spatial-diffeomorphism action, reversibility, absence of additional gravitational canonical fields, at most quadratic dependence on momentum, and no more than two spatial derivatives.

Under this condition, if the Hamiltonian constraint and momentum constraint implement hypersurface-deformation algebra as first-class, Einstein dynamics of general relativity is selected as the conditional low-energy branch.The cosmological constant term may be allowed and its specific value is a separate vacuum and observation matter.

*Recovered path equivalence + HKT assumptions ⇒ Einstein branch* (3.21)

Therefore, the correct direction of logic is not ‘path independence ⇒ Einstein equation.’‘Path equivalence ⇒ consistent deformation/gauge structure’, and ‘the structure + HKT type assumption ⇒ Einstein branch’.

*G_μν + Λg_μν = 8πG T_μν* (3.22)

Other branches are possible by allowing additional scalar, higher derivative, nonlocal variable or other canonical content.This does not mean that WRRA denies Einstein gravity, but rather makes the scope and selection assumptions of Einstein gravity clear.

The position of the 5-dimensional parent is organized in the same way.It is a chiral/spectral dilation candidate for the matter renderer, not the ultimate SOURCE.The low-energy gravity of our universe may follow the Einstein branch, but the underlying structure of the renderer may be broader.

# Opening words of Part 4

# Chapter 29 How to distinguish between re-expression, correction, and prediction

Moving the parameters of the standard model to the WRRA symbol is a re-expression.Rewriting the same numbers and the same relationships in a new SOURCE·RELATION·BOUNDARY language can provide structural insight, but does not create new numerical predictions.

Calibration is the process of determining the free parameters or boundary conditions of the renderer using limited observation data.The fact that the calibrated model reproduces the data well is a consistency check, not an independent prediction.The goodness of fit becomes meaningful only when the number of free parameters and the number of correction data are disclosed together.

Independent forecasting begins when observation quantities not used for calibration are calculated using a predetermined calculation procedure.If you change the parameters or filter structure after prediction, those values ​​become the calibration data for the next version, and a new held-out target must be specified again.

*Prediction = O_T(θ̂(C)) with T ∩ C = ∅* (4.3)

The three layers are not a relationship of superiority or inferiority, but a research order.Re-expression creates a correspondence table of different theoretical languages, correction narrows down the numerically possible domain, and independent prediction tests whether the domain actually matches nature.Only when the first two layers are accurate can a failure in the third layer be interpreted.

Predictive power is not simply measured by the number of correct numbers.The number of independent inputs used, tolerance, selection bias, and number of post-corrections must also be considered.WRRA's verification score is appropriate in the form of simultaneously recording held-out residual and complexity cost.

*χ²_hold = Σ\_(i∈T) \[(O_i^model−O_i^obs)/σ_i\]²* (4.4)

Therefore, throughout the book, the word ‘derived’ is used only when a formula and an input book are included.Translating observed values ​​into symbols is called re-expression, matching values ​​is called correction, and only matching sealed data is called prediction.

# Chapter 32: Experimental design to make independent predictions

Independent predictions cannot be declared after calculations are complete.First, divide the calibration set C, validation set V, and final test set T, and the value of T should not be used until the model and analysis rules are frozen.This principle is the minimum discipline shared by evaluation in machine learning and blind analysis in physics.

*D = C ⊔ V ⊔ T* (4.9)

Next, fix the prediction map.Write down in advance which SOURCE input, which observable is read through which renderer, and the renormalization scale, unit conversion, and tolerance.If there are many branches to choose from, the branches themselves are free parameters.

For WRRA's first blind test, a small ratio rather than a large target is appropriate.Candidates can be the mass ratio, coupling constant ratio, residue ratio, or pole spacing of a limited energy window in which the already calibrated absolute units are eliminated.

Predictions do not have to be point values.Interval prediction that separates theoretical and numerical errors is also justified.However, if the interval is widened or the observable is changed after observation, it is not a prior prediction.Failed predictions should also be left with the version DOI.

*O_pred = μ_model ± (σ_theory ⊕ σ_numeric)* (4.10)

If different runtimes reproduce the same low-energy observation log, common predictions and implementation-specific predictions can be separated.Results that are common to all runtimes are a test of the WRRA architecture, and results that appear only in one runtime are a test of its micro-implementation.

Once this procedure is established, the goals of the research will also change.Instead of fitting more constants at once, the next step is to compute a single sealed value with fewer inputs and attribute the cause of failure to the correct layer.

In the cosmology field, reclosure after opening, failure of scale invariance, excessive backreaction, and expansion history that are not compatible with observations become falsifiers.However, the version fitted by re-entering the observed values ​​is not a success of the original prediction, but a new calibration model.

# Chapter 34 Our universe is not special

This book does not attempt to prove the uniqueness of our universe.The universal principle of least computation and renderer conditions allow for multiple realizations, and our universe may be one example of these where observers and stable records are possible.

The non-privileged principle does not mean that all universes must have the same constants.What is common are the laws or acceptance conditions of creation and implementation, but boundary conditions, spectra, dimensions, combinations, and observable phenotypes can vary.

*Law_univ + θ_i → U_i* (4.13)

Therefore, it is more natural to draw a map of the realization space than to prove uniqueness.We find in which regions of parameter space locality, long-term restoration, stable matter and causal structures are possible together, and ask whether the coordinates of our universe lie within them.

*M_viable = {θ : locality ∧ stability ∧ recordability}* (4.14)

Observer selection effects are not a substitute for explanation.Saying that we are in a possible region because we exist does not calculate the size, structure, or transition probability of that region.A distinction must be made between selection condition and dynamic probability.

Other feasible universes do not require replications of our universe or parallel world narratives.It can be treated as a mathematically acceptable set of different renderer families, initial conditions and phenotypes.Whether or not they actually coexist is an additional ontology and lies beyond the minimal claims of this book.

The attitude that our universe is non-privileged does not weaken research.Rather, it prevents us from adding special rules specific to our universe and allows us to test whether the same laws create a consistent universe under different inputs.

# Chapter 37 Converting a continuous spectrum to a finite Jacobi matrix

The starting point is the positive generalized-Laguerre spectral density above the threshold s₀.If α is greater than −1, the density can be normalized, and in this study, s₀=1, Λ²=1 and α=1/2 were used.The weight of the continuum is positive and the overall integral is 1.

*ρ(s) = \[(s−s₀)^α e^(−(s−s₀)/Λ²)\]/\[Γ(α+1)Λ^(2α+2)\] Θ(s−s₀)* (5.3)

If you use the variable x=(s−s₀)/Λ², the measure will be in the form x^αe^(−x)/Γ(α+1).The ternary equation of the generalized Laguerre polynomial orthogonal to this measure determines the real symmetric Jacobi matrix.

The diagonal component of the N×N Jacobi matrix is ​​s₀+Λ²(2n+α+1), and the adjacent off-diagonal component is Λ²√((n+1)(n+1+α)).Since the matrix is ​​real symmetric, the eigenvalues ​​are real and unitary finite-time evolution is precisely defined.

*(J_N)\_(nn)=s₀+Λ²(2n+α+1), (J_N)\_(n,n+1)=Λ²√((n+1)(n+1+α))* (5.4)

If the first basis vector e₀ is selected as the visible port, the weight of each eigenvalue s_j becomes the square of the first component w_j according to the spectral theorem.Therefore, w_j is not negative and the sum is 1, and one rank-one collective port reads N hidden spectral nodes.

This structure does not declare the continuum as N independent physical particles.The goal is a Gaussian quadrature and finite state-space realization of the spectral measure.N is not the natural number of particles, but an approximate order.

The strongest point of Jacobi's construct is that positivity and self-contingency are not obtained by post hoc testing.Since it follows structurally from the measure and recurrence, it avoids the risk of arbitrarily fitting complex poles or negative spectral weights.

# Chapter 38 How one visible port reads the continuum

The Euclidean response of a continuous spectral target is read as a Stieltjes transform.When Q² is greater than 0, G_E(Q²) is the integral of spectral density divided by Q²+s.In the finite Jacobi model, the same quantity becomes the diagonal matrix element of the resolvent for e₀.

*G_E(Q²)=∫ ρ(s)/(Q²+s) ds* (5.5)

If we diagonalize the matrix, the finite response is the sum of w_j/(Q²+s_j).This expression shows that the quadrature nodes and weights are not a simple list of numbers, but come from a single self-involving Hamiltonian and visible vector.

The real-time amplitude is also owned by the same data.The continuous goal is A(t)=e^(−it)(1+it)^(−3/2), and the finite model is A_N(t)=Σ_jw_je^(−is_jt).Do not fit the Euclidean response and real-time amplitude with different parameters.

*A(t)=e^(−it)(1+it)^(−3/2), A_N(t)=Σ_j w_j e^(−is_jt)* (5.6)

One port reading all nodes means that information is compressed to rank one, not that the entire material space is one dimension.The state dimension of the hidden Jacobi chain is N, and only visible coupling is achieved through e₀.

This common-owner structure is in line with WRRA's minimal computation intuition.Rather than attaching separate sensors and separate transmitters to each component of the continuum, one collective boundary coordinate carries common spectral content.

However, this visible port is not yet a standard model particle with charge, spin, and flavor.It is a minimal interface to read the spectrum without loss, and the joint realization of physical quantum numbers remains a larger embedding problem.

# Chapter 39 How accurate is it in the Euclidean window?

The first test of the finite model was done in the Euclidean window of Q²∈\[0,10\].We increased the Jacobi order to N=2,4,8,16,32 and compared the maximum relative error of the exact Stieltjes transform and the finite resolvent.

*δ_N = max\_(Q²∈\[0,10\]) \|G\_(N,E)−G_E\|/\|G_E\|* (5.7)

The errors were, in order, 4.089×10^(−2), 4.746×10^(−3), 1.858×10^(−4), 1.623×10^(−6), and 1.763×10^(−9).At N=32, it goes down to a few parts per billion within the specified window.

The observed fit equation log δ_N≈−0.55N−3.31 summarizes the fast convergence in this finite sample.However, this is not a proven asymptotic theorem, but an empirical fit from N=2 to 32.The same slope is not guaranteed for different windows and different α.

*log δ_N ≈ −0.55N − 3.31 \[empirical\]* (5.8)

The numerical consistency of node and recurrence was also confirmed.The difference between eigenvalues ​​and quadrature nodes ranged from approximately 2.2×10^(−16) to 5.7×10^(−14) depending on the degree.This is consistent with the stability of the double-precision calculation used.

The reason why Euclidean convergence is fast is because the Stieltjes kernel is smooth outside the positive real axis and is advantageous for quadrature.This success should not be directly translated into the accuracy of Lorentzian long-term dynamics.

Therefore, the correct verdict in this chapter is fixed-window PASS.For a specified spectral density and Euclidean window, the finite Jacobi resolvent converges quickly, but completion for all scales and all complex regions is open.

# Chapter 40 Real-time tracking window and returning waves

The amplitude A(t) of the continuous spectrum decreases with phase mixing.However, since the finite Jacobi system is a finite sum of discrete eigenvalues, it cannot permanently reproduce complete irreversible decay.After a sufficiently long period of time, recurrence and revival appear.

In the calculation, the time until the relative error between continuous amplitude and finite amplitude exceeds 1% was defined as T_1%.For N=2,4,8,16,32, 0.43857, 0.96863, 1.69659, 2.67806, and 4.02408 were obtained, respectively.

*T\_(1%) = sup{T : relative error ≤ 0.01 on \[0,T\]}* (5.9)

The empirical fit for this data is T_1%≈0.84√N−0.72.Even if the order is quadrupled, the reliability time tends to roughly double.This equation is also not an asymptotic theorem, but a summary of the five-order benchmark used.

*T\_(1%) ≈ 0.84√N − 0.72 \[empirical\]* (5.10)

The long-term average survival plateau decreased to 0.700, 0.450, 0.3236, 0.2318, and 0.1650.As N increases, the average size of visible bounces decreases, but this does not result in permanent continuum damping at finite N.

This result makes the boundary between simulation and realization clear.Finite closed systems can mimic continuous response very accurately in a defined time window, but they do not possess infinite time irreversibilities and thermal arrows.

Therefore, adopting N as a physical cutoff requires a tracking window longer than the observation time, experimental invisibility of revival, or combination with the real environment.Otherwise, the model remains a finite-window simulator.

# Chapter 41: What does the recovery cycle restore?

The restoration conditions of WRRA v1.2 cannot be tested using spectrum simulation alone.For this purpose, the entire space is expanded by the tensor product of the Jacobi spectral factor, localization factor, and syndrome qubit.

The selected recovery generator is an amplitude-damping Lindblad operator.The syndrome excitation is lowered to the ground state at σ\_-, the lateral component of the density matrix is ​​attenuated at a rate of κ/2, and the population defect is attenuated at a rate of κ.

*L_rec(ρ)=κ\[σ₋ρσ₊−½{σ₊σ₋,ρ}\]* (5.11)

The corrective gap in the syndrome-transverse sector is Δ_corr,N=κ/2.This value does not depend on the Jacobi order N and recovery order.This is the exact-in-construction gap obtained because the recovery factor is separated from the spectral chain.However, since the unitary recurrence of the Jacobi sector remains, this does not mean that the entire Liouvillian is mixing.

*Δ\_(corr,N)=κ/2 \[syndrome-transverse sector\]* (5.12)

This restoration does not reverse the normal temporal evolution of the spectral amplitude.The intended unitary evolution of the Jacobi sector is maintained while eliminating errors in the syndrome factor.It follows the WRRA principle that restoration does not undo all changes.

However, factorization is a strong assumption.In an actual micro renderer, there may be interaction between spectral, localization and syndrome sectors, and the combination may reduce uniform gap or create new leakage.

Therefore, what passes here is the existence of a recovery channel and accurate gap calculation.Simulation error and recovery error have not yet been controlled for a long time with a single coupled inequality.

# Chapter 43 syndrome entropy and cost of restoration

Restoration does not become free just because it is logically possible.If the syndrome bit is excited with probability p, the Shannon entropy to be processed in one cycle is −p ln p−(1−p)ln(1−p).

*ΔS_rec=−p ln p−(1−p)ln(1−p)* (5.15)

At p=0.01 of the benchmark, ΔS_rec,N=0.0560015 nat/step.This value is independent of N and is finite.It is a bounded ledger obtained because the syndrome factor is fixed to one qubit.

Applying the Landauer lower bound, the minimum heat cost is Q_min=0.0560015 k_BT per step.This is not the actual device or space heat output, but the ideal lower limit required to irreversibly erase the selected syndrome record.

*Q_min=k_BT ΔS_rec=0.0560015 k_BT at p=0.01* (5.16)

To calculate the actual cost, cycle time, bath temperature, coupling efficiency, error production rate and backreaction are needed.Because these values ​​are not available, the cosmological heat budget has not yet been calculated.

Also, the fact that entropy is bounded is different from the fact that long-term total cost is bounded.If the cost per cycle is positive, the cumulative cost of an infinite cycle continues to increase without resource supply and release channels.

Therefore, what the benchmark has closed is a cycle of syndrome entropy ledger.How long the universe performs this restoration and what reservoir it uses are the next steps.

# Opening words of Part 6

The previous five parts started with the possible universe and progressed to minimum computation, WRRA's declarative architecture, observational verification, and the first finite benchmark.The last part organizes the results like a ledger and checks whether the fixed laws and open events, which are the starting propositions of the book, are compatible in the actual execution world.

After looking at finite open-history construction, in which different event histories are generated from the same complex SOURCE and the same law, we organize what survived, what was conditional, which paths were closed, and what remained for the next calculation.The purpose of the research, the method of human-AI collaboration, and coordinates for other researchers to start again are also left.

The end is not a declaration of completion, but a return to the question at the beginning.The role of this department is to look at the one realization that is our universe among the many possible universes, and to call the door that is still open by its correct name.

# Chapter 45 Research that started with one question

This study did not begin with a declaration to build a complete theory.We started with questions such as why do prime numbers appear to be independent cycles, are dimensions given from the beginning, and why does the universe not reveal all possible relationships at once?

The initial language was closer to intuition than refined physics terminology.The expressions filter, lens, projector, common transmitter, and small calculation window came first, followed by projector, spectral measure, Hamiltonian, recovery channel, and observable algebra.

This sequence was not a weakness.If a familiar theory had been named first, the question may have been prematurely closed within the existing classification.Intuitions were translated into several mathematical structures, and calculations and counterexamples were used to select the structures that survived.

The research didn't go in a straight line.We moved from prime numbers to dimensional filters, from dimensional filters to common carriers, from carriers to joint realizations of matter and geometry, and back to restorable renderers and finite computation benchmarks.

The midway change of direction did not invalidate the previous study.Each step leaves questions and failure conditions for the next step.Closed results became parts, broken models became boundaries, and open problems became next coordinates.

Therefore, the real protagonist of this book is not an equation, but the process by which a question survives by changing its form.The first conclusion of this book documents how curiosity about the universe turned into a computational research program.

*Question → structure → calculation → counterexample → record* (6.1)

# Chapter 46 Perspectives left behind by Minimal Computing Cosmology

The core of Minimal Computing Cosmology is not the claim that the universe is like a human computer.It is an operational intuition that asks the conditions of the size of source data needed to implement the universe, initial reading order, autonomous time evolution, selective realization, and joint recording in a single ledger.Common structures are reused rather than creating the same relationships every time, and observable differences are implemented only at necessary boundaries.

The distinction between prime and composite numbers was the first model of this intuition.Prime numbers show independence that cannot be reduced to a simple duplication of other cycles, and composite numbers show the combination of relationships that already exist.This does not mean that this arithmetic analogy is a law of physics, but it does reveal the problems of minimal creation and reuse.

Dimensional filters place a layer of choice between possibility and reality.Not all possible degrees of freedom are observed in the same way, but some relationships that pass through locality, stability, information preservation, and boundary recording appear as a reality.

This perspective made us question the ownership of values.The meaning of calculations becomes unclear if we do not distinguish whether mass and charge are primitively contained in the source, arise from transport and boundary, or are visible only in the units used by the observer.

Minimum computation is not a proven law that nature necessarily chooses the shortest program.Currently, it is a heuristic that compares multiple implementation candidates, and it is a design principle that asks whether necessary information and physical diversity are preserved while reducing duplication.

Nonetheless, this perspective tied the entire study together.Arithmetic filters, common carriers, WRRA and finite Jacobi runtime are all different expressions of the question of realizing observational reality from more possibilities to fewer common structures.

*possibility → filter → shared transport → observable reality* (6.2)

## Reality can be bigger than reality

WRRA v1.3 does not discard the existing SOURCE–RENDERER–PHENOTYPE structure but narrows its information claims.The entire state and relationship capacity included in SOURCE is classified as ‘Actual’, the state selectively implemented on the physical observation log is classified as ‘Reality’, and the single result newly recorded in that reality is classified as ‘Event’.Here, Actual is not the name of a separate entity that has been verified, but a definition that indicates a complete state within the model.

Ω_t ∈ S_actual ─R_t→ ρ_t ∈ S_reality ─I_a→ (a_t, ρ\_(t+1))

renderer R does not need to be injective.If the set of actual states compatible with the same reality state ρ is C(ρ)={Ω:R(Ω)=ρ}, the differences that do not appear in reality remain as a correlation structure with the redundancy of this compatibility fiber.Therefore, rendering deficit is not the amount of information lost, but the structure of distinction that has not yet been decomposed in reality.

If the global state evolves unitary in a larger Hilbert space and is reduced to ρ_t=Tr_hidden(Ω_t), the visible reality may be in a mixed state, but the overall information may remain in the hidden·environment correlation.What possesses hidden information and which algebras have access to it must be written down in the information/thermodynamics ledger.

Restoration conditions are also modified accordingly.The goal is not to reverse restore all Actual microstates, but to reproduce the intended lawful evolution E_lawful in the protected physical algebra A_protected within tolerances and time intervals.

D ∘ R ≃ E_lawful on A_protected

In this way, locality, common causal cone, conserved charge, and probability normalization are maintained, while actual flavor mixing, scattering, and collapse are not erased by ‘reconstruction error.’A lack of reality may not be a failure that must be automatically repaired, but rather a legitimate non-identity between the overall information and its physical realization.

**Status judgment:** The compatibility of unitary dilation and partial trace is EXACT, non-injective selective renderer and fiber deficit are CONSTRUCTIBLE, protected-algebra faithfulness is STRUCTURAL, and the argument that equates Actual with the ultimate ontology of nature is OPEN.

# Chapter 47: Fixed laws and open events

The question of whether the future of our universe should be stored as a list of events with laws from the beginning goes back to the starting point of WRRA.The new execution system specifies an open-history structure in which laws, constants, symmetries, and preservation ledgers are fixed, while individual event histories are not entered in advance.

Complex SOURCE states preserve probability and topology.The renderer calculates the conditional Born probability through a quantum instrument at each step, and the actually recorded event updates the conditional state to calculate the next probability.The past is closed as a cumulative record, but the future remains as a conditional distribution.

*p(a\|ρ)=Tr\[I_a(ρ)\], ρ_a=I_a(ρ)/p(a\|ρ)* (6.3)

In the finite calculation, one-dimensional Dirac wave packet, three-state Jacobi carrier, and spatially local lower-order Hermitian coupling were combined and four unsharp-position instruments were applied.Among 600 runs starting from the same initial complex state and the same laws, 590 different four-event histories were generated.

This diversity was not a deviation from the law.The Hamiltonian hermititicity error was 0 in configuration, the segment unitarity error was 3.63×10⁻¹⁴, the instrument completeness error was 4.44×10⁻¹⁶, and the maximum norm error after the event was 1.55×10⁻¹⁵.Therefore, it is established that open events and closed conservation ledgers are compatible in one finite execution system.

*Σ_a I_a†(Q)=Q* (6.4)

When the first event is split to the left and right of the median, the final conditional mean position is divided into −3.4283 and −1.6986, respectively, showing that the event record is not a decorative table of random numbers but actually acts on the probabilities and states behind it.Even in independent resampling, which increased the sample number from 200 to 12,800, the L¹ error of the first event distribution decreased from 0.1940 to 0.0267.

But this does not prove fundamental collapse or ontological indeterminacy.Collapse, branching, hidden choices and larger unitary dilations can reproduce the same operational instrument.Exactly closed are the operational forms of fixed laws and sequential conditional events, while the 3+1-dimensional gauge-covariant standard model runtime and observational predictions are open.

## An open future requires separate conditions

The mere fact that reality reveals only part of the Actual does not lead to the conclusion that the future is ontologically open.The same non-injective renderer may be followed by a deterministic hidden completion with all outcomes already determined.Rendering deficit and event openness are different arguments.

WRRA v1.3 places the No Predetermined Event Register condition to separate this.SOURCE may contain complex amplitudes, correlations, conservation laws, boundary data, and allowable transition logarithms, but it should not contain classical registers from which future event sequences (a₁,a₂,…,a∞) can be read directly.This condition is not a derived theorem, but a POSTULATE that requires verification.

The quantum instrument provides normalized probabilities and conditional updates of the allowed outcomes, adding only one of them to a single history.This interface does not require many worlds, but it also does not mathematically disprove many worlds.What WRRA adopts is a single history boundary that says, ‘There may be multiple possibilities, but there is only one physical event record.’

Therefore, the current view of the universe is summarized as law-closed, render-open, and event-open.Laws are closed, reality is a selective realization of the Actual, and the final list of events is not complete before execution.However, this last event-open nature of micro selection mechanism and independent observation prediction is still OPEN.

## Two passes: the boundary before quantum probability comes into play

This hypothesis divides selective realization into two stages.The first pass reads the SOURCE and the locking laws to create a support set Γ_lawful of events that fit causal reachability, conservation of energy, momentum, and charge, gauge constraints, allowed interactions, and the established stability record.This step does not select which events will occur, but only limits what can be physical candidates.

Γ_allowed=C_coarse(SOURCE,laws,constants) (6.3a)

The second pass assigns complex amplitude, action phase, interference, and Born weighting only to the paths in Γ_lawful.Probability does not create new physical possibilities, but establishes the relative weight of possibilities already allowed by the law.Observation is not human consciousness, but the formation of a stable physical record that distinguishes between alternatives, such as detection, absorption, collision, and environmental traces.

A\[γ\]=a\[γ\]exp(iS\[γ\]/ℏ), K=Σ\_(γ∈Γ_lawful)A\[γ\] (6.3b)

This nomological support set principle is a key candidate for distinguishing between WRRA and the unrestricted quantum probability perspective.Microscopic tunneling allowed by the Hamiltonian has a Born probability within the support set.On the other hand, the distant reappearance of a macroscopic object with no continuous transport, interaction, or compatible record is not an event with a very small probability, but lies outside the support set of visible reality.

supp P_WRRA⊆Γ_lawful, P_WRRA(a)=0 for a∉Γ_lawful (6.3c)

Writing this as an ad hoc formula multiplies the standard quantum probability P_QM by the universal feasibility factor χ_real.However, χ_real is not a prohibit switch that can be arbitrarily selected in each experiment.A completed WRRA must derive a common rule from the state, environment, and transportation history, and apply the same rule to the microscopic and macroscopic systems.

P_WRRA(a)=χ_real(a)P_QM(a)/Σ_bχ_real(b)P_QM(b), 0≤χ_real≤1 (6.3d)

The first numerical boundary candidate combines the mass M, the discrete displacement Δx and the redundancy R_rec of the already formed environmental record.If Ω_WRRA=R_recMcΔx/h, if a 1 g object moves 1 m without any process, it is about 4.5×10³⁸ even before multiplying by R_rec.The movement of 1 μm of a 1 μg object is approximately 4.5×10²⁶.This value is a first estimate to quantify why macroscopic discontinuous reappearances are not observed, but is not yet a definitive universal threshold.

Ω_WRRA=R_rec(Mc²/E\*)(Δx/ℓ\*)=R_recMcΔx/h, χ_est=e^(−Ω_WRRA) (6.3e)

Here, E\*≈1.54777 TeV, τ\*=2.672021665×10⁻²⁷ s, ℓ\*=8.010519428×10⁻¹⁹ m is a finite inventory microgrid that commonly divides the phase cycles of the current SM15 mass ledger.It is not a fixed value for the Planck length or the fundamental pixel of the universe, and may be further refined or exact commensurability may be lost as new masses, combinations, and widths are introduced.

This hypothesis does not replace the successful calculations of quantum mechanics.If quantum mechanics is the layer that calculates amplitude and probability, WRRA goes one step further by asking in what physical event space that probability can become a visible result.The derivation of the Born rule, the microdynamics of χ_real, the objective definition of the thresholds Ω_c and R_rec, and the operations that actually select one outcome are still OPEN.

Status judgment: The two-stage execution structure is WORKING HYPOTHESIS, the exclusion of causality, conservation, and gauge violations is compatible with existing laws, the lawful support set principle and the exact 0 of processless macro re-emergence are POSTULATE, Ω_WRRA is PROVISIONAL ESTIMATOR, and the fundamental derivation of Born weighting and single-result selector is OPEN.

## Reading the four theories at the same axiom level

The final axiom declaration juxtaposes classical mechanics, relativity, quantum mechanics, and WRRA, not as different ranks of success and failure, but as different questions posed to reality.Classical mechanics asks about motion, relativity theory asks about space-time and causal structure, quantum mechanics asks about the amplitude and probability of possibilities, and WRRA asks about the structure by which allowed possibilities are realized as a stable reality.

|**Frame** |**Fundamental Question** |**Core Raw Quantity** |**Answering Floor** |
|---------------------------|----------------------------------|-----------------------------|----------------------------------|
|classical mechanics |How do objects move |Position·Momentum·Force |Trajectory of given state and force |
|theory of relativity |In what space and time does it move |Event/Measurement/Causal Structure |Joint space-time of matter and geometry |
|quantum mechanics |How microscopic possibilities evolve |Status·Amplitude·Operator |Evolution of amplitude and Born probability |
|WRRA |How possibility becomes reality |SOURCE · Support set · Record · Phenotype |Nomological Allowance·Quantum Refinement·Event Rendering |

If you write this in one line, it is ‘movement → space and time → possibility → realization’.The notation Quantum Mechanics ⊂ WRRA does not mean that all quantum mathematics is a subset of WRRA in set theory.It is an architectural declaration that accepts the verified state, Hamiltonian, interference, and Born calculations of quantum mechanics as the Quantum Refinement layer, and places a lawful support set in front of it, and stability records and event outputs behind it.

## Eight Axioms of WRRA

① SOURCE: Visible reality is a phenotype translated from a larger information state.② Lawful support set: Probability does not create possibilities, but gives weight only to candidates allowed by the history and record of causality, conservation, charge, expression, and interaction.③ Quantum refinement: Superposition, interference, entanglement, tunneling, and Born weighting operate normally within the permitted history.

④ Open event: Although the law is fixed, the outcome of each individual event is not written in advance.⑤ Record-based realization: Observation is not human consciousness, but the formation of a stable record that physically distinguishes alternatives.⑥ Macroscopic continuity: Macrohistory, which is stable with many records, is not replaced by another history without a lawful process.

⑦ Action-record boundary: The boundary between micro possibility and macro fact depends not on mass but on cumulative action and environmental record, and the current candidate is Ω_WRRA=R_recMcΔx/h.⑧ Information preservation and phenotype deficiency: Information that does not appear in reality does not necessarily disappear and may remain in hidden·environment correlation.

The fair comparison principle is also frozen.While acknowledging the axioms of existing theories as the starting point for each theory, it does not require that only the axioms of WRRA be derived infinitely further down.Instead, the verification obligation focuses on the new claims made by WRRA—the physical creation of nomological support sets, the precise exclusion of processless macro-reappearances, the universality of Ω_WRRA, and the objective measurement of record redundancy and single-event output.

Status judgment: The question classification of the four theories and the adoption of the internal execution layer of quantum mechanics are WRRA BASE ARCHITECTURE, the eight items are AXIOMATIC DECLARATION, Ω_WRRA is QUANTITATIVE CANDIDATE, and the universal threshold, record redundancy measurement, and independent prediction beyond quantum mechanics are NEXT/OPEN.This declaration is not an end point that exaggerates the completion of the research, but is the final high-level document that unwaveringly fixes what future calculations should implement and what should be tested.

Related open research: Four Axiomatic Frameworks for Describing Reality · Version 1.0 · DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22142880

## Space in single result

The subsequent boundary calculation made it clear that it was not that a single result was not produced due to a lack of decoherence, but that decoherence and outcome selection were different operations.By combining the state and environment before measurement with a time-independent self-involved Hamiltonian, the following unitary record can be formed.

U(\|ψ⟩⊗\|E₀⟩) = Σ_a c_a \|a⟩⊗\|E_a⟩, ⟨E_a\|E_b⟩≈δ_ab

This configuration can simultaneously satisfy energy conservation, preservation of born branch weight \|c_a\|², and orthogonalization of environmental records.If you trace out the environment, the interference term in the reduced state disappears and looks like a classical mixture, but the overall state Σ_a c_a\|a,E_a⟩ still includes all branches.

With only linear unitary dynamics, it is impossible to create a deterministic selector that maps each input superposition to one of its branches while maintaining linearity, norm preservation, and born weight.Adding either one as an actual event requires at least one more of the following: conditional update of the instrument, stochastic selection, nonlinear dynamics, or separate ontological rules.

This result closes the “decoherence is collapse” shortcut, but it does not disprove all possible single-result theories.The exact range of NO-GO is linear unitary dynamics alone, and the microscopic selector itself is OPEN.The path to insert consciousness or will into a temporary collapse port is also inactive in the current interface because there is no physical coupling and no verifiable law.

**Status judgment:** The simultaneous configuration of unitary record formation, energy and branch-weight preservation, and environmental orthogonalization is CONSTRUCTED, the single result selection of decoherence alone is EXACT NO-GO under linear unitarity, and the actual outcome selector is OPEN.

The data size of the universe is not just a single number.Exact classical specification, thermodynamic entropy, gravitational capacity and generative description length must be separated.The area-based capacity of an observable radius of 46.5 billion light years is a conditional benchmark of approximately 3.36×10¹²³ bit, and the current published entropy estimate is approximately 10¹⁰⁴–10¹⁰⁵ bit.Neither is the minimum size of the WRRA SOURCE program.

*I_area ≈ πR²/(ℓ_P² ln 2) ≈ 3.36×10¹²³ bits* (6.5)

In open-history WRRA's information ledger, there is no need to initially store future event tables.A short law can create a long history, and the realized record accumulates as −log₂ p each time an event occurs.The key to this result is that reality is not a pre-recorded movie, but a history in which events are written within fixed rules.

*I_history(T)=Σ_t −log₂ p(a_t\|h\_(t−1))* (6.6)

## Possibility is plural, but actuality is singular.

There is no need to equate an open future with a multi-world divergence.WRRA's single-history hypothesis may allow for multiple subsequent events at a single point in time, but there is exactly one outcome that makes it into the actual physical record.The unselected outcome remains a counterfactual possibility that defines the next probability, and is not assumed to continue to exist as an independent physical universe.

Ψₙ → {a₁,…,aₖ}, Aₙ∈{a₁,…,aₖ}, hₙ=(hₙ₋₁,Aₙ) (6.7)

Therefore, this model differs from classical determinism, where the initial value already includes the entire ending, and from the Everett-style many-worlds interpretation, in which all outcomes are realized in each world.The future is open as a conditional possibility before realization, and after an event is recorded, it is closed as an irreversible past.

|**Model** |**Future before realization** |**History realized** |
|----------------|-----------------------------|---------------------|
|classical determinism |Initial values ​​and rules are virtually fixed |One from scratch |
|collapsing probability theory |Multiple outcomes are probabilistically possible |One per measurement |
|Everett Multi-World |All results are branchable |All branches are real |
|WRRA single history |conditional possibility plural |Accumulate only one every moment |

# Chapter 48 What WRRA actually explains

What WRRA did most clearly was to separate the implementation layer between source and observed reality.SOURCE refers to possible degrees of freedom, RENDERER refers to relationships, boundaries, transmission, and restoration conditions, and PHENOTYPE refers to jointly observed physical reality.

This separation revealed the problem of calculating mass, charge, spin, and position separately and then calling it a single particle.A physical object is established when multiple properties are jointly attributed under the same local observation algebra, translation history, and spectral ownership.

WRRA does not require that the raw output of different rendering paths be literally the same.Differences in gauge and expression are allowed, but recovery path equivalence is required, in which the same physical content is obtained in the protected observation algebra after recovery.

We also separated one-time restoration from long-term stability.Mechanisms such as summable disturbance, noiseless observable algebra or attracting code manifold, uniform ellipticity, and quasi-local gluing are required to maintain the same reality over time.

These conditions are not a completed microtheory but a declarative interface.It presents the minimum compatibility conditions that any candidate renderer must pass, and prevents the entire top question from automatically disappearing even if a specific 5-dimensional parent or Bessel carrier fails.

Therefore, WRRA's current achievements do not lie in explaining all the numbers in the universe.What needs to be explained before it can be said to have ‘embodied reality’ lies in making the sentence more accurate than before.

# Chapter 49 What failed models teach us

Some of the important results of research are not successful formulas but shortcuts that should no longer be used.It cannot be said that the mass is determined just because there is a full-rank matrix, and the physical combination is not completed just by the existence of the gauge port.

The democratic rank-one opening revealed only one direction and left the remaining fourteen directions dark.This failure demonstrated that release of all 15 channels required more structured coupling than simple symmetry.

Simple log-det energy confuses the ground contribution with the physical transition cost.The reason ground subtraction and a single total energy ledger were introduced was because the previous calculations were corrected without hiding the fact that they were wrong.

The path to exact echo of a continuous spectrum with finite causal pulses and bounded control is also closed.The recurrence of a finite Jacobi system can increase the computational window, but it does not exactly possess the permanent irreversibility of an infinite continuum.

The same lesson emerged from restoration.Restoration can erase flavor mixing, decay, and even intended time evolution.What needs to be protected is not a static state, but the logic and observable record of permitted change.

FAIL-CLOSED is not a sign to throw failure out of the study.It is a form of knowledge that preserves why a certain conclusion of a certain assumption was closed so that the next person does not pave the same path with the correct answer again.

## Closure of Zeta SOURCE privileges

The complex vector normalized from 6369 critical-line zeta samples has 2×6369−2=12,736 real degrees of freedom after removing the global phase and norm.On the other hand, the normalized power spectrum leaves only 6369−1=6,368 degrees of freedom.Since exactly 6,368 relative phase degrees of freedom are lost in the power-only summary, the shape of the power alone cannot uniquely trace back to the original SOURCE.

Compared to weak random controls, the bicoherence of zeta seeds may seem unusual.However, strong phase controls such as zero-phase and fitted-linear-phase show 1.0 and 0.998165, respectively, which are higher than the zeta sample's 0.51755.Therefore, this statistic detects a coherent phase structure but does not authenticate the intrinsic arithmetic origin of zeta.

A fixed 15-channel compiler or modulo-15 arrangement provides reproducible forwarding rules without having to match the SOURCE, but does not revive zeta privileges.Blind rank extraction also failed to produce a stable 15 as the rank of the largest gap in window 127·255·511 fluctuated to 1·4·1.Even if rank 15 is stable, U(15) rotation preserves rank and singular value, so the gauge group, chirality, representation block, and intertwiner of the standard model cannot be named by that number alone.

Therefore, the representational owner of the 15 components should not be the rank of SOURCE, but the fixed rule, renderer, or additional invariant algebra.SOURCE may or may not be Zeta.The currently freezeable sentence is ‘Fixed Laws + Renderer + Unspecified Admissible Source + Open Events’, and the zeta sample remains as a stress-test seed that is publicable and reproducible, not an ontological initial state.

Verification boundaries: scalar loop integration, required mass ratios and coefficients of freedom were consistent with independent recalculations during the editing process of this book.Ward residual, bicoherence, and rank values ​​for each window are set as REPORTED / PENDING REPRODUCTION because the runtime package mentioned with the manuscript is not included in this attachment.Before asking again about the identity of the SOURCE, the renderer's forward observable map and likelihood must first be fixed.

*FAIL-CLOSED = rejected route + preserved reason* (6.8)

## How to restore judgment criteria without rewriting the paper

The WRRA Interpretive Companion is not a document intended to overwrite, withdraw, or replace an existing paper with a revised version.It is an independent interpretation guide that reorders the current verdict based on the questions each paper actually asked, the inputs it specified, the rules it used, and the renderer and output it implemented.Therefore, the exact identity, numerical results and local NO-GO of the original calculation are preserved.

This guide does not condense performance into a single standard.Structural design, feasibility, compatibility with existing physics, identification of sources, selective realization, boundary closure and new predictions are divided into different layers.Asking a model that declares the Planck scale or standard model constant obtained from observation as legitimate input to ‘re-derive the value without input’ is not a judgment of failure of the model itself.

Attack Contract = {Research Question, Allowed Inputs, Success Outputs, Failure Conditions, Optional Objectives, Freeze Assertion}

Uniqueness, input-free derivation, and required prediction of unpublished observations are not universal passing conditions for all WRRA papers, but are additional goals evaluated only when specified in the attack contract.NO-GO is also closed only within the scope of the home.It is recalculated only when internal math, physics, or numerical defects are found, and implementation boundaries or open branches are left along with the history of the original paper.

Actual + fixed laws + lawful selective renderer → shared Reality + one recorded open history

According to this restoration rule, WRRA does not start over from scratch.The results of the already secured common carrier, rank opening, spectrum settlement, selective realization, and electronic, water, and standard model boundaries are maintained at their respective claim levels, and only the excessively imposed external demands return to the open problem.[Refer to Appendix D ‘Ranks and Rank Opening’]

# Chapter 50 How humans and AI studied together

The roles of humans and AI in this study were not the same.The starting point, analogy, direction, and determination of what we are really curious about came from humans.AI has expanded that intuition into several mathematical languages ​​and increased the speed of calculation and documentation.

The strength of AI lies in quickly unfolding many candidates, connecting expressions from different fields, and maintaining long calculation ledgers.However, that speed also increases the risk of making plausible leaps, incorrect numbers, and using already entered values ​​as if they were predictions.

Therefore, joint research required a verification structure rather than role division.Honing pushed intuition as far as possible, counter-examination looked for counterexamples and proprietary errors, and independent verification guided the calculations again.Master Ledger separated these results into EXACT, CONDITIONAL, WORKING, OPEN and FAIL-CLOSED.

The answer that AI came up with at once was not a research result.It was only possible to freeze the same proposition when it passed through formulas, numerical calculations, units, limits, and preceding structures, and did not break the preceding results even after modification.

In this method, AI does not replace the author.Rather, it is closer to an intellectual device that illuminates questions further, exposes errors, and walks together in an exploration space that is difficult for a person to handle alone.

The possibility of human-AI joint research does not lie in AI coming up with a genius answer.The goal is to transform old human questions into a calculable form and to leave traceable records of even failures.

*Human question + AI expansion + independent verification* (6.9)

# Chapter 51 Map for the next researcher to continue

Anyone who wants to continue this research does not need to read all papers with equal weight from the beginning.It is better to first read WRRA's SOURCE-RENDERER-PHENOTYPE definition and claim ledger, and then check the path equivalence, recoverability and time-uniform conditions.

To test a micro-implementation, you can start with a finite Jacobi benchmark.After reproducing the spectral density, recurrence, visible port, and calculation window, change the order N, α, threshold, and Euclidean window to see how convergence and recurrence change.

# Chapter 52: Leaving behind humanity’s knowledge warehouse

## Part 8 Claim Level Preservation Rules

EXACT — A result in which the identity, conservation, and restart identity are exactly closed within the declared finite model and assumptions.

STRUCTURAL — Not a numerical prediction, but the result of consistently constructing variables, ownership, ideas, and dependencies.

CONDITIONAL — A result that is established only when the specified LAW, boundary, matching, and data qualifications are met.

FROZEN PREDICTION — A forward prediction in which the input, model, evaluation quantity, and failure conditions are fixed before viewing the results.

PREREGISTERED TEST — A test in which subjects, controls, and statistical judgments are registered before opening external data or experiments.

OPEN — An assertion that has not yet been closed to physicalization, independent external verification, continuous limits, or empirical identification.

NEGATIVE·FAIL-CLOSED — Unsupported results from freeze testing are not deleted but preserved as evidence to limit their scope.

# Chapter 71 Superfluids and boundary transitions in WRRA 2.0

**Conclusion of this chapter: The rollin film of helium-4 is not a gravity-defying or shielding phenomenon.Surface wetting and van der Waals interactions create a thin film connecting the inside, edge, and outside of the container, and the superfluid phase stiffness transports mass along the film towards the low generalized chemical potential.WRRA 2.0 classifies this as a downstream population phenotype where BOUNDARY changes function to conditional CARRY PATH.**

## 61.1 Distinction between mercury and helium

The target of DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22289094 is not mercury but liquid helium II below the lambda point Tλ≈2.17 K.Mercury is a solid superconductor that shows loss of electrical resistance and Meissner response below about 4.2 K, and the Rollin film that goes over the container wall is a mass transport phenomenon of neutral helium atoms.The two systems share only the mathematical similarity of the topological stiffness of the complex order variable, but the transport quantity, gauge combination, and boundary conditions are different.In neither case is there any gravity shielding.

## 61.2 Phase relationship and two fluids

The low-energy phase relationship can be expressed as an order variable Ψ.In strongly interacting liquid helium-4, the condensation density n₀ and the superfluid mass density ρs are not the same, so they must be separated.At a finite temperature, a superfluid component and a normal component carrying thermal excitation, viscosity, and entropy exist together.

Ψ(r,t)=√n₀(r,t) exp\[iϕ(r,t)\]

vₛ=(ℏ/m₄)∇ϕ, jₛ=ρₛvₛ

ρ=ρₛ+ρₙ, j=ρₛvₛ+ρₙvₙ

∮ vₛ·dl = n h/m₄, n = 0, ±1, ±2, …

The circulation quantization and winding conservation of the last equation correspond to the continuous current in a closed loop.On the other hand, the outflow of the Rollin film is an open system potential flow connecting the reservoir and the external sink, and is possible without non-obvious winding.The two phenomena are not grouped into the same “permanent flow.”

## 61.3 Generalized chemical potential including gravity

Superfluid motion is described by the generalized chemical potential, which combines the internal chemical potential, gravity, and the potentials of the walls and surfaces.The gravitational term m₄gz does not disappear.On the inside wall, surface forces maintain the membrane, and on the outside, gravity aids downward drainage.

μ̃(r)=μint(r)+m₄gz+Vwall(r)+VvdW\[d(r)\]

ℏ∂tϕ=−μ̃

m₄\[∂tvₛ+∇(vₛ²/2)\]=−∇μ̃

If the film thickness at height h is d(h) and the departure from the liquid-vapor coexistence point per unit mass is Δμm, the disjoining pressure Π(d)=−dΦs/dd created by the surface free energy Φs and the equilibrium of gravity are as follows.

Π\[d(h)\]=ρ\[gh−Δμm\]

Π(d)=Aeff/(6πd³) ⇒ d(h)=\[Aeff/(6πρgh)\]¹ᐟ³

The last equation is a non-delayed van der Waals approximation near the saturation vapor pressure.The actual coefficient and application range depend on the substrate, temperature, roughness, retardation, adsorption layer, and critical fluctuation.The key point is that although surface forces can be stronger than gravity over short distances, gravity still limits film thickness with height.

## 61.4 BOUNDARY TO CARRY PATH

In the WRRA response, SOURCE is the number of preserved helium atoms and the initial thermodynamic state, RELATION is the condensation phase and density, BOUNDARY is the solid-helium interface and free surface, CARRY is the mass current of the film, TAKEOVER is the normal component/vortex/heat/evaporation, and RESET is the winding sector change or loss of superfluidity.For the boundary to open into a transport path, wetting W, superfluidity S of finite ρs, membrane connectivity C, driving force D, and strong relaxation inhibition R are simultaneously required.

∂tρ+∇·j=0

C\*=argmin_C \[Iindependent(C)+Mstored(C)+Uupdate(C)+Rrepair(C)\]

subject to Closure(C)=1, Fidelity(C)≥F\*, Robustness(C)≥R\*

Here, Iindependent is the irreducible independent input, Mstored is the storage amount, Uupdate is the state update cost, and Rrepair is the error correction/restoration cost.This objective function is not yet a law that has been proven to actually be minimized by nature, but rather an operational research hypothesis for comparing the execution structures of different areas.If the same cost terms and closure conditions are independently recovered and new values ​​are predicted in space, life, cognitive, social, and artificial systems, the universality of minimal computation becomes stronger.If not retrieved, it is limited to domain-specific metaphors.

**Judgment The proposition that “minimum calculus may be the universal logic of the universe” has been strengthened in structural possibility by applying it to life, but it is still UNIVERSAL-GRAMMAR HYPOTHESIS / OPEN.Success in reconstructing biological data is the first cross-domain compatibility test and is not proof of universality.**

**Chapter Conclusion WRRA does not equate the universe with life.We showed that two different systems can share the same computational grammar of rules, states, expressions, observations, sources, and fail-closed audits.Structural transplantation and reconstruction of public data have been successful, but biological independent prediction is still at the beginning.**

# Chapter 74 WRRA Model of Life II: Gene Expression

Conclusion of this chapter: DNA can specify proteins, but it cannot synthesize them on its own.The genetic sequence is SOURCE, the genetic code and reaction rules are LAW, the molecular concentration is STATE, the polymerase, ribosome, tRNA, aaRS, and energy regeneration systems are physical RENDERER, and the yield, speed, and error of the full-length peptide are OBSERVABLE.The key to WRRA is not to confuse information and executors.

## 64.1 Ownership of sequences and executors

DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22290829 addresses minimal genetic execution between the overall cellular structure of Life Model I and the generation calculations of Life Model III.If the execution output is Y=Φ(D,E,X₀,t), D is the DNA specification, E is the executor inventory, and X₀ is the chemical initial state.In normal DNA-directed protein synthesis, without E, there is no catalytic pathway and therefore no protein output.

Y=Φ(D,E,X₀,t), E=empty ⇒ Yprotein(t)=0

Even if DNA encodes polymerase, translation factors, and ribosomal proteins, the first decoder and generator must already exist.This no-bootstrap result is not a theorem that autonomous life is impossible, but a proprietary audit that suggests that DNA alone hides material initial conditions.

## 64.2 Accurate coding floor and shortest cassette that cannot be defined

Since a peptide of n residues including the starting amino acid requires n sense codons and one stop codon, the lower limit of the pure coding length under a typical triplet code is accurate.

Lcoding,min(n)=3n+3 nucleotides

However, the physical expression cassette requires a promoter·leader·ribosome-binding site·terminator and ion·recognition rules.In Lcassette=3n+3+R, R depends on the executor and assay, so the ‘universal shortest expression DNA’ without fixing the executor is not 0 but null/OPEN.

## 64.3 Translation processivity limits based on length

If the processivity loss section δ=1.3×10⁻³∼13.2×10⁻³ per codon of the 2021 PURE-system is entered into the independent dropout model, the probability of full-length creation decreases exponentially depending on the length.

Pfull(n\|δ)=(1-δ)ⁿ, n₅₀=ln(0.5)/ln(1-δ)

The 50% full-length scale for this calculation is 532.8 residues at the low loss boundary and 52.2 residues at the high loss boundary.This is not a universal translation law, but a model conditional ceiling that transforms the specified experience interval.In the same executor, ORF length can be changed and δ can be tested with holdout without readjustment.

## 64.4 Current boundaries between seeded execution and self-renewal

In 2001, the PURE system experimentally closed DNA-to-protein implementation on a seeded renderer supplied with purified ribosomes, tRNA, aaRS, translation factors, and energy chemistry.Cell-free expression within the vesicle adds boundaries, but does not demonstrate autonomy if executors and metabolism are brought in from outside.

In a 2026 report, 36 non-ribosomal proteins of PURE were synthesized into PURE to reconstruct a functional second-generation PURE.However, the single reaction regeneration efficiency is 8%, which is less than the minimum book value of 1 for continuous replacement.

Rnonribosomal=0.08\<1 ⇒ sustained replacement FAIL

## 64.5 Judgment and Frozen Prediction

The exact coding floor is EXACT UNDER ORDINARY TRIPLET CODING, DNA-only protein production is EXCLUDED IN ORDINARY CHEMISTRY, seeded purified expression and functional reconstruction of 36 non-ribosomal proteins are EXPERIMENTAL PASS, length-processivity is DERIVED/MODEL-CONDITIONAL, 8% continuous self-regeneration is FAIL, and seed-free ribosome·decoder generation and membrane·generation closure are OPEN.

Pre-registration candidates are ① yield, starting point, and full-length fraction according to executor-swap of the same DNA, ② log-linear passage rate of uncorrected length panel, ③ 2nd generation activity/parent generation activity of essential executor classification, ④ co-inheritance and descendant persistence according to the presence or absence of regeneration membrane.If δ or threshold value is adjusted with the same data, it is left as CALIBRATION.

Chapter Conclusion Genes are programs, not executors themselves.WRRA Life Model II has closed seeded execution of DNA-to-protein, but autonomous minimal life that reconstructs decoder, energy, membrane, division, and lineage together is still open.

# Chapter 75 WRRA Life Model III: Lineage Closure

Conclusion of this chapter: The problem of one cell passing on its viability to two daughter cells and descendants is closed with a finite number of molecules, stochastic distribution, boundary geometry, and branching processes.Mathematical genealogy layers are feasible, but independent biology predictions are OPEN because there is no molecular-level twin daughter cell holdout.

## 65.1 From mother cell to living lineage

In DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22291113, SOURCE is the genome and inherited molecular inventory, LAW is reaction, transport, replication, and distribution rules, STATE is the number and spatial arrangement of molecules by time, RENDERER is membrane geometry, division plane, location, and probability events, and OBSERVABLE is growth, division, daughter cell composition, and lineage survival.The copy number of the JCVI-syn3A 6-molecule pool used was a mixed source and measurement method, so it is not a simultaneous measurement of natural blast cells, but an explicit sensitivity scenario.

## 65.2 Exact distribution law of single fission

If the number of molecules in the mother cell is N, and each molecule is divided into independent and unbiased groups, then the number of first daughter cells, X, follows a finite binomial distribution.

X~Binomial(N,1/2), E\[X\]=N/2, Var(X)=N/4, CV(X)=1/√N

When the minimum daughter cell of each pool i is mᵢ=ceil(ϕNᵢ/2), the probability that both daughter cells pass through is the sum of binomial probabilities from x=mᵢ to Nᵢ-mᵢ.Therefore, even if the volume is exactly symmetrical, the coefficient noise of the low-molecular-water essential pool is large.

## 65.3 Sister-dependent offspring distribution and reproduction number

If the probability of one daughter cell passing all thresholds is q, and the probability of both daughter cells passing is P₂, the exact distribution of the number of descendants 0·1·2, not treating sisters as independent, is as follows.

P₁=2(q-P₂), P₀=1-2q+P₂, Rlife=P₁+2P₂=2q

If f(s)=P₀+P₁s+P₂s² is the production function of the branching process, the ultimate extinction probability ζ is the minimum solution of f(ζ)=ζ under the iid assumption that the same mother cell distribution is restored in each generation.If Rlife≤1, ζ=1, if Rlife\>1, ζ=P₀/P₂, and the finite generation is calculated as ζg=f(ζg₋₁), ζ₀=0.

## 65.4 Integer critical cliff owned by HupA

In the declared 6-pool scenario, when ϕ=6/7, the daughter cell requirement for 28-copy HupA is 12 and Rlife=1.133.When ϕ slightly exceeds 6/7, the demand jumps to 13, goes down to q and Rlife = 0.978, and the lineage changes to subcritical.This discontinuous transition is owned by HupA within the corresponding input panel.

At ϕ=0.80, Rlife=1.400, the ultimate lineage survival rate is 86.8%, and at ϕ=0.8572, Rlife=0.978, the 100-generation survival rate is 1.18%, and the ultimate survival rate is 0.These are not measured biological constants, but CONDITIONAL SCENARIO showing critical sensitivity and integer copy-number cliffs.

## 65.5 active equalization and copy-number reliability front

In the audit family in which each pool follows a possible equal distribution with probability a and an unbiased binomial distribution with 1-a, the minimum equalization ratio to restore Rlife=1 with ϕ=0.90 is 26.85%.At ϕ=1.00, 76.24% is required.This is not a specific molecular mechanism, but a calculation of the required renderer control strength.

If the daughter cell minimum of HupA is fixed at 12 copies, 35, 39, and 45 copies of the mother cell provide 95%, 99%, and 99.9% simultaneous passage of the two daughter cells, respectively.The reserve amount compared to 28-copy input is +7·+11·+17.Since there is no translation cost and no actual survival threshold, it is not an optimal point chosen by nature, but a copy cost-inherited reliability Pareto frontier.

## 65.6 Boundary geometry covariance and twin daughter cell holdout

When the volume ratio p of the first daughter cell fluctuates to Var(p)=σp², the common geometry RENDERER increases the molecular number dispersion and creates positive covariance between species.

Var(Xᵢ)=Nᵢ/4+Nᵢ(Nᵢ-1)σp², Cov(Xᵢ,Xⱼ)=NᵢNⱼσp² (i≠j)

In other words, the approximate rank-one positive co-tom mode proportional to N Nᵀ is a signal of cleavage plane/volume noise, species-specific block overdispersion is localization·packet, and the dispersion index κ\<1 is a candidate for active equalization.The symmetrical daughter cells and 105 min of the public 4D whole-cell model are constrained and corrected with experimental information and are therefore not reused as independent WRRA holdouts.

The freezing experiment connects a single mother cell ID and twin daughter cells A/B, and records the number of mother cell molecules, number of daughter cell molecules, volume ratio, division plane, next growth/division of each daughter cell, and measurement error model.Independent PREDICTION PASS is given only when the uncorrected paired-daughter log score, P₀/P₁/P₂, κ(N)·interspecies covariance, and generation survival curve exceed the pre-registration criteria.

The final grade is “WRRA-to-life mapping STRUCTURAL PASS / finite binomial distribution·sister dependence offspring law EXACT / iid restoration extinction EXACT UNDER STATED MODEL / HupA cliff EXACT FOR DECLARED PANEL / equalization·reliability front CONDITIONAL / common-p covariance EXACT UNDER MODEL / public 4D reconstruction PASS, NOT INDEPENDENT / paired-daughter prediction OPEN”.

Chapter Conclusions The WRRA Life Model III feasibly closes the mathematical layer of lineage closure.Low-molecular-water integer cliffs, active equalization, and boundary geometry covariance provide specific prediction forms.However, biological lineage closure remains open until tested with actual molecular pair daughter cell data.

# Chapter 76 WRRA Life Model 0: The First Life System

Conclusion of this chapter: First life is not the same as modern autonomous cells or LUCA.WRRA Life Model 0 operationally defines the first life as ‘the state in which a bounded chemical system maintains heritable functional closure and first achieves the expected number of functional daughter lines Rlife\>1.’This definition and mathematical threshold is workable, but the actual original sequence, place, act, and historical path is OPEN.

## 66.1 Separating First Life and LUCA

DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22291862 distinguishes chemical reproducers, protocells, first living lineages, autonomous cells, and LUCAs into different objects.Chemical structures that only grow and divide may not be inherited, and even protocells with boundaries may not preserve function across generations.Conversely, even if the first life depended heavily on external geochemical cycles, it is possible to create a selectable lineage.LUCA is the last common ancestor of existing cellular life, not the first transition of life itself.

A 2024 phylogenomic study deduced LUCA to be a complex prokaryotic-level anaerobic acetogen that dates back approximately 4.2 billion years.These results support the logical distinction between first life and LUCA, but all traits and dates in LUCA depend on phylogenetic models, fossils, and isotope calibration.

## 66.2 Minimal functional closure of five owners

The minimum candidate must have ① information that preserves the functionally relevant sequence or composition, ② a catalyst that accelerates the closed reaction, ③ a boundary that passes the feed while concentrating components, ④ energy binding that maintains the reaction out of equilibrium, and ⑤ a distribution that transfers sufficient function to the daughter lineage.If even one failure occurs repeatedly, a one-time chemical event cannot become a living genealogy.

FIRST LIVING LINEAGE ⇔ bounded system ∧ heritable functional variation ∧ Rlife\>1

This equation is not a proof of the universal definition of life, but a decision rule adopted in this study.We measure boundaries, functional inheritance, and lineage continuation instead of ‘autonomy’ so as not to exclude viruses, symbionts, non-sequential inheritance, and other planetary chemistries.

## 66.3 Precise threshold for copy errors and non-copy closures

The number of function-related sites that are independently copied is L, the average probability that one site will be copied without a function destruction error is f, and the non-copying probability that boundary, catalyst, energy, and distribution are successful together in one daughter line is K.In the declared baseline where each factor is independent and creates two daughters, the probability that one daughter maintains functional closure, q, and the expected number of functional daughters are as follows.

q=Kfᴸ, Rlife=2q=2Kfᴸ

The critical radiative accuracy for Rlife=1 is fc=(2K)⁻¹ᐟᴸ, and there is a physically possible f≤1 solution only when K\>1/2.If K≤1/2, even a perfect copy f=1 does not exceed one lineage on average, so increasing heredity accuracy alone cannot compensate for a renderer that frequently fails.

This result is EXACT within the declared binary split, average f, and independent factorization.If there are actual positional effects of errors, correlated membrane/energy failures, daughter cell asymmetry, or environmental changes between generations, q=Kfᴸ cannot be applied as is and the resulting kernel must be measured again.

## 66.4 Conditional numerical scenarios and information budget

K=0.61965 multiplied by boundary 0.90, catalyst 0.85, energy 0.90, and distribution 0.90 is a sensitivity input and not a measurement of the early Earth.If L=100, fc=0.997857, and if f=0.999, Rlife=1.121.At the same K, the critical length is approximately 10.6, 21.3, 42.8, and 214.4 sites when f=0.98, 0.99, 0.995, and 0.999, respectively.

Lmax=ln\[1/(2K)\]/ln f (0\<f\<1, K\>1/2)

This calculation shows that at low copy accuracy, the inheritance information must be short and many functions can be performed by the environment.However, as site independence and the definition of ‘function destruction error’ change, the numerical value also changes, so it is not a universal life information limit, but a MODEL-CONDITIONAL BUDGET.

## 66.5 environment renderer and evidence compatible candidates
