The initial genealogy does not need to create all monomers, gradients, and cleavage conditions in-house.Wetting-drying cycles, temperature fluctuations, photochemistry, mineral surfaces, and redox gradients provide concentration, activation, and mechanical fragmentation, and evolution may have gradually internalized those functions into catalysis, membrane machinery, and metabolism.At this time, the unit selected is not the environment itself, but a bounded lineage whose internal state changes the production of descendants.

L₀=(RNA-like information)+(short catalytic peptides)+(permeable fatty-acid boundary)+(repeatable energy cycle)

RNA-like infopolymers, short catalytic peptides, permeable fatty-acid vesicles, and repeatable energy cycles are candidate combinations that are compatible with current evidence.Common precursor chemistry, vesicle capture, growth, and division, non-enzymatic RNA synthesis in model protocells, peptide-vesicle binding, and adaptive evolution of synthetic protocells support each module.However, neither the seed-free, self-sustained abiogenesis nor the first historical genealogy that brought them together were reconstructed through experiments.

## 66.6 Group rather than one entity, genealogy more than once

Since the initial compartment is likely to have had frequent leakage, fusion, and horizontal exchange, a group with state distribution πt(x) and outcome kernel B(y\|x) is more appropriate than the ‘first one entity.’The distribution of the next generation is determined by chemical reproduction itself.

πt₊₁(y) ∝ ∫B(y\|x)πt(x)dx

Selection is not a word that adds an external selector, but rather summarizes the differences in descendant distribution that different states produce.Although there are relatively few systems with a spectral radius exceeding 1, the kernel of the actual initial environment has not yet been measured.

## 66.7 Judgment and Freezing Experiment

The experimental unit connects a mother compartment with two daughters and tracks them until their next reproduction.The mother cell sequence/copy number, catalyst/peptide composition, membrane composition and geometry, energy/supply history, composition of the two daughters, and the next division of each daughter are recorded.Functional error definition of L·f, components of K, observation window, and P₀/P₁/P₂ judgment criteria are frozen before viewing the results.

The final grade is “First life and LUCA distinction CONCEPTUALLY EXACT / Rlife=2Kfᴸ and fc EXACT UNDER DECLARED BASELINE / K=0.61965 numerical CONDITIONAL / environment renderer STRUCTURALLY EVIDENCE-COMPATIBLE / RNA-peptide-fatty-acid candidate LEADING BUT NON-UNIQUE / first group inference PLAUSIBLE / historical first lineage and“Self-sustained experimental reconstruction OPEN”.

Chapter Conclusion WRRA Life Model 0 does not determine ‘which molecule was the first’, but presents the minimum measurement boundary beyond which chemistry passes into a living lineage.The key is the joint closure between generations of the five functions, not just information or information.This biological application embodies the universal running grammar hypothesis of minimal computation, but does not count as a physical verification of the WRRA cosmology.

# Chapter 77 WRRA-Worm 0.1-0.4: Sensorimotor Loop and Embodied Intelligence

Conclusion of this chapter: The 0.1 circuit from DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22298363 and the 0.2-0.4 learning/comparison experiment from DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22502619 extend WRRA to a viable embodied AI.Food acquisition under delayed cueing and intermittent sensation showed residue dependence in internal testing, but the general prediction superiority of WRRA was rejected in same-optimal budget comparisons.The lowest crash rate is the conditional safety rating within this benchmark.The overall judgment is SUPPORTED for AI design potential / NOT SUPPORTED for general comparative advantage / OPEN for actual C. elegans reconstruction and general intelligence.

## 67.1 Response of structure, state, and boundary

The nervous system of the adult hermaphrodite C. elegans is usually described as 302 neurons, but the dynamics and behavior are not determined by the linkage table alone.In this chapter, nerve cells or selected minimum state units are set to SOURCE, chemical nerve junction, gap junction, and neuromuscular junction are set to RELATION, and sensory input, muscle cell output, body, and environment interface are set to BOUNDARY.Membrane potential-like activity v, adaptation a, and available resource q are RESIDUEs that compress the effects of the immediately preceding present into the current state without separately storing the entire past.

x(k)=\[v₁…v_N, a₁…a_N, q₁…q_N, E\]ₖ

Here, E is the environmental reservoir and all variables are dimensionless before being corrected by physical quantities.The sentence ‘Calculate the next present only with the present’ is not a verified space-time law, but a state expression rule.If past path information still remains in the observed next state, the current state vector is incomplete and a residue variable must be added or this expression must be rejected.

## 67.2 23 node contact reaction circuit

The circuit for explanation has 23 nodes and 57 relationships.The ALM/AVM sensory group in the anterior contact leads to DA/VA backward readout via AVD/AVE–AVA, and the PLM sensory group in posterior contact leads to DB/VB forward readout via PVC–AVB.There is gap-junction leveling in the left and right pairs, and weak competition is placed in the forward and backward command groups.This wiring summarizes functional motifs known from previous research and does not replicate the edge list for each neural junction of the official connectome.

The chemical relationship W is a matrix with direction and sign, and the electrical relationship G is a symmetric combination matrix.The chemical term c=W\[v\]₊ conveys positive presynaptic activity, and the electrical term gᵢ=ΣⱼGᵢⱼ(vⱼ-vᵢ) is a graph-Laplacian type diffusion that reduces the coupled state difference.The full network output when all external tables without polarity are read as +1 is only a data loading test and does not count as biological prediction.

c(k)=W\[v(k)\]₊, gᵢ(k)=ΣⱼGᵢⱼ\[vⱼ(k)-vᵢ(k)\]

## 67.3 Fixed-present update and bounded state

The leakage of v, current resource q, chemical/electrical input, sensory input u, and adaptation a are tied to one-step update.tanh and clip prevent numerical divergence.This is not a complete electrophysiological model of C. elegans, but a minimal state space model that tests sensory selectivity and elimination effects.

v(k+1)=tanh{0.72v(k)+q(k)⊙\[c(k)+g(k)+0.85u(k)-0.62a(k)\]}

a(k+1)=clip\[0.94a(k)+0.06\|v(k+1)\|,0,1\]

A restart test is used to check whether the same next state occurs when the same serialization current x(k) and the same input u(k) are given.Probability expansion should require equality of conditional next-state distributions instead of point value equality.Even with the same input as the fully measured present, this fixed-present Markov representation fails if the previous path continues to change the next-state distribution and is not removed by finite residue expansion.

## 67.4 Resource ledger and RESET

Since the sum of neural activity is not conserved energy, separate computational resources q and environmental storage E are provided.When an activity consumes q, it sends the same amount to E and returns q from E on recovery, preserving B=Σᵢqᵢ+E in the declared update.This ledger is not ATP or measured thermodynamic free energy.It is a calculation device that tracks saturation and recovery until it is corrected or replaced with metabolic data.

B(k)=Σᵢqᵢ(k)+E(k)=constant

RESET is not a supernatural event, but a saturation/recovery rule that prevents the allowable boundary from being exceeded.The intuition that ‘the exit becomes the entrance’ is implemented in the reverse flow of q → E during activity and E → q during rest.In order to claim a matter/energy analysis, a separate ledger is needed that links the units of q and E, supply, consumption, and heat release to the actual metabolic amount.

## 67.5 Exact status of internal verification results

In 320 discrete updates, forward contact was given at 45–69 steps, and backward contact was given at 190–214 steps.The average posterior score after anterior contact was 0.071587 and after AVA removal was 0.000000.The average advancement score after posterior contact was 0.021224 and after AVB removal was 0.000000.The same current restart error was 0.0, and the beginning and end of the resource ledger were 23/23.

This figure is a unit test that shows that the designed path, removal effect, deterministic implementation, and ledger preservation are working as code.AVA/AVB dependence is hard-wired and is therefore not a new biological discovery.Therefore, the current rating is BOUNDED UPDATE·RESTART·LEDGER INTERNAL PASS / DESIGNED CIRCUIT RESPONSE PASS / FULL 302-NEURON BIOLOGICAL PREDICTION OPEN.

## 67.6 c302 extension and external validation

The provided adapter can read tables such as OpenWorm c302 linked data by finding the pre/source, post/target, type, weight/count, and sign/polarity columns of CSV/XLSX.However, before executing the expansion, the sex, developmental stage, connectome version, and raw data hash must be frozen, and chemical nerve junctions, gap junctions, overlap, direction, number of contacts, and missing polarity must be separated.It cannot be determined that a complete animal has been recreated just by loading 302 names.

A fair test should compare the M0 static connectome linear model, M1 normal nonlinear state-space model, and M2 WRRA state-relation-resource model under the same input, data, and parameter budget.M2's a and q should produce repeatable gains even after complexity penalties in unused stimulus, habituation, recovery, and elimination data.Nerve output must pass through muscle cells, body, and environment to return to the next sensory input, so minimal closure is the next loop.

environment(k)→sensory u(k)→neural x(k+1)→muscle m(k+1)→body/environment(k+1)

## 67.7 0.1 disconfirmation conditions and decision

A fixed present representation should lose any additional predictive power of past paths with the same sufficient state and input.Relationship-centered explanations must match the direction and magnitude of unused stimulus, object, and elimination responses with frozen wiring·polarity.Usually there is no empirical need for a residue/resource layer if the model generalizes equally or better with less complexity.If recalibration is required for each experiment or the upstream numerological SOURCE fails to make quantitative predictions unique to the c302 data, the upstream–downstream connection remains a structural analogy.

Chapter Conclusion WRRA-Worm 0.1 does not claim to have created life, but rather turns the question of how current residual states, networks, boundaries, and limited resources can constitute a sense-action loop into an actionable question.The current achievement is the internal consistency of the toy model and the reproduction of the design response.The decision for actual C. elegans depends on comparison of frozen c302 data, polarity uncertainty, calcium/behavioral holdout, neuromuscular dynamics, body-environment closed loop, and reference model.

## 67.8 Contract separation of interpretation, calculation, prediction and control

Subsequent studies fix the task at T=(I_t,Y_tau,tau,epsilon,C_max,L).I_t is the currently available information, Y_tau is the target amount, tau is the target time, epsilon is the tolerance, C_max is the calculation budget, and L is the loss.Reading current sensory values, circuits, restarts, and resource ledgers is interpretation or calculation, and making choices after clues disappear, searching for food between intermittent senses, and performance in an unseen environment are predictions and controls that reduce future losses.Reading food signals now and acquiring food in the future are not the same contract.

This classification applies the boundary classifier of DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22447864, and does not mean that this experiment has verified the entire classifier itself, including compatible-state diameter, Bayes risk, and dynamic amplification.The number of collisions is a result of a mixture of current obstacle boundary analysis and future safety control, so it is not reduced to just analysis performance.

## 67.9 WRRA-Worm 0.2: Delay clue and present residue

Left and right clues were presented twice and then updated five times without input to select an action.The average accuracy of learning 2,500 episodes from 12 initializations increased from 41.67% to 100%, and returned to 50% when the present residue was deleted.At the same time, the maximum restart error was 0 and the resource ledger error was 3.26x10^-15.This is an internal calculation result showing that the present residue was necessary for selection in the designed binary delay task, and does not prove general predictive power or biological memory of the external environment.

## 67.10 WRRA-Worm 0.3: Embodied closed loop and food acquisition

0.3 formed a closed loop in which a two-dimensional body with position, direction, and energy moves forward, backward, and turns left and right, and the changed environment returns the next sensory input.Sense of food direction was allowed only once every four updates.The first design showed reward hacking, which only avoided collisions by rotating without obtaining food, and was saved as a failure record.After correcting for reward and current SOURCE exposure, food acquisition increased from 0.0438 to 0.9958 per episode, and average reward increased from -11.6639 to 1.7283.When residue was removed, food decreased to 0.0979 and reward decreased to -8.7640.

Rewards and food increased across all eight initializations, resulting in a one-sided Wilcoxon p=0.0039 for the initialization mean.However, this is the internal stability recovered after calibration in one designed world.Since this is a modification after the discovery of reward hacking, it is not counted as independent generalization evidence until the freeze comparison of 0.4.

## 67.11 WRRA-Worm 0.4: Same optimization budget comparison

The memoryless, Elman RNN, GRU, and WRRA policies were each set to exactly 84 optimization variables and the same Cross-Entropy Method was applied.Each model was trained with 5 iterations, 24 generations, 20 candidates per generation, and 4 training worlds, and four conditions of distribution, 8 sense cycles, 4 obstacles, and 1.3 radius were evaluated in 40 unobserved worlds for each iteration.However, since WRRA additionally contains 57 fixed sparse relations and 6 gap-junction pairs, the equality of 84 optimization variables does not mean equality of the total amount of prior information or the total amount of calculation.

Food acquisition within the distribution was memoryless 1.510, RNN 1.395, GRU 1.040, and WRRA 0.275.The average rewards were 8.116, 8.516, 4.109, and 0.859, and the average collisions were 8.760, 4.345, 15.410, and 2.600, respectively.RNN and memoryless had higher reward/feed than WRRA in all 5 repetitions, one-sided Wilcoxon p=0.03125.The average execution time was 90.91 microseconds per step for WRRA and 48.83-65.53 for the other three models.This value varies depending on Python implementation and device, so it is not a proof of algorithm complexity.

## 67.12 What exactly do negative results and low crash rates mean?

The pre-admission fix 0.4B, which now passes the SOURCE directly to the renderer, increased the intra-distribution reward from -0.319 to 0.859 and lowered the collision from 9.125 to 2.600 compared to 0.4A, but reduced the payoff from 0.310 to 0.275.Additional modifications after the results were stopped to limit benchmark overfitting.WRRA had the fewest average conflicts in all four evaluation conditions, but had weak goal acquisition.Therefore, the low collision rate is a benchmark-specific safety tendency created by a fixed sparse structure of conservative boundary prior and learning control, and is not evidence of general intelligence, reward advantage, or minimal computation advantage.

## 67.13 Minimum computation update: open residue only when needed

In the Markov task, where only the current senses are sufficient, the memoryless policy was the most efficient for food acquisition, and in the partial observation condition where the sensory interval was doubled, the small RNN was the strongest for reward and food.Therefore, minimal computation should not be a principle of always maintaining one structure, but a principle of conditionally opening a state equal to the time information lost in the current observation.In the objective function J=L+lambda C, the next implementation hypothesis is a gate that activates each residue circuit only when the future loss Delta L_i it reduces is greater than the calculation and maintenance cost lambda Delta C_i.

g_i(t)=1{Delta L_i(t)\>lambda Delta C_i(t)}

This gate is not the result of the measurement completed at 0.4, but a subsequent design specified by a negative comparison.WRRA's candidate for strength is not always greater memory, but the explicitness of being able to audit which residues and relations are open and why, and close them when unnecessary.

## 67.14 Physics, biology, AI boundaries and next exam

The current conclusion is COMPUTATIONAL PROOF OF CONCEPT.The 23 nodes were not a compressed reconstruction of 302 neurons, and the signs and weights of the 57 relationships were not appropriate for the actual connectome.The resource ledger is also dimensionless and not the same as biological energy.The next test should be conducted in the following order: equalization of the total structural information amount, event-based calculation and energy measurement, long-term partial observation task, and frozen external comparison of the measured connectome, neuronal polarity, and body mechanics.

Chapter Conclusions WRRA-Worm 0.1-0.4 showed that WRRA can be implemented as an AI design language, but did not show general prediction superiority.Current strengths include the auditability of tracking present residue·SOURCE·RELATION·resource·renderer·claim boundaries together, and the low conflict propensity for this benchmark.To become a competitive minimal computation AI, a conditional structure is needed to open and close the residue and relation circuits according to task information.This result is neither a biological reconstruction of an actual nematode nor an independent physical proof of the WRRA cosmology.

# Chapter 78 WRRA Integrative Biology: Memory, Differentiation, and Closure

Conclusion of this chapter: We link the lineage from the WRRA-Cell audit at DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22304462 to Integrative Biology 3.0 at DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22448911 with the Integrative Biology 4.1 biaxial complete audit at DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22459333.The first axis goes from DNA to RNA/protein execution, molecular distribution, and the boundary between minimal cells and first life, and the second axis goes from epigenetic memory to differentiation, genealogy, aging, cancer, spatial niche, and causal editing.The point of 4.1 is not to declare that all closures were successful, but to assign reproducible judgments to the remaining three trials.PURE non-ribosomal mass regeneration failed at 0.08, Syn3A distribution only passed within the computational model, and although the synthetic protocell continued for more than three generations under external support, autonomous closure was not established.

## 68.1 Converting the intuition of cosmology into a verification contract in biology

The question for WRRA-Cell is not whether the cell rereads its list of past events every time.We ask whether, if there is any past influence necessary to calculate the next present, is it embodied in present molecular, regulatory, metabolic, boundary states or measurable sufficient statistics?This closure principle requires the physical realization of memory, not its negation.

Xₖ=(xₖ,hₖ,nₖ,sₖ,bₖ,wₖ,dₖ),

x is fast program activity, h is slow present residue, n·s·b·w is a dimensionless ledger of nutrients, substrates, biomass, and waste, and d is damage.The six programs of GROWTH, STRESS, REPAIR, AUTOPHAGY, QUIESCENCE, and DEATH and the marker gene set are not a standard complete decomposition of actual cells, but a pre-frozen hypothetical map.RNA expression is not equivalent to protein activity, metabolic flux, or cell death.

## 68.2 0.1: Executable toy cells and internal tests

0.1 implemented fast state and slow residue, nutrient, substrate, biomass, waste, and damage ledgers with 420 discrete current updates.The maximum error for the closed dimensionless ledger M=n+s+b+w was 2.49×10⁻¹⁴, and the restart error for copying the complete current was 0.After removing the REPAIR function, the death peak increased 16.38 times from 0.050924 to 0.834114, and in a retry with fast conditions and resources, the adaptive residue reduced the death peak by 15.13%.

Mₖ=nₖ+sₖ+bₖ+wₖ, Mₖ₊₁=Mₖ \[Closed dimensionless toy boundary\]

These numbers are a unit test to ensure that the designed wiring, restart, and ledger work as code.The REPAIR removal effect and adaptive residue effect are phenomena embedded in the binding structure, so they are not biological predictions newly discovered in nature.A grade of 0.1 is STRUCTURAL PASS and biological validation has not yet occurred.

## 68.3 0.2–0.3.1: Competitive model and fair residue testing

The recursive state, simply named residue, mathematically overlaps with the existing state-space, adaptation, and memory model.To distinguish this, linear Markov M0, nonlinear Markov M1, and M2 with the present residue h added compete in the same holdout.

M0: xₜ₊₁=A\[xₜ,uₜ,qₜ\]+b+εₜ

M1: xₜ₊₁=Aφ₂(\[xₜ,uₜ,qₜ\])+b+εₜ

M2: hₜ₊₁=λhₜ+(1−λ)xₜ₊₁, xₜ₊₁=A\[xₜ,uₜ,qₜ,hₜ\]+b+εₜ

0.3 separates claims that require static data from claims that require temporal data.Static Perturb-seq can see the average program response and state distribution shift, but cannot identify the recovery trajectory of the same cell, whether the residue of the first stimulus changes the second response, or the decay rate and long-term rollout of the residue.If there is no temporal sequence, at least three time points per trajectory, independent repetition per protocol, perturbation unit train/test separation, and no time-dependent boundary input, the temporal claim gate is closed.

0.3.1 introduced full-library normalization, near-zero variance processing, direct target gene exclusion, dynamic boundary input, identical observation covariates for M0/M1/M2, validation-only tuning, and independent locus unit bootstrap.The previous procedure of calling test error BIC was also discarded.This is a fairness contract that prevents comparisons that give the residue model more information to win.

## 68.4 Conditional long-term forecast gain recovered from synthetic data

The conditional rollout RMSE of the modified residue-positive production system was M0 0.018651, M1 0.020672, and M2 0.015505.M2 was 16.87% (95% paired-bootstrap CI 15.98%–17.62%) lower than M0 and 25.00% (21.71%–27.31%) lower than M1.Conversely, in the memory-negative generation system, the nonlinear Markov M1 was the best at 0.013644, and M2 was 0.018923, 38.70% higher than M1.All four pre-registration conditions were passed.

The exact meaning of this result is that M2 was recovered in a world where the selector was designed to generate residues, and a strong Markov baseline model was able to be selected in a memory-free world.This is a synthetic mechanism recovery from the verification pipeline and is not evidence that the actual cell has a WRRA residue.

## 68.5 0.4: Real K562 Static Perturb-seq Audit

0.4 applied the 34-marker map of 0.3.1 without modification to the Harmonizome standardized gene-by-perturbation signature matrix derived from K562 essential-gene CRISPRi Perturb-seq by Replogle et al.The provider reference matrix has 8,055 genes and 2,012 perturbation columns, and 1,907 unique targets were analyzed.The input SHA-256 is 1f9e32ff09b5516807893b1515e96fe23f5e1894687b5a390b7603e9a21cb06f.

Among the six source programs, only GROWTH and REPAIR had sufficient independent targets, so only 12 out of 36 relationships could be estimated.As a result of selecting a random target set of the same size for each source 20,000 times and applying Benjamini–Hochberg correction to the 12 p values, the relationship with q\<0.05 was 0/12.Toy sign agreement was 4/12 = 33.3% (binomial p = 0.388), Pearson r = 0.365 (p = 0.243) with toy weight, and Spearman ρ = 0.462 (p = 0.131), all of which were not significant.

Therefore, the tested submatrix does not support the frozen Toy combination matrix W.This result should not be interpreted as success.At the same time, since there were no four source programs and all time axes, the entire WRRA or temporal residue cannot be rejected based on this data alone.A freeze verdict of 0.4 is PARTIAL NON-CONFIRMATION / INSUFFICIENT FOR GLOBAL VERDICT.

## 68.6 Physics and biology boundary audit

M=n+s+b+w of 0.1 is the conservation of the dimensionless token defined by the code, not the physical conservation of mass, ATP, free energy, entropy, or the sum of program activities.Actual cells are open, non-equilibrium systems that receive nutrients and oxygen and emit heat and metabolic products.To assert a physical interpretation, the unitary boundary fluxes of carbon, nitrogen, ATP, redox equivalent, heat, and emissions must be closed, respectively.

CRISPRi is an intervention, but it does not identify structural causal coefficients between programs simply by reversing the sign of the static average signature.Incomplete knockdown, compensation, growth selection, pleiotropy, cell composition, batch and pretreatment may all be involved.For the same reason, the success or failure of cellular applications does not count as physical proof or disproof of the WRRA cosmology.

## 68.7 What makes WRRA-Cell a separate theory?

If h in M2 remains a simple exponential smoother, WRRA-Cell may be a useful re-expression, but it is not an independent biological theory.To be a distinct theory, it must present measurable physical candidates for residues, joint prediction of relation weights and resource boundaries, pre-registered RESET conditions, and parameter transport between cell lines, perturbations, and batches.

The key identification predictions are as follows.Although the fast observable transcript state and the current boundary input are sufficiently matched, the rechallenge response is different in cell groups with different perturbation histories, and M2, which includes measurable present residue candidates, should improve holdout than the strong M0/M1 with the same information and the same tuning budget.If residue candidates are selectively removed, the difference disappears and must be transferred to a new cell line and batch.

## Failure condition for 68.8 0.5

If a strong Markov model repeatedly predicts as well or better than M2, the need for an empirical residue layer is discarded.Even in the matched current state, hysteresis effects remain, but if they are not compressed into a finite and measurable residue, the current finite-state closure is rejected.If the material/energy ledger with units does not match, the resource analysis is discarded.If the relation map is not re-adjusted and transported for each data, it lowers the claim of universal relational laws.

For the next experiment, the program direction, duplicate processing, minimum number of targets, and exclusion rules must be frozen before viewing the results, and more than ten independent targets per program must be secured in at least four and, if possible, all six source programs.Static relation tests and washout–rechallenge time residue tests should be separated, and raw or quasi-raw data·non-targeting controls·guide efficacy·batch·lineage information should be included.

The final grade is ‘0.1 Toy structure STRUCTURAL PASS / 0.3.1 Synthesis mechanism recovery and verification pipeline VALIDATED / 0.4 Freeze W’s K562 partial audit PARTIAL NON-CONFIRMATION / Real cell temporal residue·physical resource conservation·RESET·parameter transport OPEN’.

Chapter Conclusions The most important achievement of WRRA-Cell 0.1–0.4 is not that it declares that the cell follows WRRA, but that it reduces upstream intuitions to a computational contract that can fail and preserves first results that are not supported by actual data.The universal logical hypothesis of minimal computation becomes stronger only when it passes domain-specific falsification and independent predictions, not when it hides this disconfirmation.

## 68.9 Life Model IV: Epigenetic memory is not the survival of marks but the reconstruction of states

DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22429511 asks how cells with identical or nearly identical DNA create different identities and how they are restored after division.Genome G is a relatively stable SOURCE, methylation·histone·chromatin accessibility·3D genome organization·transcription factor binding is set to execution gate, RNA·protein·metabolism·signal is set to short- or mid-term residue, and function, form, and lineage behavior are set to OBSERVABLE.Here, germline transgenerational inheritance beyond state reorganization and embryonic reprogramming through mitosis is a separate argument.

Xₜ=(Cₜ,Mₜ,Aₜ,Hₜ,Rₜ,Pₜ,Sₜ,Qₜ), Oₜ=R(G,

C·M·A·H refers to three-dimensional chromatin, DNA methylation, accessibility, and histone state, and R·P·S·Q refers to RNA, protein, signaling/metabolism, and cellular context.Since one RNA snapshot or one label cannot uniquely identify the same internal state, cellular memory requires measuring multiple states and lineages together.

## 68.10 Operational definition of memory and replication-dilution-restoration operators

In a strong operational definition, memory is a property in which the past state controls the genome, subsequent environment, lineage, and current cell state even after removal of the guidance signal, and additionally predicts future response or functional lineage bias, and selective intervention of candidate residues changes the result.Simple mark persistence, age prediction, or cluster separation alone does not establish causal memory.

Mres(τ)=I(Xpast;Yfuture \| G,Ufuture,lineage,current state)

Xₖ₊₁=Φrep (

ρₘ is the retention coefficient for each label.A 1/2 dilution is not mandatory for all labels.DNMT1–UHRF1 restoration of parental histones, hemimethylated CpGs, mitotic bookmarking, and three-dimensional chromatin require different distribution and restoration laws.Therefore, division is not a simple copy of memory, but a distribution of material and reconstruction through a reader-writer circuit.

## 68.11 Sister cell covariance and multiple memory clocks

Cov(Xd1,Xd2)=Cov(E\[Xd1\|M\],E\[Xd2\|M\])+E\[Cov(Xd1,Xd2\|M)\]

In this total-covariance decomposition, the sign of the second term is not fixed.It can be negative in the binomial distribution of a fixed total number of molecules, but can be 0 or positive in independent Poisson generation, common environment, and active equalization.Therefore, the sign of the sister correlation must be derived from the generative model and measurement design, not from intuition.

Life Model V of DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22440193 extends memory to the eigenmodes of combined layers rather than the half-life of a single marker.The generation distance d and the elapsed time t are separated, and circular inference in which the same signal used for lineage estimation is reused for memory verification is prohibited.

Cℓ(d)=Σᵣaℓᵣλᵣᵈ+Bℓ, Zchild=Astate Zparent+ΓX+ξ

Cross-layer memory is maintained only when the off-diagonal Astate predicts the next-generation covariance of the unseen lineage better than the diagonal layer model and the no-memory model, even after a complexity penalty.If the common unity decay rate is sufficient or if off-diagonal transfer is not transferred to a new lineage, the combined layer hypothesis is lowered.

## 68.12 Life Model VI: Non-commutative sequence of cell fate manipulation

Differential prediction is that even if the total realized exposure of gate opening G, lineage transcription-factor pulse T, and maintenance-mark writing W is matched, the long-term fate may depend on the order.To avoid renaming common timing effects, the six permutations, simultaneous treatment, single treatment, catalytic-dead control, intranuclear AUC, target occupancy, washout, cell cycle, and survival must be fixed in advance.

xGTW=ΦWΦTΦGx₀, \[Li,Lj\]=LiLj−LjLi, additive null ⇒ \[Li,Lj\]=0

The general order effect is separated from the hypothesis that GTW is superior to other orders.If another order wins, the GTW-optimal hypothesis is rejected, but the noncommutativity itself may remain.After matching the realized AUC and occupancy, if the order coefficient falls within the prior equivalence interval or the difference disappears in several divisions after washout, the long-term cell fate memory claim is withdrawn.

## 68.13 Life Models VII–IX: Induction, selection, reversible editing, and extracellular memory

In aging, clonal hematopoiesis, and cancer recurrence, state induction and selection of existing clones within the same lineage must be separated.Bulk before–after differences do not distinguish between the two mechanisms.The fact that even short cellular memories can become long population memories through selective proliferation and lineage transmission is a key candidate for Life Model VII, and it must overcome the baseline of genotype and current environment from external data.

ppost(y)=Z⁻¹∫KU(y\|x)sU(x)ppre(x)dx

Life Model VIII fixes the genome and the current environment and writes→erase→rewrite the candidate memory state Z to test whether function, restimulation response, and lineage continuation are returned together.Off-target, toxicity, selection, or general stress cannot be ruled out based on expression changes in only one direction.Life Model IX separates ownership of cells, niches, and interactions with a 2×2 crossover of young/aged or untreated/stimulated cells and niches.

Z₀→write Z₁→erase Z₀′→rewrite Z₁′, ΔY=ΔYcell+ΔYniche+ΔYcell×niche

## 68.14 Life Model

The integrated paper maintains the previous conclusion that DNA alone does not carry out life.At a minimum, life requires boundary B, replicable source S, decoding relation R, catalytic polymers P, energy/material flow E, and whole-cycle closure C to be closed together enough to be executed again in the next generation.In order not to hide the work done by the environment, the autonomous closure rate Λauto=Ninternal/Nrequired is recorded separately.

Lmin={B,S,R,P,E,C}, 0≤Λauto≤1, I(Xparent;Xoffspring)\>0, Rlife=2Kfᴸ\>1

This condition does not restore historically original molecules, locations, or pathways.A once-replicated molecule or a once-divided vesicle is not automatically life.The minimum judgment unit is whether memory, execution, repair, boundary reproduction, and selection create a functional genealogy for at least several generations.

## 68.15 Integrated evidence ladder and rebuttal contract

Currently closed are coding lower bounds under stated assumptions, finite distribution equations, total-covariance identity and feasible experimental designs.Minimum cell toy calculation and C. elegans circuit passed internal calculation, but K562 audit was partially disconfirmed.Epigenetics in general and context dependence receive literature support, but WRRA's unique multilayer transfer matrix, GTW order dominance, induced-state/selection mixture, reversible rescue, niche interaction, and repeated generation closure of Λauto are OPEN.

The common rejection rule is simple baseline priority.If the common clock, independent-layer, additive-AUC, selection-only, independent-cell, or non-causal-mark model predicts holdout equally or better under the same information and complexity budget, reduce the WRRA expansion.In clinical and genetic engineering applications, there is a fail-closed policy that does not allow release if any of Function∧Identity∧Genome stability∧No tumorigenicity∧Controllability fails.

## 68.16 Position up to Integrative Biology 2.1

What the two new papers strengthen is not the declaration that ‘cells follow the WRRA law’, but an audit structure that separates DNA information and material executors, present state and past effects, individual and lineage, cell and niche, one-time output and intergenerational closure.This expands the possibility that minimal computation is a universal methodology for analyzing complex systems, but it is not independent physical evidence for cosmology.The rank of a biological theory is raised only when differential predictions are replicated in independent genealogical data and intervention experiments.

## 68.17 Four-track verification contract for Integrative Biology 3.0

DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22448911 does not combine the four tracks into one grade of evidence.Tracks 1 and 2 are independent recalculations of downloadable public data, Track 3 is a structural simulation in declared equations, and Track 4 is a synthetic prediction and wet-lab pre-registration that has not yet been performed.We apply the fail-closed principle, which does not display synthetic trajectories as measurements and author results from the literature as self-recalculations.

The expansion state is set asThis decomposition is not a standard complete decomposition of biological entities, but an audit coordinate to track which layers have been observed and disturbed.

## 68.18 Track 1: DNA methylation–protein holdout between animals

In the EPI-Clone GEO primitive matrix, cells from LARRY mouse 3 and 4 were matched to antibody-derived-tag records, and then 8,850 cells, 663 assay columns, and 20 shared protein labels were used.In reciprocal holdout, in which CpGs were selected from one animal and tested only in the other, the average balanced accuracy for 120 state-associated CpGs was 0.487 and for all eligible CpGs was 0.502.The state-excluded CpG was 0.225, and the six-state majority baseline was 0.167.

These results support a reproducible coupling between DNA methylation and independently measured protein status in different animals.However, because the downloaded raw data did not include the cell-level LARRY truth table and the refined cell-type/pseudotime information from publications, the heritable clonal residue unique to WRRA could not be directly verified.The six states were also unsupervised protein clusters rather than prospectively determined biological markers.The decision is CROSS-LAYER STATE SUPPORT / CLONAL MEMORY OPEN.

## 68.19 Track 2: Spatial tumor plasticity and optional niche information

Among the 678,067 rows of the KPSpatial derived table, 673,263 complete observations were averaged into 150 sample × tumor groups of 39 samples to reduce spatial pseudorepetition.As a result of performing block permutation 5,000 times in the sample and applying BH correction to 11 neighborhood modules, 7 modules passed FDR\<0.05.S1, which used six epithelial programs in sample-group holdout, improved the global-mean baseline's R²=-0.130 and MAE=0.0648 with an average of R²=0.201 and MAE=0.0549.

However, S2, which includes all stromal·immune modules, worsened to R²=-0.023 and MAE=0.0605.Therefore, the strong proposition that ‘the more relationship layers you add, the better’ is not supported in this audit.Correlation of observational data does not distinguish niche → state causality, tumor-state → niche reconstruction, or common cause, so cell × niche cross-intervention is still the decisive test.The decision is SELECTIVE RELATIONAL SUPPORT / NICHE CAUSALITY OPEN.

## 68.20 Tracks 3–4: Structure prediction and wet-lab pre-registration

When six G/T/W permutations with the same 6-hour treatment and 2-hour interval were calculated from the declared gate–occupancy–mark model, the final mark of G→T→W was 0.5021, and the next rank T→G→W was 0.1630.In a set of 1,000 synthetic parameters in which the nine kinetic scales and half-life multipliers were changed by 0.7–1.3 times, respectively, G→T→W was ranked first.This is an internal robustness to the order dependence of the equation, not a biological fact measured in cells.

In the write-erase-rewrite synthesis system, the mark after erase was lowered to 0.0008 and returned to 0.5090 after rewrite, resulting in a model rescue fraction of 1.008.The actual judgment passes only when the lower limit of the 95% confidence interval of the functional Rrescue in independent biological repeats exceeds 0.50, and W\>WE, WER\>WE, target mark recovery, survival/proliferation balance, and off-target constraints are all satisfied.If the effect does not persist for at least three divisions after the editor is removed, it is classified as a temporary execution rather than a heritable memory.

## 68.21 Position on Integrative Biology 3.0

The evidence level raised by 3.0 is limited to two.The combination of DNA–protein differentiation status has received support from cross-animal published data reanalysis, and some epithelial spatial programs have improved prediction of tumor plasticity in sample holdout.On the other hand, indiscriminate addition of all niche layers failed, WRRA's unique clonal residue, GTW's actual biological dominance, write-erase-rewrite causal restoration and multi-generation autonomous closure are OPEN until independent experiments.

Chapter Conclusion WRRA Integrative Biology 3.0 does not prove life as a single equation.It separates which layers interpret the current state, which terms predict the future of new samples and new genealogies, which results are just structural simulations, and which await actual intervention.When this distinction is maintained, minimal computation becomes not an investigation that brings biology into evidence for cosmology, but a candidate for a universal methodology that compares information ownership and failure conditions in complex systems.

## 68.22 Integrative Biology 4.1: What exactly does two-axis completion mean?

In DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22459333, ‘complete’ does not mean that all mechanisms of life have passed.This is the completion of the research structure in which operating variables, thresholds, current judgments, and disconfirmation conditions are assigned to each question asked on the two axes of DNA → first life and epigenetic memory → cell fate.The evidence sequence is maintained in the following order: direct measurement, original author report, reanalysis/explicit inference, mechanistic simulation, and non-implemented pre-registration.

## 68.23 PURE: Mass ledger of self-renewal rate by generation

The 2026 PURE reconstruction study reconstructed a second-generation translation system that synthesized and functioned all 36 nonribosomal proteins within one PURE reaction.However, from 350 μg of starting non-ribosomal protein, the amount recovered after purification and concentration is 28 μg, so the measured mass regeneration rate is Rmass,nr = 28/350 = 0.08.It falls short of the threshold Rmass,nr≥1 that requires non-degeneracy in iterative equivalent transfer.

Assuming a constant efficiency of 0.08, Mnr(n)/Mnr(0)=(0.08)ⁿ and the ratio after three times is 5.12×10⁻⁴.This is a mass balance extrapolation, not the observed third-generation functional trajectory.Non-ribosomal synthesis and one-time functional reconstitution are PASS, and non-ribosomal mass replacement of this implementation is FAIL.Since the ribosome·rRNA·tRNA·DNA replication·energy·boundary were not reproduced together within a lineage, the overall PURE autonomous closure is not extrapolated to failure but is left as OPEN.

## 68.24 Syn3A: Molecular distribution between mother cell and two daughter cells

To avoid confusing the survival threshold fraction ϕ with the distribution error, the distribution error of molecular species x is set as Dx=\|px−v\|.px=Nx,1/(Nx,1+Nx,2) is the ratio of the number of molecules in the two daughter cells, and v=V1/(V1+V2) is the volume ratio.Dx=0 is volume proportional distribution.Under daughter cells of the same size and unbiased binomial distribution, RMS(Dx)=1/(2√Nx), E\[Dx\]≈1/√(2πNx).

In 50 complete cell cycles of the Syn3A 4-dimensional whole-cell model, the average parent cell had 881 ribosomes, 176 RNA polymerases, and 192 degradosomes at 105 minutes.The reported distribution of ribosome·degradosome·PtsG·GapDH daughter cells was near-binomial within the model and had no significant directional bias.The RMS distribution error expected from the number of parent cells was 1.68% for ribosomes and 3.61% for degradosomes.This figure is not the actual measurement value for each daughter cell, but rather the analytical expectation of the binomial null model.

Therefore, the Syn3A decision is COMPUTATIONAL PASS / DIRECT EXPERIMENT OPEN.Actual closure requires time-resolution tracking of one mother cell and two daughter cells, volume correction for each cell, counting for each molecular species, pre-registered Dx tolerance bands, and simultaneous measurement of whether all essential pools exceed the ϕ threshold.If the directional bias of a specific molecular species is reproduced even after controlling for geometry, cell cycle, DNA exclusion, and detection efficiency, the unbiased distribution hypothesis is rejected.

## 68.25 Synthetic Protocell: 3rd Generation Separation of Sustainment and Autonomy

The 2026 Synthetic Cell Preprint reported a five-cycle experiment combining genome replication, feeder-liposome growth, division, and selection using a defined PURE translation system and a 90 kbp genome split into seven plasmids.Therefore the narrow threshold Gpersist≥3 passes with this evidence.The approximately 5–10 generation operations given in the official description are auxiliary statements and not independent recalculations.

Among the 5th generation daughter cells, approximately 30% retained all 7 plasmids.The geometric growth proxy of complete genome descendants assuming ideal dichotomy, similar survival, and similar sampling is Rgenome,eff=\[2⁵×0.30\]^(1/5)≈1.57.This is a conditional inference calculated from reported endpoints rather than the paper authors' measurements, and is not the same as Rlife.The actual system relies on feeder vesicles, membrane capture, preformed ribosomes, and external molecular support and does not regenerate ribosomes.Therefore, the judgment is ‘continuously passed for more than 3 generations, autonomous closure not established’.

## 68.26 Final closed map on both axes

Closed on axis I are DNA/protein execution, one-time functional reconstitution of PURE, computational near-binomial distribution of Syn3A, and continuation of externally supported protocells for three or more generations.What is not closed is the Rlife\>1 lineage, which includes continuous replacement of Rmass,nr≥1, simultaneous molecular measurements of the actual mother cell and two daughter cells, and the joint updating of the genome, translation, energy, and boundaries without external substitution.In axis II, there is limited support for cross-animal DNA–protein state coupling and selective spatial programming.However, this model's unique clonal residue and write-erase-rewrite function recovery across multiple divisions are still OPEN.

Chapter Conclusion Integrative Biology 4.1 does not conclude that ‘DNA alone created life.’Rather, the boundary of first life was narrowed by separating lineage continuation under external support and internal resource regeneration.Currently, the most critical Axis I experiment is to see whether a single compartmentalized lineage can update the genome·translation·energy·boundary without substitution and maintain Rlife\>1 for at least three generations.In axis II, it remains to be seen whether reversible memory editing that traces the same lineage shows both functional restoration and continuation of divisions more than three times.This biological completion is the completion of the audit contract and is not an independent physical proof of the WRRA cosmology.

Part 11 WRRA extended to socially complex systems: economics and institutional memory

# Chapter 79 WRRA-Econ 0.1–0.5: Wild Drives and Measurement Boundaries

Conclusion of this chapter: DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22305789 is a frozen report that expresses confidence·fear·trust·debt·credit condition·institutional memory as a slow residue candidate for the fixed present and tests it step by step from toy ledger to actual US macro data.In synthetic systems, E2 was also selected as a new seed when the residue was the real cause, but universal prediction improvement in real data has not been statistically established.The crisis memory section showed more favorable signs than the calm period, but the current sentiment could not beat the 36-month delayed placebo.The final decision is PARTIAL SIGNAL / MEASUREMENT OPEN.

## 69.1 Current state of the economy and wild impulses

In this chapter, wild impulses are not an explanation that reduces the economy to one emotion.It is a state space hypothesis that asks whether fear, trust, optimization, debt, and risk management practices left over from previous shocks can change the next consumption, investment, employment, and lending even if the quick observation states such as production, employment, and credit are the same.Instead of rereading the past on a separate axis, we assume that the physical and institutional sufficient state h remaining in the present acts on the next present.

Sₜ=(xₜ,hₜ,rₜ,zₜ,cₜ), Sₜ₊₁=F(Sₜ,uₜ)

x is the fast observation state, h is the slow residue, r is resources and constraints, z is the net financial position of households, businesses, banks, governments, and central banks, and c is the crisis regime.SOURCE is the private sector, government, central bank, demand, finance, policy, supply, and narrative shock; RELATION is a combination of consumption, investment, credit, wages, taxes, and interest rates; BOUNDARY is liquidity, capital, production capacity, and crisis threshold.

hₜ₊₁=Dhₜ+(I−D)ψ(xₜ₊₁,uₜ,cₜ), Σⱼzⱼₜ=0

The financial position sum of 0 is a ledger of declared inter-sectoral accounting movements.This does not equate it with the conservation of real resources, production, utility, monetary value, or thermodynamic energy.Central bank balance sheets and government debt should not disappear outside the model, but should be attributed to designated sectors and boundary transactions.

## 69.2 0.1: Restart current like running structure

0.1 implemented a dimensionless toy economy with boom, first financial shock, policy support, recovery and second shock.The maximum division accounting error was 5.329×10⁻¹⁵, and the same current restart error was 0.In a restart where the fast state was adjusted and only the residue was different, the residue distance was 0.4549, and after the second shock, the output trough was 0.5800 for residue carried and 0.4827 for neutral, a difference of 20.16%.

This effect is a structural test designed within the equation where residue is the cause.There is no data evidence that the economy actually has shock memories of the same magnitude.The level of 0.1 is ACCOUNTING·RESTART·REGIME STRUCTURAL PASS.

## 69.3 0.2–0.3: Competition model and new seed reproduction

Linear Markov E0, nonlinear Markov E1, and residue-augmented E2 were competed in the same data/segmentation/evaluation window.In the positive generator, h causally acts on x, and in the negative generator, h is recorded but does not act on x.The first specification that recursively predicted levels accumulated long-term errors, so all models were changed to a common increment target.Since this modification was made after looking at test behavior, 0.2 is left as development validation only.

Δxₜ₊₁=G₀(xₜ,uₜ)+εₜ \[E0\], Δxₜ₊₁=G₁(ϕ₂(xₜ,uₜ))+εₜ \[E1\]

hₜ₊₁=Dhₜ+(I−D)ψₜ₊₁, Δxₜ₊₁=G₂(xₜ,uₜ,hₜ)+εₜ \[E2\]

At a causal-residue generator of 0.2, the best Markov RMSE was 0.01551, E2 was 0.00687, and the relative effect was +55.72%.In the noncausal generator, the best Markov was 0.00684 and E2 was 0.00838, which was −22.66%.After freezing the specification and applying it to five new random-seed blocks and 180 independent test trajectories at 0.3, both causal 5/5 and negative-control 5/5 directions were reproduced.The causal average improvement was 49.88% (block 40.10%–59.65%), and the noncausal average was −14.21%.

This result shows that in synthetic worlds where residue is the true cause, the selector can identify it, and can return E2 as a penalty in worlds where residue is not the cause.The identifiability of synthetic data and the reproduction of new seeds is a VALIDATED SYNTHETIC PIPELINE, not a verification of the real economy.

## 69.4 0.4: External audit of US monthly macro data

The fast state was composed of industrial production, non-agricultural employment, real retail sales, housing starts, unemployment rate, policy interest rate, prices, and Baa-Treasury spread, and the residue proxy was composed of the level of Michigan Consumer Sentiment, 3-month change, and causal 12-month EWMA of sentiment innovation.Changes in industrial production, employment, real retail sales, and unemployment rate were predicted for 1, 3, 6, and 12 months.Training is until 2006, model selection is from 2007–2014, and testing is from 2015.

Since the higher-order polynomial model has unstable extrapolation, especially in pandemic shocks, the key comparison was limited to linear E2, which has the same information as linear E0 plus only the three sentiment coordinates.The overall test gain was +0.25% for 1 month, +0.78% for 3 months, +1.74% for 6 months, and +3.58% for 12 months, but the 95% CIs were \[−0.82,+1.08\], \[−3.00,+1.83\], \[−6.21,+3.23\], and \[−9.39,+6.33\], respectively.Contains 0.

Before March 2020, E2 performance for all four horizons decreased slightly from −0.18% to −0.48%, and after that, all four horizons improved from +0.26% to +5.66%.Since uncertainty cannot be replaced by a consistent point estimate, the universal improvement judgment for real data is NOT ESTABLISHED.

## 69.5 0.5: Partial signal of crisis memory regime

The official recession month and the following 24 months were designated as the crisis-memory regime, and an expanding-window forecast was created for each month since 2000.Ridge regularization was selected using only the preceding 36 months of eligibility each year.E0 and E2 are linear, equal division, and equal complexity, and only in E2, three sentiment residue coordinates are added.

In 100 crisis-memory months, E2 improvement was +0.006%, +0.751%, +1.119%, and +4.664% at 1, 3, 6, and 12 months.In the calm regime, all four horizons deteriorated to −0.635%, −0.739%, −2.789%, and −7.057%.Although 8 of the 12 episodes × horizon were in the E2 direction, there were only three independent recession episodes: 2001, 2007–2009, and 2020, and only one 3-month horizon excluded 0 from the episode-cluster bootstrap.

When using only the recession month, three of the four horizons were positive numbers, and if the subsequent 6, 12, 18, and 24 months were included, all four were positive numbers, but when extended to 36 months, only one remained.This is compatible with finite crisis memory, but since it is a sensitivity analysis with a different window in the same sample, it cannot be interpreted as an independent confirmation or universal 24-month constant.

## 69.6 36 month delayed placebo and final downgrade

The same expanding-window procedure was applied to stale E2, which moved the three current sentiment coordinates 36 months into the past.Current E2 beat lagged E2 in only 4 out of 16 squares, and all of them were on a 12-month horizon.At 1, 3, and 6 months, older sentiment was better.Therefore, the gains from the crisis period cannot be uniquely attributed to the current sentiment residue.

Although lagged sentiment may have measured actual 36-month economic memory, it may have inadvertently proxy for omitted structural variables or specific events or sample composition.Current data do not distinguish between the two explanations.Therefore, rather than ‘36-month memory discovery’, the crisis-calm direction contrast is frozen at PARTIAL SIGNAL, and the measurement of residue and time alignment is frozen at OPEN.

## 69.7 Data quality and claim boundaries

The key limitation is construct mismatch.WRRA residue may be structural memory distributed across debt, credit supply, risk management practices, employment and investment delays, asset losses, and institutional trust, but the main proxy was a consumer survey.The number of independence crises is small, 2020 dominates the error, and the current revised FRED series is different from the real-time vintage forecasters saw at the time.The 3, 6, and 12 month target errors also overlap, reducing the effective independent sample.

Therefore, this chapter does not claim that WRRA predicted the economy, proved animal spirits, or discovered 36-month memory.Economic applications are not physical proof of Minimal Computing Cosmology, and are a separate field from biological applications.What is strengthened here is the complexity analysis process, that is, the methodology of decomposing states, relationships, boundaries, ledgers, and slow candidates and freezing failures with a strong baseline model, negative control, fresh-seed replication, and temporal placebo.

## 69.8 WRRA-Econ 0.6 Resumption Conditions

The next residue candidate must be separated into multiple dimensions, such as Hₜ=(Hsentiment, Hdebt, Hcredit, Employment, Institution), and different decay times must be pre-registered.Rather than additional fitting of the same US sample, non-US countries or multi-country panels, real-time data vintage and actual announcement delays, household/corporate debt, lending standards, credit spread, canceled investment, employment continuity, and institutional trust are needed.

The observed variables, complexity, splitting, and tuning budget of E0/E1/E2 must be kept the same and the lagged placebo and negative control must be passed together.The rating is not raised until episode-level consistency, cluster uncertainty, and calm-regime negative control are simultaneously closed in the out-of-event validation of the new event.

The resume gate is NEW DATA + PREREGISTERED MULTIDIMENSIONAL RESIDUE + TEMPORAL PLACEBO + OUT-OF-EVENT VALIDATION.WRRA-Econ 0.1–0.5 is frozen until all four conditions are ready.

The final rating is ‘0.1 Accounting/restart/regime STRUCTURAL PASS / 0.3 Synthetic residue identification new seed REPLICATED / 0.4 Reality universal prediction NOT ESTABLISHED / 0.5 Crisis-calm direction comparison PARTIAL SIGNAL / Current sentiment’s time uniqueness/structure residue measurement MEASUREMENT OPEN’.

Chapter Conclusion WRRA-Econ 0.1–0.5 is not a theory that proves the shock memory of the economy, but a freeze report that preserves how far it is calculated and where data prevents identification.The result of the current sentiment failing to beat time and placebo is the starting point for the next research.If WRRA is a complex system analysis tool, its value is more evident in revealing these measurement boundaries and freezing resumption conditions than in successful fitting.

Part 12 Extending WRRA to Naturally Complex Systems: Frontiers in Meteorology and Forecasting

# Chapter 80 WRRA-Met 0.1–0.5: Forward verification of tropical cyclone track residuals

Conclusion of this chapter: DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22306663 is a freeze study that used JMA Western Pacific best-track data to test whether present residue, the compressed influence of past trajectories, provides independent information for tropical cyclone track prediction.Although causal residues were recovered in the synthetic system, the weak linear signal of the real data was less unique than the raw finite history and disappeared in the strong nonlinear current state baseline.The final decision is TRACK-ONLY RESIDUE NOT ESTABLISHED / ENVIRONMENTAL STATE REQUIRED.

## 70.1 Interpretation and prediction are not the same task

When interpreting already formed DNA or material structures, the information to be analyzed currently exists in the object.On the other hand, the next location of a tropical cyclone depends on the upcoming steering flow, pressure field, vertical wind shear, moisture, sea surface temperature, ocean heat content, cold wake, and ground interaction.Therefore, the ability to read the meaning of the present structure and the ability to guess the next present that has not yet been closed cannot be treated as the same performance standard.

Ynow=G(Xnow,Rnow), Xnext=F(Xnow,Rnow,Enow,ξnow)

Xnow is the accessible current state, Rnow is the compressed residue remaining in the present, Enow is the environmental state, and ξnow is the input that the model and observation cannot resolve.This test intentionally excluded Enow.Therefore, failure is not a general absence of memory, but a determination that the next state cannot be closed based on the path history alone.

## 70.2 Data, state, causal residue

The year 2026, which is ongoing in the 1951–2026 JMA RSMC Tokyo best-track archive, was excluded from the evaluation.We constructed 154,144 samples for 6, 12, 24, 48, and 72-hour forecasts from 1,823 tropical cyclones in 1,900 sections with exactly 6-hour intervals and a length of 14 or more.The study was divided forward into 2014, verification from 2015 to 2019, and final exam from 2020 to 2025, and the tropical cyclone section did not exceed the division.

In the current state Iₜ, latitude, circular longitude, longitude/latitude movement and acceleration, central pressure, maximum wind intensity, intensity change, and seasonal terms were included.The residue of movement, acceleration, pressure, and wind intensity change qₜ was updated with an exponential moving average without future values.

Rₜ⁽τ⁾=(1−ατ)Rₜ₋₁⁽τ⁾+ατqₜ, ατ=1−exp(−1/τ), τ∈{2,4,8}

The default τ corresponds to 12, 24, and 48 hours.This definition creates a finite state that is updated in the present without preserving a list of past points in separate coordinates.However, the mere fact that it is a computable amount of compression does not make it an independent physical state variable.

## 70.3 Comparison model and failure-first verification

We compared persistence P, linear current state M1, H1 with raw 6, 12, 24, and 48-hour history, and W2 with EWMA residue.Using W2-S, which mixed residues within the year, and W2-L, which used residues 72 hours ago, as placebo, the current state N1·raw history N2·residue N3 of nonlinear Extra Trees was tested on the same target and split.

The tests were strengthened in the following order: 0.1 synthetic identifiability, 0.2 fixed future period, 0.3 four-epoch forward recall, 0.4 nonlinear baseline, and 0.5 decay time sensitivity.Uncertainty was calculated using cluster bootstrap 2,000 times per tropical cyclone section, without counting time point rows as independent samples.

## 70.4 synthetic system passed the detector

In the 15 synthetic cells where residue was the actual source of production, 15/15 were positive and the average improvement was 10.744% (range 6.966–16.766%).The average for production systems not caused by residues was −0.021% (range −0.127–0.044%), effectively 0, and only 4/15 were positive numbers.Afterwards, the negative results of the real data cannot be explained as being caused by the detector itself not being able to find the residue.

## 70.5 Weak linear signal in real data

In the 2020–2025 fixed trials, linear W2 was only slightly better than M1 at 12, 24, and 48 hours and lagged behind at 6 and 72 hours.The 95% section of the weighted improvement in the tropical cyclone section included 0 for all five times.raw-history H1 was significantly better than W2 at 6·12 hours, and direct comparison between W2 and shuffled residue was also not significant.

In the four forward eras (2000–2005, 2006–2011, 2012–2017, and 2018–2025), W2 beat M1 in 18 of 20 squares, with a median improvement of 0.460%.However, W2 beat H1 only 11/20 times.This is compatible with the repetition of weak linear history signals, but does not support the inherent superiority of residue compression.

## 70.6 Critical boundary created by non-linear baseline

The nonlinear current state N1 produced approximately 1.98–5.66% lower error over time than the linear M1.N3, which added residue, beat N1 at 0/5 hours, the weighted improvement in the tropical cyclone section was all negative, and at 6, 24, and 48 hours, all 95% sections were below 0.N3 couldn't even beat raw-history N2 at 0/5 time.

Therefore, it is most conservative to view the small W2 gain seen in the constrained linear model as a replacement for the nonlinear structure of the current state missed by the linear equation, rather than as a new independent residue state.In the four attenuation series of 6/12/24, 12/24/48, 18/36/72, and 24/48/96 hours, the significant positive result was 0/20.

## 70.7 Ownership of failure and an open future

The trajectory of a tropical cyclone center is not a closed system.If lingering traces of real dynamics exist, their owner is more likely to be the coupled atmosphere-ocean environment field than the compressed record of the centerline.This is not an established fact, but an OPEN HYPOTHESIS to be tested in WRRA-Met 0.6.

H(Xfuture\|Xnowobserved)\>0 ⇏ Ontological indeterminism

The fact that we have not been able to retrieve a single future path from the limited observational present shows the non-uniqueness of the prediction, but it is not metaphysical proof that the universe is inherently indeterministic.Predictability can be increased if sufficient missing initial conditions and environmental fields are provided.What WRRA must distinguish is the existence of a law, the completeness of information in the observed present, and the predictability of future states.

## 70.8 WRRA-Met 0.6 Resumption Conditions

To resume, it is necessary to time align the multilayer wind, altitude, sea level pressure, moisture, and SST/OHC of ERA5 or equivalent data, and predefine local, remote, and multiple τ residues using only the environmental field to date.Nonlinear models of the same input, raw time series, current environmental fields, and possible operating guidelines should be used as strong baselines, and time lag, cross-storm permutation, spatial rotation, and unrelated remote fields should be used as placebo.

Forward division of tropical cyclone units, probability distribution and calibration, one-shot external verification of independent basin or follow-up season are performed, and the grade is raised only when cluster CI\>0 in multiple pre-registered leads simultaneously beats the raw history and non-linear baseline.Until then, track-only hyperparameter tuning is suspended.

The final grade is ‘0.1 synthetic causal residue identification EMPIRICAL PASS / reality linear history signal SUPPORTED BUT NOT ESTABLISHED / residue uniqueness compared to raw history NOT ESTABLISHED / residue REJECTED HERE compared to non-linear current state / improvement of tropical cyclone path prediction NOT ESTABLISHED / environmental field residue and ontological indeterminism OPEN’.

Chapter Conclusions The achievement of WRRA-Met 0.1–0.5 is not in creating a new tropical cyclone forecaster.The goal is to separate interpretable current information from information needed for future transfer, lower claims when weak gains disappear from stronger baselines, and move the research boundary from failed variables to the next information owner candidate.As a complex system analysis tool, WRRA should not be a model that predicts the future, but rather a methodology that audits the ownership of information that actually remains in the present and the conditions under which the next state is closed.

Part 13 WRRA is an interpreter: The boundary between information closure and prediction

# Chapter 81 Integrated format of interpretation, prediction, and quantization boundaries

Conclusion of this chapter: DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22330256 and 10.5281/zenodo.22331940 define WRRA as an interpreter of information closure and record formation, and DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22447864 defines compatible-state diameter, Bayes risk, computational complexity, critical crossing, dynamics amplification, conditional uncertainty andIt was expanded into an operational boundary classifier that bundles record quality.WRRA is not a device that predicts the entire future, but a methodology that determines the form of answer allowed by current information and the conditions for its failure.

## 71.1 Mathematical boundaries between interpretation and prediction

Set the current observation information as ℱₜobs=σ(Xₜ,Rₜ,Eₜ,Recordsₜ).It is an accurate interpretation if the conditional law of target Y focuses on one value G(ℱₜobs) determined by the current information.The fact that computation times are long or that there are no known algorithms does not mean that the information is open.

P(Y∈A\|ℱₜobs)=1A(G(ℱₜobs)) a.s.⇔ P(Y∈·\|ℱₜobs)=δG(ℱₜobs) a.s.

If the conditional law does not degenerate, prediction begins.In discrete output, H(Y\|ℱₜobs)\>0 can be used, and in continuous output, the diameter of the pre-registered trust set, Dₜ(ε)\>0, can be used rather than the differential entropy that depends on coordinates.In actual measurements, effective macro determinism is determined by whether the optimal conditional risk R\*(ℱₜ) is below the tolerance.This is not a declaration of fundamental determinism of the entire universe.

## 71.2 Integrated internal structure of WRRA 2.0

The integrated state ledger is 𝒮ₜ=(SOURCE,Γₜ,ρₜ,Xₜ,Rₜ,Eₜ,Recordsₜ,Φₜ).These terms are not a single physical vector with the same unit, but a bundle of structures from different layers.SOURCE is responsible for upstream possibilities and constraints, Γlaw is support allowed by law, ρ and quantum channel are responsible for changes in quantum state, instrument is responsible for event probability and condition state, and Records and Φ are responsible for distinguishable and continuous expression records.

Γₜ=Γlaw(SOURCE,Xₜ,Rₜ,Eₜ), ρₜ₊₁⁻=ℰₜ(ρₜ;Γₜ,Xₜ,Eₜ)

p(yₜ\|ℱₜ)=Tr\[𝓘yₜ(ρₜ₊₁⁻)\], ρₜ₊₁\|y=𝓘y(ρₜ₊₁⁻)/p(y\|ℱₜ)

Xₜ₊₁=F (

Φₜ₊₁=QΦ(Xₜ₊₁,Recordsₜ₊₁;ε,τ\*,δ), Rₜ₊₁=M(Rₜ,

Standard quantum mechanics possesses states, born weights, instruments and decoherence.Assuming that there is no predetermined event register, the present residue ledger and phenotype record boundary are WRRA's interpretive suggestions.For the latter to become a physical claim that goes beyond the standard theory, it must make independent forward predictions and pass experiments.

## 71.3 Correction of the intuition of ‘second to third order’

The initial intuition that ‘it is analysis up to the 2nd order and prediction from the 3rd order’ is wrong if you read it as the theorem of polynomial degree.There are radical solutions for the 3rd and 4th order general equations, and lower order dynamics can also be opened due to external input.The Abel–Ruffini theorem is a proposition that general 5th degree and higher cannot be solved using radicals alone, but does not mean that there are no roots or that they cannot be calculated.

The place where intuition comes alive is in the closure of relationships.If Y=G(X,R) is closed with only the current target X and the implemented residue R, it is an interpretation.A single future is opened when the next state requires an environment/event term J that is not in the current information and Xnext=F(X,R,J), J∉ℱₜobs.Here, the ‘third term’ is not the number of variables or the order of the equation, but the role of the context that has not yet been entered.

Rational numbers and irrational numbers are also not boundaries.1/3 is an infinitely repeating decimal, but it is finitely specified as two integers, and √2·π·e is calculated to the desired precision with finite rules even if it has infinite non-repeating expansion.The key criteria are finite generation rules, computational complexity, numerical conditions, initial state sensitivity, and current information closure.

## 71.4 Four problem areas and legitimate output

In domain I finite information restoration, the answer is fixed in the finite input.Although integer prime factorization and base sequence reading may be difficult, it is not a prediction of future events, and accurate values ​​and verification certificates are the output.In domain II infinite structure closure, infinite static structures defined such as the zeta function and the Riemann hypothesis must be compressed into a finite proof.Verification up to a finite height is not global proof.

In domain III micro-macro amplification, small observation errors are magnified by Lyapunov growth even if the laws and ideal initial conditions are closed.The legitimate outputs of the three-body problem are finite-time trajectories and predictable horizons.Here, microscopic refers not to quantum size but to classical state differences below observation resolution.

In Area IV information-open transition, the next environment or event is not available in current information or has not yet been realized.Cell fate, tropical cyclone tracks, and economic changes all encompass this area to varying degrees.A legitimate output is not a single number, but a conditional distribution and scenario for which calibration, sharpness, and tail risk have been verified.A real problem may have more than one domain.

## 71.5 quantization threshold and accumulation of 0/1 records

Small differences do not always differentiate between 0 and 1.In the critical map bθ(x)=1{x≥θ}, a perturbation δ changes history only when x and x+δ are on different sides of θ.How much probability mass is concentrated in the critical corridor must first be measured.

bθ(x+δ)≠bθ(x) ⇔ (x−θ)(x+δ−θ)\<0

P\[bθ(X+δ)≠bθ(X)\]≤P(\|X−θ\|≤ε)=2fX(θ)ε+o(ε)

At several stages, the Jacobian product amplifies the difference as δThe union bound is only an upper limit and does not prove that the branching probability necessarily goes to 1.Correlation, saturation, and nonlinear feedbacks must be modeled separately.

## 71.6 From quantum events to phenotypic records

The unitary density operator evolution is deterministic, but the recorded results follow the Born distribution.The quantum instrument separates the event conditioned on the average channel and the outcome y.Decoherence due to environmental entanglement suppresses local interference of the pointer basis, but does not by itself lead to an ontological real result.

Dtr(ρ₀,ρ₁)=½\|\|ρ₀−ρ₁\|\|₁, Perr,opt=(1−Dtr)/2

qΦ,loc is a channel-specific minimal event equivalent that first satisfies Dtr≥1−2ε and τrec≥τ\*.qΦ,obj here requires the redundancy Rδ of the independent environmental record.This is neither a particle, nor a ħ, nor a universal minimum energy, and if the resource is multidimensional or non-monotonic, it must be defined as a Pareto minimum in the pre-registered order.

Therefore, the macro-micro boundary is not determined by one size.Sufficiently isolated macroscopic degrees of freedom can sustain interference, and even a single microevent can encounter strong amplification and immediately become a macrorecord.In this book, macro-determinacy refers to the valid historical fixation of records with distinguishability, continuity, and environmental redundancy.

## 71.7 Reading math, life, weather, and economics as a single standard

Factorization and multiplication certificates are required for prime factorization, global theorem, proof, and counterexamples are required for the Riemann hypothesis, finite time paths and horizons are required for the three-body problem, ensembles and conditional scenarios are required for weather and economics, and separation of current function analysis and future phenotype distribution is required for DNA and cells.The question and the information set, not the field, determine the output.

The Riemann hypothesis is not a prediction of the future, but an infinite structure closure.DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22346428 makes this distinction even stricter.The record of not finding a counterexample in finite height, finite sample, finite jet, and finite precision is only an analysis of the scope, and does not certify the absence of an infinite tail that has not yet been opened.If the long-distance Gram remainder attenuation of the preceding Wonsik–Jeongin filter is established under strict assumptions, it can be a partial result of closing the far tail budget, but the finite-to-mesoscopic corridor, in which the diagonal growth and negative off-diagonal terms of close scales are uniformly canceled out, requires a separate global theorem.This does not mean the absolute unprovability of RH or the independence of ZFC.

Previous audits of biology, neurology, meteorology, and economics showed the same conclusion.Already formed sequences, cell states, connectomes, current analysis fields, and economic indicators can be interpreted, but single-value contracts become inadequate in the future when new environments, interactions, policies, and collective judgments enter.New observations and interventions replace some uncertainties with current information, so boundaries move with information and experimentation rather than fixed size lines.

## 71.8 WRRA Application Procedure Preserving Failure

The order of application is ① fix the goal whether it is current structure analysis or next state prediction, ② create a time and source ledger ofPre-register.

When the residue effect disappears after a strong non-linear current state contrast with the raw history, ownership is transferred to the environment.Even under the hypothesis that the current information closes the next state, if the conditional width is stably greater than 0, point prediction is stopped and the distribution is output.If an argument for an infinite structure relies only on finite range calculations or presupposes an offset equivalent to the conclusion, global proof is not claimed.

To claim WRRA's unique physical effects, quantum instrument, open system dynamics, detector/thermal noise, standard non-Markovian memory, and state uncertainty must first be frozen.It is not enough that two preparations with different pasts look the same at one point in time; Δhistory≠0 must be reproduced even after multitemporal predictive equivalence at the process-tensor level.

## 71.9 Task-relative boundary classifier

Declare the task T=(Iₜ,Q,tQ,ε,B,L) and create a state set Ω(Iₜ)={ω:ω compatible with the declared record, constraint, and error} that is compatible with the current information.The target diameter DQ(Iₜ)=diam{Q(ω):ω∈Ω(Iₜ)} and the dimensionless diameter dQ=DQ/sQ measure whether the current information effectively fixes Q within the tolerance.Ω is not an ontological list of possible physical universes, but an epistemological compatible set within a chosen model family.

Restorability alone is not enough.We also see whether R\*(Q\|Iₜ)=infₐE\[L(a,Q)\|Iₜ\] is below the standard for each objective, whether C_Q(ε) is within budget B, how much a small information perturbation moves the objective, what the threshold-crossing probability and Lyapunov·Jacobian amplification are, and whether there remains any conditional uncertainty of Q that has not yet been recorded.
