Five contracts are selected from the boundary vector BQ(Iₜ)=(dQ,R\*,CQ,AQ,HQ,qΦ).Ⅰ Interpretation outputs stably restored values ​​and uncertainty, Ⅱ Calculation outputs algorithms, complexity, and approximation that obtain a fixed answer, Ⅲ Prediction outputs distribution, interval, and ensemble, Ⅳ Proof Closure outputs finite proof, counterexample, and conditional theorem, and Ⅴ Pending outputs non-closure declaration and request for additional information.Classification is not mandatory unless goals, information, errors, budget, and losses are declared.

## 71.10 Record quality and pre- and post-incident reclassification

The recording quality is set as qΦ=wD Drec+wP Prec+wR Rred, and the weight sum is 1.Drec denotes the distinguishability of alternative outcomes, Prec denotes persistence during the required time, and Rred denotes the redundancy between independent channels or pieces of the environment.qΦ and threshold q\* are not universal constants or particle sizes, but are task-relative measures that are calibrated before viewing the results according to the field and device.

Before recording, a valid description of an event may be a non-degenerate conditional distribution.When the result, through amplification and critical crossing, becomes a distinct, continuous, and overlapping record, the task of reading the record changes into interpretation.This framework does not claim that decoherence automatically proves a single-outcome ontology, and it does not combine quantum probability, classical chaos, observation noise, and model error into a single cause.

## 71.11 Pre-registration, verification, and prevention of misclassification

Analysis verifies reconstruction errors and recording stability between observers, devices, and repeated measurements.Forecasting compares calibration, coverage, sharpness, and strictly proper score with the standard model in unused future data, new regions, and new cohorts.The nominal 90% prediction interval should show approximately 90% coverage in the declared verification environment, and the results of widening the interval after the fact are not counted as forward predictions.

Discard the shortcuts of ‘if it is quantum, always predict’, ‘if it is deterministic, always interpret’, ‘if it is complex, predict’, and ‘if it is infinite, predict’.Recorded quantum results are interpretable, deterministic chaos requires distributional predictions behind a finite precision horizon, fixed hard answers are computational problems, and infinite static propositions such as the Riemann hypothesis are proof-closure problems.The classification unit is not the name of the object, but the information-target contract.

The final grade is ‘Conditional Dirac boundary MATHEMATICAL DEFINITION / Four-area classification PROPOSED METHODOLOGICAL FRAMEWORK / Polynomial order·rationality·magnitude boundary REJECTED / Born instrument·trace distance·decoherence ESTABLISHED STANDARD PHYSICS / No Predetermined Event Register WRRA INTERPRETIVE ASSUMPTION / qΦ CHANNEL-SPECIFIC OPERATIONAL DEFINITION/ Residues that exceed standard memory and unique selection dynamics are UNTESTED.

Chapter Conclusion The way to expand WRRA is not to match all futures with one operator.It is to distinguish between information already implemented in the present, infinite structures that require global proof, closed dynamics where errors are amplified, and environments and events that have not yet entered, and select the appropriate answer format for each.WRRA is a boundary interpreter that reads the current ledger and marks where interpretation ends and prediction opens.

# Chapter 82 The Riemann hypothesis cannot be verified through finite search

Conclusion of this chapter: DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22346428 does not assert the unprovability of the Riemann hypothesis itself.The correct conclusion is narrower and stronger.The mere fact that no counterexamples were found in finite height, finite samples, finite order derivatives, finite precision, and fixed cutoff does not certify that there are no counterexamples in the entire infinite critical band.A single counterexample can be a finite witness, but a non-counterexample in an infinite domain cannot be closed by search alone without a separate global invariant.

## 72.1 Correct scope of the problem and finite search environment

For a non-obvious zero point ρ, RH is a global proposition that Re(ρ)=1/2 if ξ(ρ)=0.If the existence of a zero point outside the critical line is recorded as bₖ for each height window Iₖ=\[Tₖ,Tₖ+H\], RH becomes an infinite conjunction of bₖ=0 for all k.

bₖ=1{∃ρ: ξ(ρ)=0, Re(ρ)≠1/2, Im(ρ)∈Iₖ};RH ⇔ bₖ=0 for all k

Here, finite search is a procedure that ① directly investigates only a finite height or compact area, ② queries only function values and derivatives of a finite order at a finite number of points, ③ expresses the value with finite precision or finite interval inclusion, and ④ declares a global no-counterexample simply because there was no counterexample within the search range.Analytical proofs that compress an infinite domain into finite logic with functional equations, Euler products, positivity, and spectral invariants are excluded from this definition.

FINITE SEARCH = finite window + finite queries + finite resolution + no independent global invariant

After any finite K, N\\1,…,K} still has the same countable cardinality as N.This does not mean that K+1 is infinite, but rather that the infinity of the unexamined tail does not disappear no matter how much the finite prefix is ​​increased.

## 72.2 Completeness of finite fields and failure of fixed cutoffs

Let Πₚ and Qₚ=I−Πₚ, which remove multiples of prime p, be written as S_cut(z)=product\_{p\<=z}(I−Πₚ).In z\<n≤z², passing S_cut(z) is equivalent to n being a prime number.The composite number n is eliminated because it has prime factors less than or equal to √n≤z, and a prime number with n\>z is not divisible by any p≤z.

z\<n\<=z^2: S_cut(z;n) survives iff n is prime

However, if we take different prime numbers such that p,q\>z for every finite z, then m=pq is a composite number and passes through a fixed S≤z.This does not mean that decimal determination of individual input n is impossible.If z≥√n is selected for each n, the decision can be made in finite time.What fails is the claim to permanently close all natural numbers with a single fixed cutoff.

For every finite z, there exist primes p,q\>z such that m=pq is composite and S_cut(z;m) survives.

## 72.3 Zero insertion theorem preserving finite jets

Let us assume that the general entire function f is accurately observed up to the rⱼ degree at each finite point zⱼ.Select one unobserved point w and create the following P and g.

P(z)=product_j (z−z_j)^(r_j+1), g(z)=f(z)−f(w)P(z)/P(w)

Since P disappears to rⱼ+1 order or more at each zⱼ, g^(k)(zⱼ)=f^(k)(zⱼ) for 0≤k≤rⱼ, and at the same time, g(w)=0.Therefore, global zero-freeness of unobserved regions cannot be certified with only finitely accurate jet transcripts.

This theorem is an information-theoretic no-go for general entire-function classes.This does not mean that the constructed g preserves the Euler product of ξ, the exact growth rate, the arithmetic coefficients, and the functional equations.Therefore, the possibility of a global structure theorem specific to ξ is not excluded.

## 72.4 Boundary between finite precision and critical line identity

If the zero point is written as ρ=1/2+δ+iγ, the numerical judgment of RH asks whether δ is exactly 0.If quantization Qε of resolution ε binds \|δ\|≤ε to the same output, δ=0 and 0\<\|δ\|\<ε are not distinguished.

Qε(δ)=0 if \|δ\|≤ε, 1 if \|δ\|\>ε;Qε(0)=Qε(δ) for 0\<\|δ\|\<ε

We can continue to reduce ε, but at every finite step we are left with a smaller non-zero δ.This is not an assumption that physical space is quantized, but a deterministic limitation of finite precision representation.The observation that it is ‘numerically very close to 1/2’ is not a substitute for exact identity.

## 72.5 Local detector and Jensen closure

The 2×2 mixed-corner difference telescopes along the cell boundary but can give zeros at symmetrically placed internal zeros.The 3×3 nine-point Laplacian can be used as a compact domain recursive detector by amplifying the isolated zero signal faster than the harmonic background, but no fixed finite stencil can exceed the finite sample limit of 72.3.

When ξ(c)≠0 and there is no zero point in the boundary circle, the Jensen circle average defect is set as follows.

Jₕ(c)=(1/2π)∫₀²π log\|ξ(c+h e^(iθ))\|²dθ−log\|ξ(c)\|² = 2Σ\|ρ−c\|\<h mρ log(h/\|ρ−c\|)≥0

If there is no zero point in the open disk, Jₕ(c)=0, and if there is a zero point, it is a positive number.If \|ρ−c\|≤h/√2 from grid spacing h to the nearest center, Jₕ(c)≥log 2 at a sufficiently small admissible scale.If the disk is restricted to Re(c)−h\>1/2 or Re(c)+h\<1/2, it becomes an off-line detector excluding the critical line zero.This does not mean that the entire infinite height is completed at a finite time, although it is complete in each finite disk.

## 72.6 The global zero-point mass integral is a definition, not yet an algorithm.

If u(s)=log\|ξ(s)\|², then Δu(s)=4πΣρ mρδρ in the distribution sense.We can define the following global quantity for the right semicritical band Ω+={1/2\<Re(s)\<1} and the admissible weight w, which is positive at all heights while decaying fast enough for the weighted sum to the zero density to converge.

E+(w)=(1/4π)∫Ω+ w(Im s)Δlog\|ξ(s)\|²dA(s)=ΣRe(ρ)\>1/2 mρw(Im ρ)

If the admissible w, where the weighted sum and integral converge, is strictly positive at all heights, then RH ⇔ E+(w)=0.Although this equation is a complete definition that includes all infinite heights, an independent rule for calculating its exact value as 0 by a finite procedure is not yet given.The fact that infinite information is written in one line using an integrator symbol is different from the fact that infinite information is compressed into finite calculations.

## 72.7 Prime–Zero N×M Relationship Ledger

If the relationship between ρⱼ=1/2+δⱼ+iγⱼ and the natural number n is written as Aⱼn=n^(−ρⱼ), the radial factor of all rows above the critical line is 1, and the rows outside the critical line have a contraction or expansion of n^(−δⱼ).

Aⱼn=n^(−1/2)e^(−δⱼlog n)e^(−iγⱼlog n), Rⱼn=\|Aⱼn\|/n^(−1/2)=n^(−δⱼ)

The direct comparison ledger has size M×N for M zero points and N arithmetic modes.However, this should not be claimed as the lower bound on the computational complexity of all algorithms.The multiplicative and analytical transformation of Aⱼ,mn=AⱼmAⱼn can compress the composite number sequence.The correct conclusion is that a fixed, finite-dimensional sample ledger cannot terminate the possibility of infinite new zero rows just by searching.

## 72.8 Main theorem: Non-authentication within defined search classes

Theorem 72.1 — PROVED WITHIN THE DEFINED CLASS.A search procedure that only uses a finite height, a finite number of accurate jets or a finite precision enclosure, and the fact that there are no counterexamples in the observation range cannot logically and completely verify the truth of RH.

The three steps of proof are clear.First, if the procedure terminates in finite time, regions higher than any T will not be investigated.Second, if the set of queried points and differential orders is finite, g of 72.3 has both the same transcript and an out-of-observation zero point.Third, the finite precision is weaker than the exact jet and has an additional critical line identity limit of 72.4.Therefore, in order to make a global conclusion, a global invariant law independent of the search data must be introduced, and at that moment the procedure goes outside the defined search-only class.

This theorem does not state that RH is independent of ZFC, and does not exclude finite analytic proof using the entire structure of ξ.Like Euclid's proof of prime infinity, finite sentences can compress infinite objects.What is frozen is the path of ‘completing the global proof by endlessly increasing the finite list of null examples.’

## 72.9 The border between analysis and prediction

Calculate finite K windows and b₁=…It is the analyzed present that obtains =bK=0.The moment you declare bₖ=0 even for all k\>K that are not opened, it becomes a prediction, not a proof.

finite analysis: b₁=…=bK=0;unopened-tail prediction: bₖ=0 for every k\>K

The way to turn this back into a proof is not to visit all windows, but to show that the finite state S_k and the transition T preserve the safe set A: S_0 in A, T(A) subset A, S_k in A =\> b_k=0.WJNS's research on finite-window transport, boundary carry, and no-burial was an attempt to find such a transition invariant, but safe set invariance has not yet been proven for the actual arithmetic kernel of ξ.

## 72.10 Calculation man-hours and minimal-computation principle

According to the Riemann–von Mangoldt law, up to a height T the number of non-obvious zeros increases with the leading order T log T.

M(T)≈(T/2π)log(T/2π)−T/2π;Cdirect(T)∝N(T)M(T);Cgrid(T,h)∝T/h²

When compared directly with N(T) modes per zero, the book cost is N(T)M(T), and a planar grid with spacing h requires approximately T/h² cells.If h(T) → 0 is reduced along with T, the direct search effort increases more rapidly.This is not a universal complexity lower bound for all possible algorithms, but a scale audit of the direct search, grid, and relational ledger models defined in this chapter.

The methodological interpretation of Minimal Computing Cosmology is that instead of exhausting an infinite ledger item by item, reality should be realized with finite generators and preserved transition invariants.Therefore, the brute-force path that expands to T log T, N(T)M(T), and T/h(T)² as the scale increases does not match the minimal-computation principle.However, the principle that ‘nature should be simple’ alone does not prove the independence or impossibility of RH.

## 72.11 Final freeze decision

FROZEN THEOREM.No matter how large the finite environment is made, the fact that zero points were not detected within it does not certify that there are no zero points outside the critical line in the entire infinite region.Increasing the size only moves the border, it does not remove the border.

The frozen path is a search-only attack that attempts to complete RH with a higher cutoff, tighter grid, larger stencil, and longer numerical calculation.The remaining path is the functional equation of ξ, the Euler product, and the positiveness or independent global invariants that compress the infinite height into a finite law in the spectral structure.

In the chapter conclusion, the correct final sentence is not ‘RH is unprovable’ but ‘the truth of RH cannot be proven just by completing a finite search.’Unless a separate analytical theorem compresses the infinite zero problem into finite structural laws, the search only increases the analyzed past and does not eliminate predictions of the unexamined infinite tail.

The two methodology papers group the previous application results into one standard.Where WRRA is strongest is not when it fixes all futures as point values, but when it distinguishes whether current information closes a single value, whether infinite structures require global proofs, whether microscopic errors are amplified by large records, or whether the environment and new events are still open.Therefore, the statement ‘WRRA is an interpreter’ does not mean giving up on prediction, but rather rejecting prediction contracts that do not fit the problem and choosing a legitimate output format.

If different levels such as space, life, economy, and weather can all be explained by a universal methodology called minimal computation, this goes beyond a simple analogy between fields and suggests the possibility that the principle of the universe itself is based on minimal computation.However, this is not a currently proven conclusion.The final research proposition of this book is to test not whether the common grammar produces the same single prediction in each field, but whether it correctly classifies information ownership and closed areas and maintains different output contracts of value, proof, trajectory, and distribution under a strong baseline and independent data.

Our universe may be one of several possible universes.However, the history we actually live in is one.Even if the laws are fixed, future events are not stored as complete data.Fortunately, we may be able to decide our own destiny.

# Chapter 94 WRRA Go 0.1: From proven transition to actual launch

Instead of enumerating the 0.2 legality, transition, and residue structures in 9×9, WRRA Game 1.0 explicitly calculated the 11 renderer channels for each legal number and let MCTS compare candidates within a limited simulation budget.Search is not a prediction that observes the future, but an operation that creates and compares successors allowed by the current rules.

In controlled 5×5 color alternating matches, WRRA Go recorded 15 wins and 1 loss in 16 countries against the uniform MCTS control group and 16 wins in 16 countries against the random control group.The 9×9 self-match ended with two consecutive passes in 82 moves, and White won by a margin of 8.5.Code, JSON ledger, SGF notation, deterministic rule checking, and SHA-256 manifest were preserved together in the reproduction package.

This figure does not prove the power of a professional Go engine or prediction of the future in the real world.This is the first implementation test that shows that the transparent renderer is connected to actual action selection and can be compared with the control algorithm under the same rules, board size, simulation number, and scoring method.

Assertion grade EXACT (rule checking, state transition, recorded code output), CONDITIONAL/disciplined search (15–1, 16–0 in controlled game), STRUCTURAL (minimal sufficient state → Renderer → finite search → Ledger chain), OPEN (professional strength, large-scale generalization, external benchmark).

# Chapter 96 Conditional boundary calculation of causal horizon and finite universe

The causal horizon study, published on September 24, 2026, did not view the event horizon as a material wall or internal time still surface, but formalized it as a causal port boundary beyond which certain external renderers cannot directly record internal differences.Assuming causal locality and external observable constraints, the external direct channel capacity of internal operations before the arrival of permitted carriers is zero.This does not mean that entanglement, conservation changes, Hawking radiation, or even total information are zero.

Direct internal → external channel capacity = 0 ≠ correlation 0 ≠ total information 0

A flat Lambda CDM reproduction with Planck 2018 centroids gives the current covariant radius of the observable universe at 46.13 Gly and the cosmological event horizon at 16.68 Gly.The two values ​​are the computational output of the selected cosmological model and are not identical to the topological size of the entire universe.If the dark energy model changes, the event horizon and length ratio may also change or become undefined.

The indiscernibility that the minimum spatial unit cannot be uniquely determined with only two macroscopic lengths is EXACT.If we adopt the additional correction of 1 bit per horizon area, we get an effective boundary spacing of 2.69It is not promoted to the absolute unique minimum length or absolute floor number of the universe.

The dark section has independent quantum gravity B_Q, B_E derived from mass phenotype, continuous geometry B_C, and scale crossing or dual technology B_X as parallel hypotheses.Neither branch is accepted as the correct answer, and if the same dark effect is added twice in one calculation, the calculation is invalid.

Claim class WRRA-EXACT (no signal and minimum unit non-identifiable under causal assumption), ADOPTED CALCULATION (46.13 Gly and 16.68 Gly), CONDITIONAL CALIBRATION (2.69×10⁻³⁵ m and 139 layers), OPEN (size of the entire universe, elimination of singularities, full quantum gravity).Research genealogy is linked to DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22931512.

WRRA Light 0.1 does not equate common carriers with photons.The common carrier is the local causal transport basis shared by multiple channels, and light is the two massless transversely polarized modes allowed in the U(1) electromagnetic channel above it.In the final continuous Maxwell profile, both polarizations and all frequencies share a single cone and internal propagation ratio in the local inertial frame of a homogeneous vacuum.

Ω = χγ \|K\|, vph = vg = χγ

299,792,458 m/s is not a new value derived from internal calculations, but a definition constant established in the SI unit map.After the correction, the calculated value through Maxwell, quantum rules, and general relativity input is the output of the final WRRA Light profile.The reproduction of already known values ​​is a matter of consistency and explanation, and new SI numbers are not a prerequisite for establishing a theory.

The installed quantum law and renderer calculated Mach Zehnder single-photon interference, Hong Ou Mandel co-detection 0, CHSH 2√2=2.828427 of polarized singlet, and signal-free peripheral distribution.Free light and ideal quantum optics have been implemented, but Born's rule's core internal microsources, single result selection, transverse QED vertices, renormalization, dispersion and absorption in real media, and strong QED are only partially implemented or OPEN.

# Chapter 99 Dual-component model of mass gravity and spatial distortion stress

On September 27, 2026, v0.4 decomposed the observed gravity of the weak field into the direct component gM of ordinary matter and the stress component gT stored in the distortion and deformation of space.It is not a declaration of adding two fundamental forces, but a calculation ledger that distinguishes between direct material source and spatial response.In covariant completion, only one of the effective stress source method and the corrected response method must be selected to prevent double calculation.

g = gM + gT

The closed equation aT=cH0√(fT/8) with the current space input c, H0=67.4 km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹, and fT=0.265 gives aT=1.191812669×10⁻¹⁰ m s⁻².The coefficient 1/8 is not an independent prediction because it is a value corrected for a known low acceleration scale.The difference from the diagnostic fit value of 201 SPARC deep low acceleration points of 1.20073×10⁻¹⁰ m s⁻² is about 0.75%.

The average residual of 201 points for the fixed deep low acceleration equation was +0.0103 dex, and the RMS was 0.1683 dex.The RMS 0.1329 dex obtained by applying the external empirical transfer function to a total of 2,693 points is a support test and is not yet the result of the inherent transfer function derived from the WRRA action.The universe average equivalent source ratio gT:gM=5.37425:1 and 84.31% vs. 15.69% calculated with the same observed component ratio are also normalized calculations by transferring input information to the WRRA ledger, not independent predictions.

The current judgment passes the first round of reality consistency.The argument that warp stress is the cause of the actual dark mass is strengthened when we pass through galactic rotation curves, lenses, galaxy clusters, solar systems, redshift evolution, CMB·BAO·structural growth together in one fixed response function without recalibration.If the rotation curve is correct but lens or structure growth fails, the causal hypothesis is discarded or modified at the core level.

The new scheduling model reduced 1,103 states and 4,188 action evaluations by 26.2178% to 3,090 and maintained an exact makespan discrepancy of 0.Even with the same remaining work and machine availability time, if the final work group residue was different, the optimal makespan was different to 10 and 11.

# Epilogue The questions never end

I did not start this research to own the universe.There were things I wanted to know for a long time, and now, with AI, I felt like I could follow those questions much further than before.

Along the way, we obtained several accurate formulas, created several possible structures, and learned that quite a few paths were blocked.The model that once seemed like the answer was reopened, incorrect assumptions were erased, and only the surviving parts were passed on to the next document.

Even now, I don't think our universe is unique.The universe we see may be one of many possible universes.What is important is not that it is unique, but that matter, time, and memory are held together here and we can question that fact.

This book is not the final blueprint of the universe.It is a map that asks how possibility is narrowed into reality, how multiple properties are jointly realized as one object, and how that reality can be restored and observed over time.

There are many empty places on the map.The independent prediction of particle mass and coupling, the origin of dynamical three-dimensional space, the perturbation of the early universe, and the reconstruction of the actual energy ledger and gravitational backreaction are still open.Writing an empty space as an empty space is the most important honesty that this research can maintain.

However, I got a more specific answer to one question than before.The fact that the laws are fixed does not mean that the order of events is stored in advance.Even under the same complex state and the same renderer, the recording of reality can be conditional at the moment an event occurs.

Someday, if someone comes across the same question and takes a sentence or an operator from this record and takes it to the next step, this research will come to life again.It's okay if that person doesn't remember my name.

I followed through on the questions I had and saw with my own eyes how far I could answer them.And that path was left in the knowledge warehouse of mankind.

The questions don't end here.However, the book closes by pointing out where the next question should begin.

**Fortunately, we may be able to decide our own destiny.**

Zenodo is a cross-disciplinary open science research repository operated by CERN.DOI is a unique identifier for consistently locating and citing specific published works.However, DOI and repository disclosure do not guarantee the authenticity of the content or that it will pass peer review.In this book, DOI is not used as a certificate of completion, but rather as an anchor point in the disclosure history that tracks who published what, when, and how it was modified.

# Appendix D Glossary of terms for readers new to mathematics and physics

This appendix is not a dictionary that replaces formulas, but a reading guide that helps you not to miss the logic of the text.‘Established concepts’ explain the meanings commonly used in textbooks and academia, and ‘model concepts in this book’ explain the usages suggested in WRRA research.A ‘hypothetical connection’ is an unverified interpretation that connects two layers.A formula compresses the exact relationship, and this commentary explains what the formula is trying to say in everyday language.

# D-1 Classifications to know first

**Established concept** Words that have already been defined as standard in mathematics and physics, such as Bessel function, zeta function, eigenvalue, and Hamiltonian.This book is not a new concept.

**Model concept in this book** Like WRRA, SOURCE-RELATION-BOUNDARY, and selective realization, it is a word proposed in the author's research lineage.It may be useful, but it is not yet an accepted standard theory.

**Hypothetical Connection** This is an interpretation that the established mathematical structure is connected to the actual creation and observation method of the universe.The mere fact that something can be calculated mathematically does not prove its physical reality.

# D-2 Key math terms

## function

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is a correspondence relationship in which an input is input and output is produced according to a set rule.You can think of it like a vending machine button and a drink, but input and output can also be numbers, vectors, or functions themselves.

**In this book** It is the most basic language that describes how the input information in the universe is converted into an output state.

**Note** A function does not necessarily mean that it is a physical device that operates in time.

## Bessel function

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** This is a special function that appears naturally when calculating waves spreading out from the center like a circle or cylinder.If the sine wave of a guitar string is the language of linear vibration, the Bessel function is closer to the language of round membrane, cylinder, and radial waves.

**In this book** It is used to express the time-distance dependent response in WRRA's common carrier and 15-channel model, and to calculate the conditions when the rank opens or the spectrum settles.

**Caution** The mere fact that the Bessel function appears in an equation does not prove that the universe is actually cylindrical or that the carrier in question is real.

## Riemann zeta function

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is initially defined as a function that adds up all of the forms 1/nˢ, and is handled in a wider range of complex numbers through analytical extension.It is also connected to the product of prime numbers, so it compresses the multiplication structure of natural numbers.

**In this book** In this book it is used as a reproducible mathematical seed to create finite mode windows and SOURCE candidates.

**Caution** It has not yet been proven that the zeta function is the only initial state of the universe or that nature necessarily reads the zeta function.

## zero

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** This is an input that makes the function value 0.It is similar to finding the location where the height of a mountain meets sea level.

**In this book** The zero point of the zeta function or the zero point of the determinant marks a candidate point where a specific mode disappears or a new condition is established.

**Caution** Mathematical zero point and annihilation of physical particles are not the same thing.A separate response rule is needed to connect the two.

## Complex numbers and phase

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** Complex numbers express not only magnitude but also phase, which is the angle of rotation.Waves of the same size can be added to or erased from each other if they are in different phases.

**In this book** we describe the arrangement of interference, mixing, lossless angles, and zeta modes between channels.

**Caution** Phase is not a vague everyday word for atmosphere or phase, but mathematically defined angular information.

## operator

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is a rule that changes not a single number but an entire state or function into another state or function.The rules for translating a sentence are similar to those that apply to the entire sentence.

**In this book** WRRA is the basic unit that mathematically expresses the operation of reading SOURCE, updating relationships, and passing boundaries.

**Caution** Writing down operators does not mean that you have discovered the actual natural operating device.

## Eigenvalue·Eigenvector

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** An eigenvector is a special state in which the direction is maintained no matter what transformation it undergoes and only the size changes at a certain ratio, and that ratio is the eigenvalue.

**In this book** Used to find stable modes, allowable energies, and spectral positions.

**Caution** In order for the eigenvalue to be the same as the mass or energy of the observed particle, the units and corresponding equations must be verified.

## spectrum

**Characteristic math and physics concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is a collection of unique values allowed by a system.Just as a prism spreads light into various colors, a complex system is unfolded into possible vibration and energy components.

**In this book**, this is the key output to compare which modes and particle-like representations WRRA allows.

**Caution** Obtaining a spectrum with a similar shape is different from independently predicting the particle value of the Standard Model.

## mode

**Characteristic math and physics concepts**

**Easy meaning** A pattern in which a system can oscillate or change independently.It is similar to the different notes of an instrument.

**In this book** It is used as a calculation unit that constitutes the SOURCE and transfer structure in a finite calculation window such as 6,369 mode.

**Caution** The number of modes does not mean the number of particles or the total amount of information in the universe.

## matrix

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is a tool that calculates relationships and transformations at once by arranging multiple numbers in a rectangle.

**In this book** we treat channel combinations, transitions, mixing, ranks, and eigenvalues ​​as finite calculations.

## Jacobi matrix

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is usually a triple diagonal matrix with values only on the main diagonal and the next diagonal.It succinctly represents a long chain of close neighbors.

**In this book** we transfer continuous spectra into a finite chain model to enable numerical calculations and error checking.

**Caution** Since the finite Jacobian matrix is ​​an approximation of the original infinite/continuous system, truncation error and convergence checks are required.

## Determinant

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** A number that represents how much a matrix expands or distorts the volume of space.If it is 0, which direction is pressed, the information is not released independently.

**In this book** We find rank-opening candidates through the zeros of the 15-channel Bessel determinant.

**Caution** The zero point of the determinant alone does not prove the creation of matter, and further dynamics and observational correspondence are needed.

## Rank and rank opening

**Established linear algebra concepts + Interpretation of this book**

**Easy meaning** Rank counts how many independent information directions there are in a matrix.As the rank increases from low to high, previously bound degrees of freedom can move independently.

**In this book** ‘Rank opening’ refers to an event in which the channel of the common carrier is released to an independent degree of freedom and the conditions for creating matter are opened.

**Caution** It is important to distinguish between the calculation fact of rank increase and the physical analysis of actual Big Bang/particle creation.

## kernel

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** Two meanings are common depending on the context.It is a function that describes the set of states that an operator sends to 0, or the way the influence of the input spreads to the output.

**In this book** In this book, it is mainly used in the second sense of a rule connecting input and output in interaction, transfer, and many-body calculations.

**Caution** You must check the meaning of each sentence, and it is different from the kernel of a computer operating system.

## propagator

**Quantum field theory concept with established characteristics**

**Easy meaning** It is a mathematical function that indicates how the influence of one point or time is transmitted to another point or time.

**In this book** we examine whether the responses of common carrier and electronic interactions are consistent across time and space.

**Caution** A propagator is not the trajectory of a single particle flying along its classical path.

## Green function

**Characteristic math and physics concepts**

**Easy meaning** This is a basic response function that tells you how the system responds to a stimulus applied to a single point.Responses to multiple stimuli can be calculated by overlapping them.

**In this book** It is used to solve propagation problems with linear response and boundary conditions, and is closely related to the propagator.

**Caution** Green's function and propagator are related concepts that may be the same or have different constant and boundary processing depending on the context.

## Hilbert space

**Characteristic Mathematical Concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is a fully equipped space that can define the length and angle of a vector and handle limits stably.You can think of it as a stage containing quantum states.

**In this book** it is used to attempt to treat filters, modes, and states in a strict vector space.

**Caution** Abstract state space is not the same as actual physical space.

## Hamiltonian

**Personality-established physics concepts**

**Easy meaning** It contains the energy structure of the system, and in quantum mechanics, it is an operator that generates how the state changes over time.

**In this book** This is the key when extending the static selection rules of dimensional filters to execution models with time evolution.

**Caution** To call a matrix Hamiltonian, conditions such as self-involvement, unity, stability, and physical state space must be checked.

# D-3 Key physics terms

## field

**Personality-established physics concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is a structure in which physical quantities are located at each point in space and time.Just as a temperature field has a temperature at each location, a quantum field has states and operators at each point.

**In this book**, this is the background for understanding particles not as independent small balls but as local excitations or phenotypes of a field.

## particles

**Personally established physics concept**

**Easy meaning** In modern physics, particles are expressed as local detection events, quantum field excitation, and states with specific mass, charge, and spin depending on the situation.

**In this book** This is a representative example of the observable phenotype that WRRA seeks to produce.

**Caution** The word ‘particle’ does not always mean the classic little bead.

## Quantum state and overlap

**Concept of Quantum Mechanics with Established Character**

**Easy meaning** Quantum states contain possible measurement results and probabilities for a system.Superposition is a method of expressing multiple classical possibilities together as a single state vector rather than simply not knowing them.

**In this book**, this is the starting point for the selective realization problem, where each possible state is represented by a single actual record.

**Caution** There is no need to immediately interpret superposition as meaning that all different universes actually exist.

## Measurements and observations

**Established physics concept + interpretation problem**

**Easy meaning** Measurement is the process of creating a readable record through the interaction of a system, device, and environment.It does not mean only the act of human consciousness looking.

**In this book** WRRA is extended to the hypothesis that physical record queries trigger phenotypic output, i.e. the renderer.

**Caution** The claim that reality exists only through observation or that human consciousness creates the universe is not a necessary conclusion of this book.

## Gauge symmetry

**Personally established physics concept**

**Easy meaning** This is the principle that the same physical state can be expressed in multiple mathematical notations and that the observed value must be the same even when the notation changes.

**In this book** we check that charges and interactions are consistent and that notation choices do not change predictions.

**Caution** Gauge is different from its usual meaning of instrument panel or simple measuring scale.

## Chirality

**Established particle physics concept**

**Easy meaning** This is the property in which two structures reflected in a mirror, such as the left and right hands, do not overlap just by rotation.Weak interactions treat the left and right components differently.

**In this book** This is the key condition that asks how a 15-channel common carrier can allow for a one-handled material structure.

**Caution** Finding geometric asymmetry alone does not complete the exact chiral interaction of the standard model.

## Gauge anomaly

**Quantum field theory concept with established characteristics**

**Easy meaning** This is a phenomenon in which the symmetry of classical theory is broken during quantum calculation.If more than the gauge remains, the probability preservation and consistency of the theory may be lost.

**In this book** we check whether the first generation standard model inventory can offset anomalies in charge combination.

**Caution** Although cancellation is a necessary condition, it is not a sufficient condition that the particle content is uniquely derived from nature.

## Ward–Takahashi identity

**Characteristic established quantum field theory relationship**

**Easy meaning** This is a relational expression that states that the propagator and the interaction vertex must fit each other without contradiction due to gauge symmetry.

**In this book** We check that the transfer equation and vertex preserve the same symmetry in the electronic gauge closure.

**Caution** Even if the identity is closed, the lateral structure or the value of the coupling constant does not automatically become unique.

## Particle Physics Standard Model

**Established physical theory**

**Easy meaning** It is a quantum field theory that very accurately describes quarks, leptons, gauge bosons, Higgs, electromagnetic force, weak force, and strong force.Gravity is not included.

**In this book** This book reads the Standard Model as an ‘expressive grammar’ of observable particles and interactions, and asks whether WRRA can be a candidate for the generative structure underneath it.

**Caution** This does not mean that the Standard Model is just a classification scheme with a name.It is a theory that has succeeded in making numerous precise predictions, and it is not at the stage to say that WRRA has replaced it.

## Higgs field and Higgs particle

**Personally established physics concept**

**Easy meaning** The Higgs field is a field that causes elementary particles to have a mass term in a vacuum where preweak symmetry is broken, and the Higgs particle is the quantum excitation of that field.

**In this book**, it is used as a representative example to distinguish between ‘existence prediction’ and ‘correction of parameter values’.The Higgs mechanism required the existence of a particle, but the mass value itself could not be accurately determined based on pre-experiment theory alone.

**Caution** We should not simplify by saying that the Higgs creates all the mass.Most of the mass of protons and neutrons comes from strong interaction energy.

## metric

**Characteristic and established geometry and gravity concepts**

**Easy meaning** It is a rule that determines the distance and time interval between two events in space and time.In general relativity, the gravitational field is expressed as a change in space-time metrics.

**In this book** we check whether the renderer preserves distance relationships when transferring its internal state into observable space and time.

**Caution** It cannot be said that Einstein's equation is satisfied simply by obtaining similar distance tables.

## Euclidean signature and Lorentz signature

**Established mathematical physics concept**

**Easy meaning** In the Euclidean signature, all axes are treated like space, and in the Lorentz signature, one time axis has a different sign from the space axes.The latter describes causal space and time.

**In this book** we distinguish whether the results of easy-to-compute Euclidean windows can lead to Lorentzian worlds with real time evolution.

**Caution** The stability of Euclidean calculations does not guarantee causality and stability of the actual universe.

## Causality and delayed response

**Personally established physics concept**

**Easy meaning** The cause must come before the effect, and delayed response is a response rule designed to prevent future stimuli from changing the past state.

**In this book** we check whether the linear response of the common carrier has a physically acceptable time direction.

**Caution** Just having a time variable in the formula does not guarantee causality.

## Scale invariance and primitive fluctuations

**Concept of established cosmology**

**Easy meaning** Scale invariance is the property that the statistical shape is almost the same even when the size is enlarged or reduced.Density fluctuations in the early universe are the seeds of the cosmic background radiation and galaxy structure.

**In this book** we test the possibility that WRRA transfer conditions can be linked to the observed nearly scale-invariant raw spectra.

**Caution** Fitting the qualitative shape and fitting the observations including amplitude, slope, and non-Gaussianness are two different steps.

## Planck scale

**A scale based on established physical constants**

**Easy meaning** It is a standard of very small length and very high energy created by combining the speed of light, gravitational constant, and Planck's constant.Quantum mechanics and gravity are expected to become important at the same time.

**In this book** When estimating the amount of space information or minimum resolution, it is used as an externally given physical standard.

**Caution** This does not mean that WRRA has derived a new Planck value.This value uses already known physical constants as input.

## Hologram principle

**Strong hypotheses and research principles of personality theoretical physics**

**Easy meaning** The idea is that the maximum amount of information in a space can be proportional to the area of the boundary, not the volume.It comes from research on black hole thermodynamics and quantum gravity.

**In this book**, this is the background for comparing the compression question of minimum computation cosmology with the existing estimate that the amount of information in the entire universe is about 10¹²² to 10¹²³ bits.

**Caution** This number is not independently predicted by WRRA, but is an estimate of the existing series using the Planck area and the size of the cosmic horizon.

## Entropy and information amount

**Physical and information concepts with established characteristics**

**Easy meaning** Entropy is a quantity that measures the number of possible microstates or the size of uncertainty.The amount of information is related to the number of bits required to distinguish and record certain states.

**In this book**, we distinguish between the cost of storing the complete image of the universe and the compression cost of storing only the laws, SOURCE, and execution rules.

**Caution** Thermodynamic entropy, Shannon information, and algorithmic information are related but not the same quantity.

## renormalization

**Established quantum field theory and statistical physics methods**

**Easy meaning** It is a procedure that organizes how effective parameters vary depending on the observed energy or length scale and consistently connects the divergence of calculations with physical observed quantities.

**In this book** Used to discuss how filters and combination structures lead to different computational scales.

**Caution** The explanation that this is a shortcut to arbitrarily erase infinity is inaccurate.

## superconductivity

Characteristics Established condensed matter physics concepts + conditional implementation of this book

Simple meaning: The Meissner effect, in which direct current resistance disappears below the critical temperature and the magnetic field is excluded from the inside of the bulk, is a quantum collective phenomenon in which finite superfluid stiffness and phase order appear together.

In this book, it is not a property directly provided by SOURCE, but is interpreted as a downstream group phenotype that occurs when electron pair formation, phase locking of the relationship path, phase slip suppression, and electromagnetic boundary response are closed together.

Caution: Resistance reduction alone is not sufficient; bulk Meissner fraction, h/(2e) flux quantization, superfluid density, and reproducible critical conditions must also be checked.The 300 K Toy parameter is not the discovery of a true room temperature superconductor.

## superfluidity

Characteristics Established Condensed Matter Physics Concepts + Structural Reclassification of the Book

Simple meaning: It is a collective quantum phenomenon in which a superfluid component with very small viscous loss transports mass according to the phase gradient of a complex order variable, and circulation is quantized in a closed path.

In this book, the wetting film of helium-4 transforms the container boundary into a continuous mass CARRY PATH, and the topological relationships are interpreted as downstream population phenotypes that are reconstructed at each current.

Note: The Rollin membrane is not a gravity shield, and the condensation density n₀ and the superfluid mass density ρs are generally not the same.A distinction must also be made between open membrane outflow and closed loop winding current.

## living closure

Personality Operational definitions for applications of this book outside of biology

It is a condition for self-reconfiguration of the reaction network, translation device, cell membrane, and replicable state while being limitedly open to an easy-to-understand environment, and to continue the feasibility even after division.

In this book, it is defined as the intersection of bounded openness, renderer self-reconstruction, and intergenerational executable.

This does not mean that the cautionary universe is life, nor does reproducing the minimal cell public data verify the WRRA cosmology.105 min is an existing correction value, so it is not counted as an independent prediction.

## biochemical executor

A term reclassifying an established molecular biology component to WRRA ownership.

In simple terms, it is a physical collection of polymerase, ribosome, tRNA, aaRS, translation factor, and energy chemistry that reads DNA and RNA specifications and turns them into actual molecular products.

Having a description of an executor in the state's DNA does not mean that the first executor arises spontaneously.Distinguish between seeded expression and seed-free bootstrap.

## Lineage reproduction number (Rlife)

WRRA Life Applications of Characteristics Established Branching Process Expected Values

Simple meaning: It indicates how many viable daughter cells one parent cell leaves on average for the next generation.If the probability of two daughter cells passing through is P₂ and the probability of only one passing through is P₁, then Rlife=P₁+2P₂.

Note that Rlife\>1 is the supercriticality of the declared probability model, and does not mean the permanent survival or overall evolutionary fitness of the actual species.

## Paired daughter cell holdout (paired-daughter holdout)

Personality WRRA Life Model III Independent Verification Data Structure

Rather than comparing group averages, it is an unused validation set that links the number of molecules, volume, and subsequent division of two daughter cells, A and B, from one mother cell with the same record.

Note: If symmetric splitting has already been used to build the model, the same data is not counted as an independent prediction success.

## first living lineage

Personality WRRA Life Model 0 Operational Life Boundaries

A chemical system with a simple boundary passes on functional differences to its descendants and continues to create more than one functional daughter line on average.

Note: It is not the same as LUCA, the first protocell, or a modern autonomous cell, and does not specify the actual historical first entity.

## environmental renderer

Personality OriginsStructural Role in Life Scenarios

In simple terms, the wetting-drying, temperature, photochemical, mineral, and redox cycles are external execution conditions that instead perform the enrichment, activation, and division of the initial compartment.

This is not a concept that calls the attention environment itself life or conscious choice.The unit of choice is a bounded lineage whose internal state changes the production of descendants.

## copying-error threshold

Accurate boundaries in character-declared basin models

In simple terms, the longer the inheritance sequence, the higher the copy accuracy of each site, which is a condition for functional descendants to exceed one lineage on average.

Caution: fc=(2K)⁻¹ᐟᴸ holds only under the assumption of two daughters, average f, and independent factorization, and is not a universal life constant.

## fixed-present neural state

Applications outside of biology applying the present-residual analysis of WRRA to normal state space expressions

Simple Meaning: Instead of rereading the entire past record, it is an expression that calculates the next neural state only with current activity, adaptation, available resources, and current input.

Note: Markovness does not mean that it is automatically true.If the past path better predicts the next state in the same observed present, the state is insufficient, and if a finite expansion does not resolve it, this expression fails.

## computational resource ledger

Personality WRRA-Worm Toy model internal preservation device

In simple terms, it is a ledger in which local resource q moves to the environmental storage E during activity and returns during recovery to keep B=Σq+E constant.

Caution: It is not a measurement of ATP, heat, or free energy, and units and metabolic data must be linked for actual biophysical analysis.

## relationship port

Hypothetical physics interface for personality WRRA 2.3

# D-5 Reading order when encountering formulas

1. First look at the left side of the equal sign.Check what this formula ultimately calculates.

2. Divide the symbols on the right into input, rule, and output.Values ​​already obtained from observations are marked as ‘input’, and newly calculated values ​​are marked as ‘output’.

3. Check the units.If you just added values ​​with different units, such as length and energy, there is a high possibility that the equation is wrong.

4. Check whether it is an exact or approximate expression.≈, number of cutting modes, small parameter assumption limits the scope of application.

5. Separate mathematical establishment and physical interpretation.Calculating the determinant to be 0 and claiming that an actual particle was created require different evidence.

6. Finally, find disconfirmation conditions.The better the hypothesis is, the clearer it is whether certain observations or calculation results should discard this claim.

#D-6 Recommended order for further reading

When reading for the first time, it is enough to understand why this function appeared rather than ‘the exact series expansion of the Bessel function’.In the second reading, the input and output values ​​are displayed, and in the third reading, the definitions, assumptions, errors, and disconfirmation conditions of the original paper are checked.The formula in this book is not a barrier to test the reader, but a device to fix the scope of claims that can be blurred by words.

# D-7 Re-entry guidance when starting study again

**Step 1 · Perspective recovery** Reread parts 1 and 2 to recover the starting point of ‘possible universes may be plural, but the history recorded to us is only one’ and the large structure of SOURCE-RELATION-BOUNDARY-RENDERER.

**Step 2 · Recovery of computational language** Read the Bessel function, zeta function, operator, eigenvalue, spectrum, Hamiltonian, and propagator items in this appendix, and return to the finite Jacobi benchmark in Part 5.Rather than memorizing all the formulas, it is enough to be able to distinguish between input, operation, and output.

Step 3 · Check the current boundary Compare the multi-disciplinary verification and denial results in Part 8, Core 1.0 freezing and MCC 2.3.2 restoration in Part 9, Game 0.1–1.0 in Part 10, and causal horizon, light, total data load, dual component gravity, and Cross Domain 1.0 in Part 11 with the claim grade.

Step 4 · Re-entry to the original The upper-level grammar starts from WRRA Core 1.0 (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22650956), and the cosmology starts from MCC 2.0 (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22643792) and 2.3.2.The physical boundaries are causal horizon (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22931512) and dual-component gravity (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22984608), and the application cross-validation is Game 1.0 (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22985378) and Cross Domain 1.0 (DOI).10.5281/zenodo.22985973).

Principle of Restart: Do not trust or abandon all previous achievements.Restore the original level and ownership of EXACT·STRUCTURAL·CONDITIONAL·FROZEN PREDICTION·PREREGISTERED TEST·OPEN.Independent forecasting is not a required condition, but items declared as forecasts are not recalibrated after the fact.

## D-8 WRRA 2.0 Additional Terms

prime SOURCE: An independent arithmetic marker that is not reducible to a composite product structure.There is no automatic identification with physical particles or energy.

composite relation port: Potential relationship address specified by the composite number's valuation incidence.

fixed-present ownership scaled simplex: current ownership state space with q_k ≥ 0, finite support, and the condition 1ᵀq_k=Q₀.When Q₀\>0, only p_k=q_k/Q₀ is a probability simplex coordinate, and q_k is not a quantum density matrix.

WJNS arithmetic scale gate: An arithmetic selection device that connects the registration layer and the execution layer using a multiplication scale and a completed reflection structure.

E_lift / R_phys: E_lift is a lifting that raises the classical registry·carrier state to quantum Actual, and R_phys is a physicalization idea that connects Actual to field, geometry, and observation algebra with units.Both are currently OPEN.

WRRA-Cell residue h: A slow state candidate that retains the effects of previous stimuli in the present in finite dimensions.It is not distinguishable from a latent state or exponential smoother until a measurable physical counterpart has been identified.

identifiability gate: A rule that closes claims that are not allowed by given data before calculation.Static Perturb-seq alone does not identify the temporal residue, recovery, and re-challenge of the same cell.

partial non-confirmation: A pre-frozen prediction is not supported in testable areas, but the data do not have the scope to confirm the entire hypothesis.It is neither a success nor a complete disproof.

economic residue: A slow state candidate in which the effects of previous shocks remain in the current state of debt, credit supply, employment practices, investment delays, risk management, and institutional trust and affect the next economic state.It is not automatically equated with one consumer sentiment index.

crisis-memory regime: An analysis period that tests whether the slow effects of shocks are different from periods of calm, combining the observation window after the official recession and the pre-declared recession.Window length cannot be promoted to a universal constant after viewing the data.

temporal placebo: A controlled test in which present residue candidates are intentionally moved to a past point in time to see whether the current alignment is actually more explanatory.If an older proxy is better, the temporal uniqueness of the present residue is not identified.

real-time vintage: Values ​​of economic indicators that were released and actually visible to forecasters before they were later revised.Hindsight can be entered by using only the current final revision.

track-derived residue: A variable that compresses the past movement, acceleration, and intensity changes of the tropical cyclone center into the current finite EWMA coordinates.Proof is needed that it is not the same as the environmental field itself and is an independent physical state variable.

environmental-field ownership: The hypothesis that the current environmental field, including steering flow, pressure field, wind shear, moisture, ocean heat, and ground interactions, may be the actual owner of dynamical information rather than the traces left behind in the observed trajectory.

forward validation: Validation that learns from past data and performs model selection and final testing only in a later period.It prevents leakage of future information, but does not eliminate the change of times itself.

storm-cluster bootstrap: An uncertainty estimation method that resamples the entire observation section without incorrectly counting multiple points in time within a tropical cyclone as independent samples.

predictive non-uniqueness: The state of not being able to uniquely retrieve a future from a limited observational present.This alone does not prove ontological indeterminism.

information closure: The property in which a declared set of current information determines the goal as a single value.This is distinguished from the fact that the computational cost is large.

conditional Dirac law: A state in which the conditional distribution of the target concentrates all on one value, given the current information.This is the correct interpretation standard for WRRA v2.1.

information-open transition: A transition in which the conditional distribution does not degenerate because the environment, event, or boundary input required for the next state is not in the current information or has not yet been realized.

finite proof compression: A proof that closes the global proposition with a finite invariant, symmetry, reductio, and recursion structure rather than examining infinite cases one by one.

threshold corridor: A region in which states lie close to the quantization threshold such that small perturbations can change 0/1 or the discrete record.

effective macro-determinacy: A state in which the conditional risk at the pre-registered resolution and loss function is less than the tolerance and the record remains stable.It is not the same as fundamental determinism.

Output contract: A rule that prefixes a legitimate result format among value, certificate, global proof, finite time trajectory, ensemble, and conditional partial distribution depending on the closure type of the problem.

finite-search non-certifiability: A scope limitation theorem that states that a global no-counterexample in an infinite area cannot be certified with only a no-counterexample record of finite height, finite query, and finite precision.This does not mean that all finite mathematical proofs are impossible.

finite jet: Local data that contains function values ​​and derivatives up to a finite order at a point.Out-of-observation zero points can be inserted while preserving the entire finite jet.

Jensen circle detector: log\|ξ\| of circle circumferenceAn accurate local sensor that detects the weighted number of the zero point in the disk based on the difference between the average and the central value.It is used in admissible disks where there is no zero point on the boundary circle.

global invariant: A law that finitely compresses global conclusions into functional equations, positivity, conservation transition, and spectrum structures rather than exploring infinite cases one by one.

Epigenetic memory: A phenomenon in which physical residues of a past state reconfigure functions or future responses even after removal of inductive signals and cell division, without changing the DNA sequence.It is not the same as simple survival of the cover.

mark-specific retention: The principle of assigning distribution and restoration coefficients for each mark rather than enforcing a single universal dilution rate for different memory layers such as methylation, histone, bookmarking, and three-dimensional chromatin.

coupled memory eigenmode: A candidate expressing the attenuation and interlayer transfer of multiple molecular layers as a single state transition matrix eigenmode.Should be better than the diagonal model in independent genealogy holdout.

Non-commuting fate operation: When the synthesis order of gate, transcription factor, and maintenance marker treatment changes long-term cell fate even with the same total exposure.It must be distinguished from simple AUC or immediate response.

distributed biological memory: A model in which the owners of past effects can be distributed not only inside cells but also in niches such as lineage, extracellular matrix, immune cells, inflammation, and tissue space.

autonomous-closure ratio Λauto: The proportion of the total work required for the next execution of a living lineage that is taken over by an internal system.It is a ledger that does not hide the actual contribution of the environment and is not a universal constant of life.

compatible-state diameter: The width of the value that states compatible with the declared current information produce for the target Q.It is not a measure of the number of possible universes, but a measure of epistemological non-closure under the chosen model and errors.

Bayes recovery risk: Minimum expected loss, given information and loss function, that cannot be lowered by any accepted decision rule.The effective accuracy of the interpretation is determined for each purpose.

WRRA five-contract classifier: A methodology that classifies tasks as analysis, calculation, prediction, proof-closed, or open and requires different outputs: value, algorithm, distribution, proof, and additional information.

record quality qΦ: A task-relative measure that weights and combines record distinguishability, continuity, and independent channel redundancy.It is not a universal macro-micro size constant.

Four-track validation: Verification ledger for Integrative Biology 3.0 that preserves public data reanalysis, structural simulations, synthetic predictions, and real experiment pre-registration at different levels of evidence.

non-ribosomal mass-regeneration ratio Rmass,nr: The non-ribosomal PURE protein mass provided by one generation to the next generation divided by the mass of the previous generation.It must be greater than 1 to be non-degenerate in iterative equality.

molecular partition error Dx: The difference between the fraction px of a specific molecular species and the volume fraction v of the two daughter cells \|px−v\|.It is a measure of distribution bias that is distinct from the survival threshold ϕ.

lineage persistence gate Gpersist: A threshold that asks whether a functional lineage persists beyond the minimum number of generations declared.We do not equate continued external support with autonomous closure.

Two-axis completion audit: Completion of the research structure that assigns variables, thresholds, judgment, and falsification conditions to all requested questions of DNA → first life and epigenetic memory → cell fate.This does not mean that all biological closures are successful.

# Appendix E Where does information in the universe come from?

**Guide for the general reader of the WRRA Information Ledger**

When asked about the size, mass, shape, location of particles, and future events of the universe, WRRA does not just provide numbers.The layer that first had the answer, the minimum data actually read, the calculation path, the observation instruments, and the final record are presented together.This appendix is ​​a translation of the essence of 「The WRRA Information Provision Ledger」 v1.0 (doi:10.5281/zenodo.22139233) written on August 28, 2026 in the language of the book.

|**Central Principle** Do not secretly calculate derived phenotypes with SOURCE data, and do not treat events that have not yet occurred as prewritten future.|
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

# Six routes through which E-1 information is provided

|**Grade** |**Where does it come from** |**Easy example** |
|---------------------------|---------------------------------------------|----------------------------------|
|INPUT |Read directly at boot time |Law·Constant·Initial Relationship·Phase·Boundary |
|DERIVED |Calculation from current state and law |Universe age/matter distribution/molecular structure |
|CALIBRATED |Fit parameters with observations |Current mass/mixing/cosmology parameters |
|OBSERVED |Convert query to observation record |Distance estimation/detector video |
|OPEN EVENT |Add one result when an event occurs |Electron detection location and decay results |
|OPEN |Owner or calculation method not yet determined |Ultimate SOURCE·Independent WRRA Forecast |

*P(X)=(owner, payload, execution map, trigger, record, status, precision)*

This equation means that when calculating the value of the physical quantityIf a value that is an input today is derived from a deeper rule tomorrow, the ledger's rating moves from CALIBRATED to DERIVED.

# E-3 Path to provide particle and molecular information

|**Question** |**Provided Path** |**Current Judgment** |
|------------------|---------------------------------------------|---------------------|
|Type of charge |Gauge expression in expressive grammar |STRUCTURE+INPUT |
|Spin |Expression of rotation/Lorentz symmetry |STRUCTURAL OUTPUT |
|mass |Correction value and the pole/threshold of the propagator after opening |CALIBRATED→EXECUTED |
|electronic location |Born probability density of quantum state |PROBABILITY OUTPUT |
|Detected electron position |The incident device records one acceptable result |OPEN EVENT |
|Atomic/Molecular Structure |Calculate many-body state using charge, mass, and interaction |DERIVED |
|Isotope Shift |Implement the same electron law by changing only the nuclear mass |DERIVED |
|future measurements |Probabilities evolve, but results are not stored in advance |OPEN EVENT |

The standard model is a visible expression grammar that determines what forms particles, fields, and interactions can appear in this table.This does not mean that the explanatory power of the standard model is lowered.While WRRA faithfully executes the precise grammar, it additionally asks at what layer the information needed for the grammar is provided.

Although 815,232 bits is an exact arithmetic value, it does not mean that it is the minimum size of the information amount of the universe or the ultimate SOURCE.The maximum information capacity of direct array storage, shortest generation program, running memory, event record, temporary render workspace and causal area are different ledgers.

*K_U(T)=K_boot+K_initial+K_event(0:T), Memory_live≈K_boot+K_state+C_render*

# E-5 Procedure from question to answer

1. Decide what you want to know and with what precision.Distinguish between ‘total size of the universe’ and ‘observable universe radius’.

2. Find the first owner among LAW HEADER, SOURCE, global section, renderer, expression grammar, current state, and event device.

3. Remove redundant fields that can be recreated in the previous step and read only the minimum payload that is actually needed.

4. Declare the scale, boundary, renormalization method and error tolerance and then run the execution rule.

5. Decide whether the answer is a state value, probability distribution, corrected estimate, or newly realized event.

6. Attach condition ratings and uncertainties to stable records, and separate inputs and independent predictions.

# E-6 Audit inspection that must be passed

**No hidden pre-input** The claimed predicted value must not already be contained in the SOURCE or correction fields.

**Reconstruction check** Even if the DERIVED field is deleted, it must be possible to recalculate within the declared error.

**Reading order check** The order should be stored as separate information only when there is a non-commutative dependency.

**Address/Space Distinction** Do not secretly equate SOURCE addresses with visible 3D coordinates.

**Global definition check** The overall size and mass of the universe must have phase, area, and gravitational definitions.

**Shared record matching** Overlapping observation queries must produce the same answer from the protected common observation quantity.

**Open event preservation** Future results should not be hidden in a boot package under a different name.

**Separation of calibration and prediction** Separate the observations used for parameter fitting and the observations used for prediction testing.

# E-7 Current judgment and next execution

What is exactly closed is the arithmetic that the direct storage amount of 6,369 complex float64 arrays is 99.515625KiB, and the ledger definition that the information provision path can be separated.What is structurally fixed is that the dimensional filter remains inside the WRRA and the SOURCE address is not a visible 3D point.What remains as conditional inputs are global phase, initial scale, and boundary data, and most of the observed masses and combinations of the Standard Model are still CALIBRATED.The new physics prediction independent of the ultimate SOURCE is OPEN.

|**Run next** SOURCE v0.2 + LAW HEADER + WRRA → Electronic status → Spatial probability → Single event record + Full provision trace.The first goal is to separate and output in one run where the charge, mass, position, photon coupling, and detection events were each provided.|
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

This ledger is not a document that found the correct answer to the universe.However, no matter what value you get in the future, it makes you ask ‘Where did it come from?’Minimum computation cosmology goes one step further from the idea of ​​making the storage amount small, and becomes a theory that does not refer to input information, calculated information, and unborn events interchangeably.
